### [1] [On the Kähler MMP and the transcendental base-point-free theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24986)
*Christopher Hacon,Lingyao Xie*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文建立了大gklt Kähler对的最小模型纲领，特别证明了Tosatti的超越基点自由猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复几何中，Kähler流形上的极小模型纲领是代数几何中经典MMP的推广，但之前仅适用于某些特定情形。Tosatti提出的超越基点自由猜想是该领域的一个核心未解决问题，其解决对于理解Kähler对的分类理论至关重要。

Method: 本文通过建立大gklt（广义Kawamata对数终端）Kähler对的最小模型纲领，采用超越方法（非代数方法）处理Kähler几何中的奇点与收缩映射，推广了代数MMP中的关键技术如锥定理、收缩定理和flip存在性到Kähler设定中。

Result: 成功证明了大gklt Kähler对的最小模型纲领成立，并作为推论证明了Tosatti的超越基点自由猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作将代数几何中的核心工具MMP完整推广到了超越Kähler几何中，解决了Tosatti猜想这一长期开放问题，为Kähler流形的双有理分类提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this article, we establish the minimal model program for big gklt Kähler pairs, and in particular we prove Tosatti's transcendental base-point-free conjecture.

</details>


### [2] [A counterexample to a conjecture of Duncan on versal actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25118)
*Federico Scavia*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个具体的反例（(C₇ ⋊ C₃) × C₂ 在二次 Del Pezzo 曲面上的作用），否证了 Duncan 的一个猜想，该猜想声称忠实有限群作用的普适性可以在 Sylow 子群上检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Duncan 曾猜想：一个忠实有限群作用是否具有普适性（versality），可以通过检查其所有 Sylow 子群的作用来判断。本文旨在寻找反例来否定这一猜想。

Method: 构造了一个具体的反例：考虑群 (C₇ ⋊ C₃) × C₂ 在二次 Del Pezzo 曲面上的忠实作用，并分析该作用及其 Sylow 子群作用的普适性。

Result: 发现该群作用本身不是普适的（non-versal），但其所有 Sylow 子群的作用都是普适的，从而否证了 Duncan 的猜想。

Conclusion: Duncan 关于“忠实有限群作用的普适性可通过 Sylow 子群检测”的猜想不成立，因为存在一个反例使得整体作用非普适而所有 Sylow 子群作用普适。

Abstract: We disprove a conjecture of Duncan asserting that versality of a faithful finite group action can be detected on Sylow subgroups. Our counterexample is an action of $(C_7\rtimes C_3)\times C_2$ on a degree-$2$ Del Pezzo surface.

</details>


### [3] [The Miyaoka-Yau inequality and the delta invariant for Fano varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25181)
*Tomoyuki Hisamoto,Masataka Iwai*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文建立了任意n维klt Fano簇（可能K-不稳定）的Miyaoka-Yau不等式，用delta不变量表示；并推广到等变情形（涉及Kähler-Ricci孤子），将Chern类替换为等变Chern类，delta不变量替换为加权delta不变量。作为推论，得到了每个存在Kähler-Ricci孤子的光滑Fano流形的等变Miyaoka-Yau不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立K-不稳定Fano簇的Miyaoka-Yau不等式，并将其推广到等变孤子情形，从而统一和推广已知的Miyaoka-Yau型不等式。

Method: 利用delta不变量（及其加权版本）对Fano簇的代数几何性质进行刻画，推导出带delta不变量的Miyaoka-Yau不等式；并将Inoue和Hallam-Lahdili的近期工作推广到等变孤子框架下。

Result: 得到了带delta不变量的Miyaoka-Yau不等式，以及等变版本；特别地，每个存在Kähler-Ricci孤子的光滑Fano流形都满足等变Miyaoka-Yau不等式。

Conclusion: 本文的结果统一并推广了Fano簇上的Miyaoka-Yau不等式，将其从K-稳定情形扩展到K-不稳定情形，并建立了与Kähler-Ricci孤子理论之间的联系。

Abstract: We establish the following Miyaoka-Yau inequality for any $n$-dimensional klt Fano variety $X$, possibly K-unstable, in terms of its delta invariant:
  $$ \left(2(n+1)\widehat{c}_2(X)-n c_1(X)^2\right)\cdot c_1(X)^{n-2} \ge -n \left(1-\min\{1,δ(X)\}\right)^2 \cdot c_1(X)^n. $$ Furthermore, inspired by recent work of Inoue and Hallam-Lahdili, we formulate and prove an equivariant version of this inequality in the soliton setting, in which the Chern classes and the delta invariant are replaced by the equivariant Chern classes and the weighted delta invariant, respectively. As a consequence, we obtain the equivariant Miyaoka-Yau inequality for every smooth Fano manifold admitting a Kähler-Ricci soliton.

</details>

### [5] [A smooth projective counterexample to Bondal-Polishchuk's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25391)
*Anya Nordskova*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了某个光滑射影（弱Fano）三维簇$X$上，辫群在$D^b(X)$的全体极异常集合上的作用不是传递的，从而否定了Bondal和Polishchuk于1993年提出的猜想。此前Chang、Haiden和Schroll已通过部分缠绕Fukaya范畴族给出反例，但本文是首个形如$D^b(X)$（$X$为光滑射影簇）的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳Bondal和Polishchuk关于辫群在光滑射影簇导出范畴的极异常集合上作用具有传递性的1993年猜想，填补该猜想在代数几何框架内无具体反例的空白。

Method: 选取特定的光滑射影（弱Fano）三维簇$X$，分析其导出范畴$D^b(X)$中极异常集合的结构，检验辫群作用是否传递。

Result: 辫群在$D^b(X)$的全体极异常集合上的作用不是传递的，给出了该猜想在光滑射影簇情形下的反例。

Conclusion: 否定了Bondal和Polishchuk的猜想，提供了该猜想在代数几何（光滑射影簇导出范畴）中的首个反例，扩展了先前只在缠绕Fukaya范畴中成立的结论。

Abstract: For a particular smooth projective (weak Fano) threefold $X$ we show that the braid group action on the set of full exceptional collections in $D^b(X)$ is not transitive. This provides a counterexample to a conjecture of Bondal and Polishchuk from 1993. The conjecture was first disproved by Chang, Haiden, and Schroll, who constructed a family of partially wrapped Fukaya categories for which the transitivity fails. However, no counterexample of the form $D^b(X)$ for $X$ a smooth projective variety was previously known.

</details>


### [6] [Categorical absorptions of cone singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25406)
*Martin Kalck,Nebojsa Pavic,with a joint appendix by Yujiro Kawamata,the authors*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kuznetsov-Shinder关于某些锥奇点的范畴吸收，推广了他们对节点奇点的结果。特别地，给出了cones over Fano varieties的tilting objects的自同态代数的显式描述。在"分裂情形"下，这些代数是Keller意义下某些Calabi-Yau完备化的截断，该情形出现在许多Fano簇（如射影空间、光滑二次曲面、次数大于4的del Pezzo曲面等）的反典范射影锥上。一般情况下，这些代数是分裂情形的形变。作为推论，得到了有限维代数奇点范畴与某些锥奇点范畴之间的三角等价，并在负K-理论上得到消没结果。在Kawamata合作的附录中，给出了加权射影空间$\mathbb{P}(1^d, m)$的tilting objects的显式描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Kuznetsov-Shinder的范畴吸收方法，从节点奇点推广到更一般的锥奇点，并给出tilting objects的显式代数描述，以建立奇点范畴之间的三角等价关系。

Method: 利用Bridgeland和Stern的几何例外序列概念，对cones over certain Fano varieties构造tilting objects；在分裂情形下联系Keller的Calabi-Yau完备化的截断；在一般情形下视为分裂情形的形变。与Kawamata合作，对加权射影空间给出显式描述。

Result: 给出了锥奇点的tilting objects的自同态代数的显式描述，区分了分裂情形（截断的Calabi-Yau完备化）和一般情形（形变）。建立了有限维代数奇点范畴与锥奇点范畴之间的三角等价，得到了负K-理论的消没结果。对加权射影空间$\mathbb{P}(1^d, m)$给出了tilting objects的显式描述。

Conclusion: 成功将Kuznetsov-Shinder的范畴吸收方法推广到更一般的锥奇点，并通过tilting objects的代数描述建立了奇点范畴之间的等价关系，为理解这类奇点的范畴结构提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We study Kuznetsov-Shinder's categorical absorption for certain cone singularities, generalizing their results for nodal singularities. In particular, we give explicit descriptions of the endomorphism algebras of tilting objects for categorical absorptions of cones over certain Fano varieties admitting a geometric exceptional sequence, in the sense of Bridgeland and Stern. In the simplest ``split case'', these algebras are truncations of certain Calabi-Yau completions in the sense of Keller. The split case occurs for anticanonical projective cones over many Fano varieties (like projective spaces, smooth quadrics, del Pezzo surfaces of degree greater than $4$, smooth del Pezzo threefolds of degree five, and finite products of these varieties). In general, the algebras are deformations of the split case.
  As a consequence, we obtain triangle equivalances between singularity categories of finite dimensional algebras and singularity categories of certain cone singularities, which also yields vanishing results in negative $\mathsf{K}$-theory.
  In a joint appendix with Yujiro Kawamata, we give an explicit description of tilting objects for weighted projective spaces $\mathbb{P}(1^d, m)$.

</details>


### [7] [Type A Nilpotent Hessenberg varieties with Hessenberg function $h(i)\le i+1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25520)
*Zijing Zhuang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类特殊的类型A幂零Hessenberg簇（称为广义抛物线Peterson簇），证明了其可分解为若干子簇Pet_{λ,α}的并集；当α被λ控制时，最大维数分支的基数等于Kostka数K_{λα}，并给出了维数公式与Poincaré多项式的递推关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解满足h(i)≤i+1的Hessenberg簇（广义抛物线Peterson簇）的几何结构，部分回答关于Hessenberg簇几何的开放问题，并探索其表示论意义。

Method: 将Hessenberg簇分解为子簇Pet_{λ,α}（λ为整数分拆，α为整数合成且被λ控制）；通过组合论方法分析最大维数分支的计数（联系Kostka数）与维数（仅依赖λ和α长度）；推导Poincaré多项式的递推公式。

Result: (1) 广义抛物线Peterson簇可分解为Pet_{λ,α}的并集；(2) 当α被λ控制时，Pet_{λ,α}的最大维数分支的基数等于Kostka数K_{λα}；(3) 其维数仅由λ和α的长度决定；(4) 给出了Poincaré多项式的递推公式。

Conclusion: 部分回答了Hessenberg簇几何的开放问题，但留下了关于这些几何事实背后的表示论原因的进一步疑问。

Abstract: We study the type A nilpotent Hessenberg varieties associated with Hessenberg functions that satisfy $h(i)\le i+1$. We call these the generalized parabolic Peterson varieties. We show that such varieties can be decomposed into the union of specific generalized parabolic Peterson varieties $\operatorname{Pet}_{λ,α}$, such that $λ$ is an integer partition, $α$ is an integer composition, and $α$ is dominated by $λ$. We prove that when $α$ is dominated by $λ$, the cardinality of the maximal dimensional components of $\operatorname{Pet}_{λ,α}$ equals the Kostka number $\mathcal{K}_{λα}$, and its dimension is determined only by $λ$ and the length of $α$. We provide a recursive formula for the Poincaré polynomial of $\operatorname{Pet}_{λ,α}$. These results partially answer open questions about the geometry of Hessenberg varieties but raise further questions about the representation-theoretic reasons for these facts.

</details>


### [8] [Instanton slices and their superpolynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25666)
*Ivan Cherednik*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文建立了平面曲线奇点的动机超多项式与对应瞬子切片超多项式（含导子的Nekrasov型瞬子和）之间的联系，并将此推广到孤立曲面奇点。通过此框架获得了非代数纽结的超多项式的动机解释，并给出了任意秩瞬子和的新公式。此外，提出了关于单项式导子的超对偶猜想，将新超多项式与DAHA超多项式及约化Khovanov-Rozansky多项式关联起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立平面曲线奇点的动机超多项式与瞬子切片超多项式之间的关键定理联系，并探索孤立曲面奇点的推广，以期影响仿射Springer纤维理论和相关领域，同时为纽结理论提供新的动机解释。

Method: 通过连接动机超多项式与带导子的Nekrasov型瞬子和，利用瞬子切片（与紧化Jacobian相关）作为桥梁；将瞬子和表示为平面曲线奇点超多项式的归纳极限，并针对任意$\mathbb{F}_q[[x,y]]$中的理想导子构建超对偶性框架。

Result: (1) 获得了双曲纽结K12n242和K12n725等非代数纽结超多项式的动机解释（计数某些stack的$\mathbb{F}_q$-点）；(2) 得到了任意秩瞬子和的新的闭式公式；(3) 提出了关于单项式导子的超对偶猜想，将对应的瞬子超多项式与新型DAHA超多项式及Coxeter纽结的约化Khovanov-Rozansky多项式联系起来。

Conclusion: 本文建立的动机框架统一了平面曲线奇点理论与瞬子物理学，为非代数纽结提供了新的代数几何解释，并预示了与DAHA、EHA及Khovanov-Rozansky理论的深层联系，对仿射Springer纤维（至少A型）等领域有重要影响。

Abstract: The key theorem is a connection between motivic superpolynomials of plane curve singularities in any ranks with superpolynomials of the corresponding instanton slices, Nekrasov-type instanton sums with conductors. In this case, instanton slices are related to compactified Jacobians, but they form a much wider class and, generally, have no connection to plane curve singularities. Moreover, they can be defined for any isolated surface singularities, but we focus on $(0,0)\in \mathbb{A}^2$ in this paper. This development is expected to impact theory of affine Springer fibers (at least, in type $A$), and related fields. For instance, we obtain a motivic interpretation (counting $\mathbb{F}_q$-points of some stacks) of superpolynomials for hyperbolic knots K12n242 and K12n725, among many other non-algebraic knots. New formulas for instanton sums in any ranks are obtained using that they are inductive limits of superpolynomials of proper families of plane curve singularities.
  Generally, the conductors are arbitrary ideals in $\mathbb{F}_q[[x,y]]$ provided the superduality of the corresponding superpolynomials. An important direction of this paper is when they are monomial. We conjecture that the corresponding instanton superpolynomials, if they are superdual, coincide with the DAHA superpolynomials of new type, parallel to the EHA-superpolynomials due to Galashin-Lam. They are expected to coincide with the reduced Khovanov-Rozansky polynomials of the corresponding Coxeter knots. The hyperbolic knots above are the simplest non-algebraic examples.

</details>


### [9] [A Hodge Theoretic Generalization of $\mathbb{Q}$-Homology Manifolds II: Local Complete Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25861)
*Bradley Dirks,Sebastian Olano,Debaditya Raychaudhury*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究复代数簇的Hodge有理同调流形不变量HRH(Z)，特别关注局部完全交子簇情形，建立了该不变量与Bernstein-Sato多项式、Dimca-Maisonobe-Saito谱等经典不变量之间的联系，并揭示了超曲面情形与高余维情形在可完全刻画性上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前引入了复代数簇的奇异性不变量HRH(Z)，本文旨在进一步理解该不变量的性质，将其与已有的经典奇点不变量建立联系，并探究不同余维数情形下的刻画能力差异。

Method: 聚焦于局部完全交子簇，系统比较HRH(Z)与Bernstein-Sato多项式、Dimca-Maisonobe-Saito谱等已知不变量之间的关系。区分超曲面情形（余维1）与高余维情形分别进行分析。

Result: 在超曲面情形下，HRH(Z)可被上述经典不变量完全刻画；但在高余维情形下，这种刻画关系更为复杂微妙，无法简单地完全表征。

Conclusion: HRH(Z)与经典奇点不变量之间存在深刻联系，但该联系依赖于余维数：超曲面情形完备，高余维情形则需要更精细的分析。

Abstract: Recently, the authors introduced and studied a singularity invariant of a complex algebraic variety $Z$, written $\HRH(Z)$ (for ``Hodge rational homology'' manifold level). In this paper, we focus on local complete intersection subvarieties. We relate $\HRH(Z)$ to various well-known invariants, like Bernstein--Sato polynomials and the Dimca-Maisonobe-Saito spectrum. In the hypersurface case it turns out that $\HRH(Z)$ can be completely characterized by these invariants, though higher codimension case is more subtle.

</details>


### [10] [The Cautis-Logvinenko conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25982)
*Alastair Craw,Ryo Yamagishi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了当有限子群G⊂SL(3,ℂ)的McKay箭图无环时，Cautis-Logvinenko猜想成立（包含多数二面体、三面子群及六个散在子群），并通过计算一维权丛的纯层揭示线性化映射Gale对偶矩阵的符号一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cautis-Logvinenko猜想是关于有限子群G⊂SL(3,ℂ)的Bridgeland-King-Reid导出等价下，平凡丛张量不可约表示ρ的像是否为纯层的问题。该猜想为理解奇异点解消中的导出范畴行为提供关键桥梁。

Method: 利用McKay箭图的无环条件，将问题转化为对一维权丛支撑的显式计算；通过分析线性化映射的Gale对偶矩阵的符号一致性，直接读取纯层的支撑与上同调次数。

Result: 在McKay箭图无环的条件下（覆盖SL(3,ℂ)中大多数二面体、三面子群及六个散在子群），证明了Cautis-Logvinenko猜想；建立了Gale对偶矩阵符号一致性与纯层支撑-上同调次数的对应关系。

Conclusion: 该结果系统地验证了SL(3,ℂ)中一大类有限子群的Cautis-Logvinenko猜想，并为导出等价中纯层的分类提供了组合不变量（Gale对偶矩阵）的直观判据。

Abstract: For a finite subgroup $G\subset \operatorname{SL}(3,\mathbb{C})$, the Cautis--Logvinenko conjecture states that for each nontrivial irreducible representation $ρ$ of $G$, the image of the sheaf $\mathcal{O}_0\otimes ρ$ under the derived equivalence of Bridgeland--King--Reid is a pure sheaf on the $G$-Hilbert scheme. We prove this when the McKay quiver of $G$ contains no loops; this includes many dihedral and trihedral subgroups of $\operatorname{SL}(3,\mathbb{C})$, as well as six of the eight sporadic finite subgroups. In doing so, we compute the relevant sheaf explicitly whenever its support is of dimension one. Our main result implies that a matrix defining the Gale dual of the linearisation map is sign-coherent, thereby allowing us to read off the support and cohomological degree of the pure sheaves directly from the matrix.

</details>

### [11] [FinAbstain: Uncertainty-Calibrated Multimodal RAG for Selective Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24875)
*Dorothy Torres,Wei Cheng,Henan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FinAbstain框架，一种面向金融预测的时间安全、不确定性校准的多模态检索增强生成（RAG）系统，通过选择性预测（择时弃权）机制，在不确定性高时主动放弃预测，从而降低选择性错误和回撤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在金融预测中常因证据稀疏、过时或矛盾而表现出高置信度的错误判断，这在实际交易和风控中代价高昂。现有方法缺乏对多模态金融证据的不确定性校准和选择性预测机制。

Method: FinAbstain框架包含：(1) 时间点检索器，仅获取预测时刻前的公开信息；(2) 多智能体（基本面、新闻、技术、风险、验证）对各模态证据进行概率评估；(3) 聚合机制融合检索相关性、证据矛盾度、重复采样一致性及历史校准统计量；(4) 四种不确定性校准方法（温度缩放、保序回归、共形预测及提出的混合不确定性分数）；(5) 控制器在不确定性低于阈值时输出看涨/看跌/中性，否则弃权、请求证据、减少暴露或转人工审核。

Result: 论文报告的是显式标注的模拟结果而非实证结果，表明校准后的弃权机制可以用覆盖率换取更低的择时错误率和回撤。评估涵盖1天和5天异常收益率方向、20天波动率区间、弃权决策等，使用准确率、校准度、风险-覆盖率、引用、交易、延迟和成本指标。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一个时间安全的架构、一个复合不确定性公式，以及一个可复现的、基于证据的择时金融预测评估蓝图。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can synthesize financial narratives but may express high confidence when evidence is sparse, stale, or contradictory. This failure is especially consequential in forecasting, where filings, news, prices, volume, and technical signals can disagree. We present FinAbstain, a research framework for uncertainty-calibrated multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with selective prediction. A point-in-time retriever admits only information public at the forecast timestamp and supplies modality-specific evidence to fundamental, news, technical, risk, and verification agents. Their probabilistic assessments are aggregated with retrieval relevance, evidence contradiction, repeated-sample consistency, and historical calibration statistics. Temperature scaling, isotonic regression, conformal prediction, and a proposed hybrid uncertainty score are evaluated under a common chronological protocol. A controller predicts bullish, bearish, or neutral outcomes only when uncertainty is below a validated threshold; otherwise it abstains, requests evidence, reduces exposure, or routes the case to human review. The evaluation covers one- and five-day abnormal-return direction, twenty-day volatility intervals, and abstention decisions, using accuracy, calibration, risk--coverage, citation, trading, latency, and cost metrics. To make the design auditable before a full data collection is complete, we report explicitly labeled simulated results rather than empirical claims. These results illustrate the intended hypothesis: calibrated abstention may trade coverage for lower selective error and drawdown. The contribution is a time-safe architecture, a composite uncertainty formulation, and a reproducible evaluation blueprint for evidence-grounded selective financial forecasting.

</details>


### [12] [Human Preference aligned Tabular Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24880)
*Frederik Hoppe,Astrid Franz,Marianne Michaelis,Lars Kleinemeier,Udo Göbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有表格嵌入主要针对预测任务优化，而非人类偏好对齐的相似性排序。本文指出标准下游指标不足以评估嵌入在相似性搜索中的可信度，并提出了一种基于人类偏好对齐的评估流程，通过PLM用例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前任务无关的表格嵌入方法在PLM等业务系统中被广泛用于相似性搜索，但这些嵌入主要针对预测任务优化，未能充分对齐人类偏好的相似性排序。标准的下游评估指标不足以评估嵌入在相似性搜索中的可信度，因此需要引入基于人类偏好对齐的评估方法。

Method: 提出了一个具体的人类偏好对齐评估流程，并通过产品生命周期管理（PLM）用例来验证和说明该评估方法的必要性及有效性。

Result: 通过PLM用例表明，现有表格嵌入方法在相似性搜索中存在与人类偏好不一致的问题，而提出的评估流程能够有效揭示这些缺陷。

Conclusion: 嵌入方法的评估不能仅依赖标准下游指标，需要引入人类偏好对齐的评估维度，以确保模型在相似性搜索任务中的可信度。

Abstract: Task-agnostic tabular embeddings are increasingly used for similarity search in real-world business systems such as Product Lifecycle Management (PLM). However, leading embedding approaches are optimized primarily for prediction tasks - not for producing human preference aligned similarity rankings. We argue that standard downstream metrics are insufficient to fully assess embedding trustworthiness for similarity search and that human preference aligned evaluation is a necessary and currently missing component. We present a concrete evaluation procedure and illustrate the problem through a PLM use case.

</details>


### [13] [Using Data-Derived Priors to Guide CNN Architecture Design for NIR Chemometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25636)
*Dário Passos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了光谱数据集属性（如熵、内在秩、小波能量支撑比等）如何作为近红外化学计量学中一维卷积神经网络设计的经验先验。基于25个回归任务，发现卷积感受野与数据集属性关系最显著，并将这些关系转化为启发式规则，其性能可与贝叶斯超参数优化相媲美（中位测试RMSE比为0.953-1.017）。联合预处理与CNN超参数优化在19/25任务中优于标准化光谱方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外化学计量学中的CNN设计通常采用通用架构规则，但不同光谱数据集在采样、平滑度、冗余度和样本量上差异显著。作者希望探究这些数据集属性是否能为CNN设计提供经验先验，从而减少对昂贵超参数优化的依赖，并提升模型在多样化光谱任务中的适用性。

Method: 在25个近红外回归任务中，计算了数据集大小、光谱长度与间距、熵、内在秩、自相关和小波尺度结构等描述符。设计了两种可解释的1D-CNN架构（最小单卷积模型和扩展浅层模型），使用五折交叉验证贝叶斯超参数优化。从近最优试验中提取关系转化为热启动启发式，并通过直接验证和留一数据集验证评估。

Result: 最清晰的关系涉及卷积感受野：最小CNN中，核比例随光谱熵和内在秩增加而减小，随小波能量支撑比增加而增大，学习率随训练集大小增加而降低。直接和留一数据集启发式与HPO相比，中位测试RMSE比分别为0.953和1.017。联合预处理与CNN HPO在19/25任务中优于标准化光谱HPO。

Conclusion: 光谱数据集描述符可为CNN设计提供实用的经验先验，能够在目标特定调优之前将浅层近红外模型引导至合理的超参数区域，减少对广泛超参数优化的依赖。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) for near-infrared (NIR) chemometrics are often designed using generic architectural rules, although spectral datasets differ in sampling, smoothness, redundancy, and sample size. We tested whether these properties can provide empirical priors for CNN design. Across 25 NIR regression tasks, we computed descriptors of dataset size, spectral length and spacing, entropy, intrinsic rank, autocorrelation, and wavelet-scale structure. Two interpretable 1D-CNN scaffolds (a minimal single-convolution model and an extended shallow model with optional branching, dilation, etc) were optimized using five-fold cross-validated Bayesian hyperparameter optimization (HPO). Relationships extracted from near-optimal trials were converted into warm-start heuristics and evaluated directly and through leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) validation. The clearest relationships involved convolutional receptive fields. In the minimal CNN, the preferred kernel fraction decreased with spectral entropy and intrinsic rank, increased with the wavelet energy-support fraction, and the learning rate tended to decrease with training-set size. Direct and LODO heuristics were competitive with HPO, with median test-RMSE ratios of 0.953 and 1.017, respectively. The extended CNN showed similar but less transferable structure across branch usage, dilation, dropout, filter counts, and receptive-field choices. Ten stochastic refits showed seed sensitivity comparable to that of HPO-selected configurations. In a separate experiment, joint preprocessing and CNN HPO outperformed standardized-spectra HPO in 19 of 25 tasks, although gains were dataset-dependent. These results show that spectral descriptors can provide practical CNN design priors, guiding shallow NIR models toward plausible hyperparameter regions before target-specific tuning

</details>


### [14] [Behavior-Driven Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24881)
*Caroline Dominik,Rolf Drechsler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于行为驱动开发（BDD）的结构化场景来生成系统解释的方法，称为行为驱动可解释性（BDX），并通过RISC-V处理器异常解释案例验证了该方法在系统设计阶段的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着系统复杂性大幅增加，单个人或团队难以完全理解整个系统的所有方面，尤其在安全关键系统中，最终设计必须可信，因此可解释性成为现代系统的重要需求。

Method: 利用行为驱动开发（BDD），以结构化场景形式给出期望的系统行为，每个功能对应一系列动作序列，这些场景可直接转化为解释。该方法称为行为驱动可解释性（BDX），适用于任何开发阶段或抽象层次。

Result: 通过RISC-V处理器中异常解释的案例研究，验证了BDX方法在系统设计阶段提供了有效的支持。

Conclusion: BDX方法能够基于规范生成可解释性，适用于系统开发全生命周期，特别在安全关键系统的设计阶段有助于提升系统的可信任度。

Abstract: As system complexity has vastly increased, it has become significantly more challenging for a single person or a team to fully understand all aspects of an entire system. Particularly, this holds when considering all the different stages of a system's development life cycle, such as, e.g., design or maintenance. But especially for safety-critical systems it is essential that the final design can be trusted. Because of this, explainability is becoming an important requirement for modern systems. In this paper, we aim to achieve this goal by utilizing Behavior-Driven Development (BDD), where the expected system behavior is given in the form of structured scenarios. These scenarios give a sequence of actions for each functionality, and by this can be directly translated into explanations. We introduce this method of deriving explanations based on the specification as Behavior-Driven Explainability (BDX). While applicable at any development stage or abstraction level, a case study for the explanation of exceptions in a RISC-V processor shows the support this concept adds during system design.

</details>


### [15] [A Physics-Informed Neural Operator for Thermal Ranking of Low-Cost Wall Materials in Hot-Dry Climates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25668)
*Muhammad Akbar Khan,Fahim Raees,Ubaida Fatima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段计算框架，结合有限差分法（FDM）和物理信息神经算子（PINO），对五种低成本本土墙体材料进行热性能排名。在热干气候条件下，草泥粘土砖具有最佳性价比；气候扫描揭示了排热与散热两种热工制度转变边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热干气候地区低收入农村住宅夏季温度常超45°C，亟需识别经济有效的本土建筑材料以最小化墙体热穿透，改善室内热舒适性。

Method: 两阶段计算框架：阶段一使用经验证的Crank-Nicolson有限差分法求解一维瞬态热方程，通过拉丁超立方采样生成1500个周期性日解；阶段二使用基于傅里叶神经算子（FNO）的物理信息神经算子（PINO）学习参数到解的算子，同时强制数据保真度和PDE一致性。

Result: PINO相对L2场误差5.14e-4，峰值内表面温度平均绝对误差0.201 K，精确保持FDM材料排名；PINO在150个FDM样本上训练即可匹配使用双倍样本的数据驱动FNO。热干条件下草泥粘土砖性价比最优；气候扫描揭示了排热与散热两种热工制度转变边界。

Conclusion: 该框架支持热干地区灾后重建中基于证据的材料选择。

Abstract: Identifying cost-effective indigenous building materials that minimise heat penetration through walls is critical for indoor thermal comfort in low-income rural housing in hot-dry climates, where summer temperatures routinely exceed 45 C. We present a two-stage computational framework for thermal ranking of five low-cost indigenous wall materials: mud brick, clay-straw adobe, lime-stabilised bamboo panel, fired clay brick, and lime-mud composite. First, a validated Crank-Nicolson finite difference method (FDM) solves the one-dimensional transient heat equation with Robin boundary conditions under diurnal solar and outdoor air-temperature forcing, generating 1500 periodic-day solutions across a nine-dimensional parameter space by Latin Hypercube sampling. Second, a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) with a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) backbone learns the parameter-to-solution operator mu -> T(x,t), enforcing both data fidelity and PDE consistency. The trained PINO attains a relative L2 field error of 5.14e-4 and a 0.201 K mean absolute error on the peak inner surface temperature, preserving the FDM material ranking exactly; PINO trained on 150 FDM samples matches a data-only FNO trained on twice as many, so the physics loss is most valuable when data are scarce. The periodic-day formulation also yields the ISO 13786 time lag and decrement factor, reproduced to within 0.99 h and 0.010. At nominal hot-dry summer conditions, clay-straw adobe achieves the best cost-performance index among widely available materials. A climate sweep, confirmed by FDM spot checks, reveals a regime boundary: under sub-ambient outdoor conditions the ranking inverts to conductive fired clay brick, delineating heat-exclusion and heat-rejection regimes. The framework supports evidence-based material selection for post-flood reconstruction in hot-dry regions.

</details>


### [16] [Multiclass Classification without Labels via Posterior Simplex Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24943)
*Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini,Johann Ioannou-Nikolaides,Troels Petersen,Vincenzo Piuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多类别无标签分类的扩展方法，证明在仅有混合数据（无标签）的情况下，通过区分不同混合比例的数据集，可以恢复潜在的类别结构和最优分类器，并在多个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多分类问题中，可靠的实例级标签不可用，但可以构建弱监督的未标记样本（如不同数据来源、实验条件等导致潜在类别比例变化但未揭示真实标签）。作者希望利用这种弱监督信息解决多类别分类问题。

Method: 1) 将CWoLa从二分类扩展到多分类(K>2)；2) 证明Bayes最优混合分类器g*将数据点映射到混合后验空间中的(K-1)单形体，其K个顶点由未知混合矩阵诱导；3) 提出两种先验无关的方法：后处理单形拟合和瓶颈架构，从混合分类器中提取潜在类别结构。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和Galaxy10 DECaLS数据集上的实验表明，仅利用混合数据身份即可恢复潜在类别及其在混合中的比例，缩小了弱监督与全监督性能之间的差距。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种数学上严谨、可扩展的多类别发现工具，适用于标签稀缺的领域，通过混合数据身份即可实现有效的多类别分类。

Abstract: In many classification problems, reliable instance-level labels are unavailable. However, it is often possible to construct weakly enriched unlabeled samples: datasets selected by different cuts, sources, populations, or experimental conditions that change latent class proportions without revealing them. Classification without Labels (CWoLa) shows that, in the binary case ($K=2$), a classifier trained to distinguish two impure mixtures with different class proportions can recover an optimal class discriminator without knowing the mixture proportions. We extend this principle to multiclass learning from several unlabeled mixtures ($K>2$), where the learner observes only mixture identity and neither latent class labels nor class-prior matrices. We prove that, for a multiclass mixture model, the Bayes-optimal mixture classifier $g^\star$ maps data points into a $(K-1)$-simplex embedded in mixture-posterior space. The $K$ vertices of this simplex are induced by the latent classes through the unknown mixing matrix. Leveraging this geometry, we propose prior-free procedures that train a standard classifier to distinguish mixture identities and then extract latent class structure using either post-hoc simplex fitting or a bottleneck architecture. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Galaxy10 DECaLS show that mixture identity alone can recover latent classes and their fractions in the mixture. By narrowing the gap between weakly supervised and fully supervised performance, we provide a mathematically grounded, scalable tool for multiclass discovery in label-scarce domains.

</details>


### [17] [Eliminating Propagation Delay: Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24885)
*Jinpeng Chen,Ziyu Yu,Tao Wang,Jun Ma,Hongbo Gao,Senzhang Wang,Zufeng Zhang,Kaimin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为A-STFGCN的注意力时空融合图卷积网络，通过设计时空融合模块消除传播延迟误差，并利用基于掩码矩阵的多头自注意力机制捕获长短期时序特征，在五个真实数据集上取得了最佳性能，同时具有较高的计算和数据利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图卷积模型存在两个问题：1）忽视了相邻节点间不同的信息传播延迟；2）堆叠复杂结构导致训练时间过长，忽略了模型的时效性需求。

Method: 提出A-STFGCN网络，设计时空融合模块（Spatial-Temporal Fusion Block）去除传播延迟误差以提取时空特征相关性，并基于掩码矩阵的多头自注意力机制捕获数据的长期和短期时序特征。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，与八种基线方法相比，该方法在整体性能最优的同时，具有良好的计算和数据利用效率。

Conclusion: A-STFGCN通过解决传播延迟和时效性问题，在交通流预测任务上实现了性能与效率的平衡，是一种有效的时空融合图卷积网络。

Abstract: Predicting traffic flow is crucial to optimizing transportation systems and improving urban mobility. Many graph convolution-based models have been proposed to extract spatial-temporal features and predict traffic flow. However, most focus on spatial-temporal and semantic correlation in topological relationships. There are two primary problems to address. Firstly, the convolutional structure in the model focuses on utilizing static spatial dependencies and spatial-temporal relationships in topological structures, while neglecting the different information propagation delays between adjacent nodes in the convolution. Secondly, these methods often stack a large number of complex structures, resulting in a substantial increase in computational time during the model training phase, thereby disregarding the model's requirements for timeliness. In this paper, we propose a novel network called the Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network (A-STFGCN). We design a spatial-temporal fusion block to extract the spatial-temporal feature correlations with propagation delay errors removed and to capture both long-term and short-term temporal characteristics of the data within a multi-head self-attention mechanism based on a mask matrix. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves the best overall performance while having good computation and data utilization efficiency compared with the eight baseline methods.

</details>


### [18] [Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24983)
*Ziwei Zhang,Jonathan Yu-Meng Li,Zhihao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成式分布鲁棒优化（GDRO）框架，通过采样器-Sinkhorn配对方法，允许任意可采样的条件生成器作为名义模型，并将最坏情况分布限制在选定的条件生成器族内，无需似然或分数访问，在库存管理和社交导航任务上分别降低60%和50%的遗憾/碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在分布鲁棒优化中的应用存在权衡：接受任意采样器的方法不限制最坏情况分布到生成器族，而生成器参数化的对抗方法依赖特定模型访问（如似然、分数或训练数据），缺乏一个统一且模型无关的框架。

Method: 提出GDRO框架，核心是采样器-Sinkhorn配对：采样器精确表示条件分布，Sinkhorn散度无需似然访问即可比较分布，支持有限样本近似和可微的原始-对偶实现。

Result: 在显式和隐式生成器上，该方法在稀有上下文库存管理中减少60%遗憾，在SocialGAN导航中减少50%碰撞，相较名义决策有显著提升。

Conclusion: GDRO提供了一个灵活、模型无关的分布鲁棒优化框架，适用于各种生成模型，并通过理论保证（Lipschitz损失下Sinkhorn半径约束退化）和实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly adopted in distributionally robust optimization (DRO), but existing approaches trade off model compatibility and adversarial structure: methods that accept arbitrary samplers do not restrict worst-case laws to a generator family, while generator-parameterized adversaries rely on model-specific access such as likelihoods, scores, or training data. We propose Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization (GDRO), a principled framework that accepts any sampleable conditional generator as the nominal model and restricts worst-case laws to a chosen conditional generator family. The key is the sampler-Sinkhorn pairing: samplers represent the conditional laws exactly, while Sinkhorn divergence compares their induced distributions without likelihood access and can be estimated from samples alone. The resulting population problem admits a direct finite-sample approximation and differentiable primal-dual implementation at the active decision context. For Lipschitz losses, the population Sinkhorn radius bounds downstream degradation. Across explicit and implicit generators, our method reduces rare-context inventory regret by 60% and SocialGAN navigation collisions by 50% relative to nominal decisions.

</details>


### [19] [Mechanisms of Width Scaling in Normalized Residual Networks: The Effective Alignment Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24887)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Guangming Tan,Fangming Liu,Daning Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数保持的残差扩展中，从有限训练数据识别出的扩展方向能否在未见数据上保持有效的问题。引入有效对齐维度（effective alignment dimension）这一可测量量，描述激活梯度的信号-噪声几何结构，并推导了训练与测试梯度内积的均值和方差，得到误对齐概率的有限样本上界。该边界仅依赖于有效对齐维度和有效样本量，无需协方差谱假设或预设宽度增长率。实验表明，更宽的模型具有更大的有效对齐维度和更低的经验误对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络宽度理论主要刻画渐近极限，但对于有限训练数据下识别的扩展方向能否泛化到测试数据缺乏指导。本文旨在解决这一实际问题，为残差扩展提供有限样本保证。

Method: 提出有效对齐维度（effective alignment dimension）作为信号-噪声几何结构的度量；推导训练梯度与测试梯度内积的精确均值和方差；建立误对齐概率的有限样本上界，该上界仅需有限二阶矩和非零总体梯度，不依赖协方差谱假设或预设宽度增长率；将该理论证书集成到训练-测试残差扩展框架中，得到测试风险改善的高概率条件。

Result: 实验表明：更宽的模型（LLaMA风格Transformer、Pythia、ResNet-20）表现出更大的有效对齐维度和更低的经验误对齐；直接残差干预实验证实对齐统计量可以预测保留集损失变化的符号和幅度。

Conclusion: 有效对齐维度是衡量残差扩展方向泛化能力的有效指标，理论边界在实际中具有预测力，为网络宽度与泛化性能的关系提供了更细粒度的理解。

Abstract: Existing theories of neural-network width characterize asymptotic limits, but provide limited guidance on whether an expansion direction identified from finite training data remains beneficial on unseen data. We study this problem for function-preserving residual expansion and introduce the effective alignment dimension, a measurable quantity describing the signal-noise geometry of activation gradients. By deriving the exact mean and variance of the inner product between independently estimated training and test gradients, we obtain a finite-sample upper bound on misalignment probability. The bound depends only on the effective alignment dimension and an effective sample size, requiring finite second moments and a nonzero population gradient, without covariance spectral assumptions or prescribed width-growth rates. We integrate this certificate into the train-test residual-expansion framework, yielding a high-probability condition for test-risk improvement. Experiments across width-controlled LLaMA-style Transformers, Pythia, and ResNet-20 show that wider models exhibit larger effective alignment dimensions and lower empirical misalignment. Direct residual interventions confirm that the alignment statistic predicts the sign and magnitude of held-out loss changes.

</details>


### [20] [GAUGE: Grading Agent-Built Financial Models Without a Golden Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24889)
*Jiacheng Lu,Sinuo Wang,Wentao Zhao,Rui Sun,Cheng Hua,Tao Song,Hui Cai,Beidi Luan,Zhengze Wu,Lingjing Teng,Yijia He,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai,Haibing Guan*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融模型评测基准使用单点参考答案（如唯一合理的目标价）来评分，但通过分析65家公司108对独立构建的分析师模型发现，中位数单点参考评分仅0.33，92.6%低于0.70，没有同一时期的分析师对在10%范围内对隐含价格达成一致。因此点容差评分会惩罚专业分析师之间本就存在的合理分歧，需要建立基于实际分析师实践的多答案评测基准。

Method: 构建GAUGE基准：使用1001份供应商分类的分析师工作簿和196项评测任务，设计三层观测实践包络（允许合理差异范围）、56个可审计层面、八个有效性验证门和确定性结构检查。通过55人已知组别研究（资深分析师、初级分析师、金融学生）、公司分组交叉验证和评审稳定性审核进行验证。采用失败感知评分φ₀，并评测24个智能体的1011次生成结果。

Result: 资深分析师平均φ₀得分88.3，初级分析师66.0，金融学生43.2。最佳智能体得分53.4，高于学生均值但低于所有资深分析师和大多数初级分析师。智能体通过93%的机械性层面和78%的判断性层面，舰队中位数差距为26分。现有智能体在模型构建能力上显著强于估值判断能力。

Conclusion: GAUGE基准通过基于分析师实践的多答案评测框架，更合理地评估金融模型构建智能体的能力。当前智能体与专业分析师之间存在显著差距，尤其在需要专业判断的估值环节。研究公开了方法论、去标识化数据分层、受控训练集、48任务评测核心和预留刷新池。

Abstract: Financial models combine public disclosures with analyst assumptions to produce forecasts and valuations. While some components can be checked mechanically, forecasts, discount rates, and target prices often admit multiple reasonable answers. Existing benchmarks nevertheless tend to grade such outputs against a single expert reference. Using independently built analyst models for the same companies, we find that across 108 directed pairs covering 65 companies, the median single-reference score is 0.33, 92.6% score below 0.70, and no same-vintage pair agrees on implied price within 10%. Point-tolerance grading can therefore penalize disagreement already present among professionals. We introduce GAUGE, a benchmark for evaluating agent-built valuation models against observed analyst practice rather than a single point answer. GAUGE uses 1,001 vendor-classified analyst workbooks and a 196-task evaluation set, with a three-layer observed-practice envelope, 56 auditable facets, eight validity gates, and deterministic structural checks. We validate the benchmark with a 55-participant known-groups study, company-grouped cross-fitting, and judge-stability audits. On the failure-aware score $φ_0$, senior analysts average 88.3, juniors 66.0, and finance students 43.2. Across 24 agents and 1,011 scored generations, the best agent scores 53.4, above the student mean but below every senior and most juniors. It passes 93% of mechanical facets and 78% of judgment facets, with a fleet-median gap of 26 points. Current agents are substantially stronger at model construction than valuation judgment. We release the methodology, a gated de-identified data tier, a controlled training split, a versioned 48-task evaluation core, and a withheld refresh pool.

</details>


### [21] [Algorithmic Separation between Constant-Depth and Logarithmic-Depth Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25200)
*Yunwei Ren,Zihao Wang,Jason D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了恒定深度网络与对数深度网络之间存在算法层面的分离：对数深度网络可以通过分层坐标下降高效学习一类具有层次化傅里叶谱的布尔函数，而恒定深度网络在该任务的某些子类上必然产生常数级别的L^2近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深层网络在实际中优于浅层网络，但理论上的深度分离主要集中在表示能力（逼近能力），而算法层面的结果大多限于两层与三层网络的比较。作者希望填补这一空白，证明在算法可学习性方面更深网络与恒定深度网络之间存在本质差异。

Method: 作者识别了一类具有层次化傅里叶谱结构的布尔函数，并证明对数深度网络可以通过分层坐标下降（layerwise coordinate descent）以层次化和自适应地重建谱结构，从而高效学习。同时，针对一个子类，作者证明任何具有正则激活函数和受控谱范数的恒定深度、多项式宽度网络，在超立方体上的均匀分布下必然产生常数级别的L^2逼近误差。

Result: 首次在算法层面证明了恒定深度网络与对数深度网络之间的分离：存在一类函数，对数深度网络可以高效学习，而恒定深度网络无法以低误差逼近。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了深度网络在算法可学习性上超越浅层网络的第一个严格理论证明，揭示了深度在算法效率而非仅仅表示能力上的关键作用。

Abstract: Despite the empirical advantages of deep networks over shallow ones, theoretical depth separations largely concern approximation power, while algorithmic results are mostly limited to comparisons between two- and three-layer networks. In this work, we prove the first algorithmic separation between constant-depth and logarithmic-depth networks.
  Specifically, we identify a class of Boolean functions with hierarchically structured Fourier spectra that logarithmic-depth networks can learn efficiently using layerwise coordinate descent by reconstructing the spectra hierarchically and adaptively. We also exhibit a subclass for which every constant-depth, polynomial-width network with sufficiently regular activations and controlled spectral norms must incur constant $L^2$ approximation error under the uniform distribution over the hypercube.

</details>


### [22] [LLM as Forecasting Planner: Training-Free Text Conditioning for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24892)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dai Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAFP框架，将TSFM作为模拟器生成数值轨迹，LLM作为策略和价值函数通过MCTS进行规划，无需训练即可实现文本条件时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件时间序列预测需要同时具备可靠的数值预测能力和文本上下文理解能力，但TSFM与LLM融合困难——直接让LLM生成或修正预测值会破坏TSFM捕获的时间结构。

Method: 将预测建模为规划问题：冻结的TSFM作为模拟器生成数值延续序列，LLM分为Ranker（作为策略函数指导候选选择）和Judge（作为价值函数评估完成轨迹），通过MCTS在预测时域上进行搜索，无需额外训练。

Result: 在Context-is-Key和Time-MMD数据集上，使用两种TSFM骨干（Chronos和TimesFM）和四种LLM，LAFP在所有模型组合下均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 序列搜索是一种有效的免训练文本条件预测方法，LAFP框架成功弥合了TSFM和LLM之间的模态差距，无需重新训练任一模型。

Abstract: Text-conditioned time-series forecasting predicts a series from both its numerical history and natural-language context, allowing forecasts to account for events and constraints that the past alone cannot reveal. This requires both reliable numerical forecasting and the ability to interpret contextual information. Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide strong numerical forecasts, while large language models (LLMs) can reason over text, but combining their strengths remains challenging because asking an LLM to generate or revise forecast values directly can distort the temporal structure captured by the TSFM. We instead formulate forecasting as a planning problem over TSFM-generated trajectories. The frozen TSFM acts as a simulator that proposes numerical continuations, while the LLM acts as a policy and value function that guides candidate selection and evaluates completed trajectories against the context. We instantiate this as \rc{} (\textbf{L}LM \textbf{A}s \textbf{F}orecasting \textbf{P}lanner), a training-free framework that bridges the modality gap without retraining either model, using Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) over the forecast horizon with a \emph{Ranker} LLM as policy and a \emph{Judge} LLM as value function. Experiments on Context-is-Key and Time-MMD across two TSFM backbones (Chronos and TimesFM) and four LLMs show that \rc{} delivers consistent improvements across model choices, supporting sequential search as an effective training-free approach to text-conditioned forecasting.

</details>


### [23] [Inverse RL Helps Align AI by Imitating Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24900)
*Michał Wiliński,Liu Leqi,Chirag Nagpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PARED方法，从演示中恢复隐含奖励函数，将其表示为响应层面特征的显式函数，通过轻量级判别器区分演示与策略样本，实现对AI的对齐优化，无需偏好标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法（SFT、RLHF）依赖人工偏好标注或奖励模型，但未充分探索是否仅凭演示就能获得可检查、可重用且可在策略上优化的隐式奖励来对齐AI。

Method: 受逆强化学习启发，PARED通过轻量级判别器在响应层面特征空间中区分演示样本与策略自身样本，从而恢复演示背后的隐式奖励作为这些特征的显式函数。

Result: PARED在推理时重排和对抗性在策略RL实验中提升了基础策略性能，在标准SFT后进一步优化仍有增益，并实现了面向不同受众偏好的上下文对齐。

Conclusion: 仅凭演示即可恢复可用的隐式奖励，无需任务特定的偏好标注；该奖励可用于多种对齐策略，并支持上下文相关的灵活对齐。

Abstract: Language model alignment aims to make model behavior reliably reflect desirable properties such as helpfulness, safety, and instruction following. Current approaches typically use supervised fine-tuning on demonstrations or reinforcement learning with rewards derived from verifiers or human feedback. These paradigms leave an important question underexplored: can demonstrations alone yield an implicit reward that can be inspected, reused, and optimized on-policy to align AI? Motivated by inverse reinforcement learning, we introduce Projected Alignment Reward Estimated from Demonstrations (PARED). PARED recovers the implicit reward underlying expert demonstrations as an explicit function over a small set of response-level features, learned by a lightweight discriminator that separates demonstrations from the policy's own samples in this feature space. Unlike a standard reward model, PARED requires no task-specific preference annotations: demonstrations provide the task-specific supervision, which can be augmented with AI feedback as additional dimensions of supervision. Through experiments involving inference-time reranking and adversarial on-policy RL, we show that the recovered reward improves a base policy without a supervised loss and yields further gains when optimized after standard supervised fine-tuning. Additionally, we demonstrate that PARED can be used for contextual alignment, in which a single policy can be tailored to the preferences of different audiences.

</details>


### [24] [Contextual Deconvolution for Variance-Stable Demand Sensing: Kernel-Modulated Operators in Promotional Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25664)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出**上下文解卷积**（CD），一种两阶段需求感知估计器，通过核调制带状算子将促销驱动的瞬态冲击与平滑结构基线分离，并利用分层部分池化实现目录级部署。在M5（30,490 SKU）和Favorita（2,845 SKU）数据集上，CD在安全库存、持有成本和订单方差上表现更优，但会欠供事件峰值，仅在持有成本超过缺货成本约20%时降低总成本。其核心贡献在于预测可靠性（最低的跨截面误差离散度）而非中心趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习需求预测虽优化统计精度，却会留下过度的运营波动性，导致安全库存膨胀和牛鞭效应加剧。现有方法在促销驱动场景下难以同时实现统计精度与运营稳定性。

Method: 两阶段估计器：第一阶段使用核调制带状算子将需求分解为促销冲击（瞬态）和结构基线（平滑）；第二阶段通过分层部分池化实现大规模目录级部署，无需每个SKU单独训练。算子由数据驱动而非预设，在促销响应为脉冲性时退化为恒等映射。

Result: 在M5（30,490 SKU）和Favorita（2,845 SKU）数据集上，CD在安全库存、持有成本和订单方差方面优于基准方法。但会欠供事件峰值，仅在持有成本超过缺货成本约20%（95% CI [17%,25%]）时降低总成本。CD在11个基线中实现了最低的跨截面误差离散度，误预测SKU比例仅0.8%（基准为9.9-20.6%）。

Conclusion: CD的精度贡献在于可靠性而非中心趋势。当持有成本较低时，它是一个运营稳定性和库存资本层，而非期望成本最小化器。方差比率和基于标准差的安全库存应视为诊断指标而非优化目标。

Abstract: Machine learning demand forecasts optimize statistical accuracy yet leave excess operational volatility that inflates safety stock and amplifies the Bullwhip effect. We introduce \textbf{Contextual Deconvolution} (CD), a two-stage estimator that reframes demand sensing as a convex decomposition: a kernel-modulated banded operator separates transient promotion-driven shocks from a smooth structural baseline, and hierarchical partial pooling enables catalog-scale deployment without per-SKU training. The operator is data-derived, not imposed---it reduces to the identity wherever the promotional response is impulsive (most of M5, all of Favorita) and contributes only where genuine multi-day carryover exists, so the gains rest on the structural decomposition itself. Evaluating strictly out-of-sample on 30,490 M5 SKUs and 2,845 Favorita items, with calendar-aware baselines given CD's identical future calendar, we anchor the contribution on a full inventory-cost accounting: CD lowers safety stock, holding cost, and order variance but under-provisions event spikes, reducing total cost only when holding costs exceed $\sim$20\% of stockout costs (95\% CI $[17\%,25\%]$); otherwise it is an operational-stability and inventory-capital layer, not an expected-cost minimizer. Its accuracy contribution is reliability rather than central tendency: across eleven baselines, CD attains the lowest cross-sectional dispersion of per-SKU error and mis-forecasts by more than 200\% on 0.8\% of SKUs versus 9.9--20.6\% for every baseline, ranking first on both in all four M5 draws. Because the Variance Ratio and std-based safety stock are minimized by any sufficiently smooth forecast, we treat them as diagnostics, not objectives. A supporting analysis shows the learned demand operators are non-normal, yet CD's compact parametric kernel matches their operational performance interpretably.

</details>


### [25] [Stable FP4 Training via Transposition-Invariant Block Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24953)
*Mehdi Rahimifar,Amin Darabi,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Xing Huang,Yao Wang,Zhijun Tu,Yufei Cui,Yunke Peng,Hongliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型低精度训练中FP4量化不稳定的问题，发现根本原因在于张量转置导致的前后向缩放因子不一致（scale inconsistency）。提出基于2D块FP4量化的训练框架，通过强制转置不变缩放保持前后向一致性，结合无截断缩放和随机舍入控制量化误差。在7B密集模型和30B MoE模型上验证，仅牺牲不到1.3%的困惑度和下游准确率即可稳定实现FP4训练，性能接近BF16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低精度训练（如FP8）在进一步推进到FP4时遭遇训练不稳定问题，阻碍了训练效率的进一步提升。作者试图找出FP4不稳定的根本原因并提出可行的解决方案。

Method: 提出2D块FP4量化框架，解决1D块量化中前向和反向因张量转置产生不同缩放因子的scale inconsistency问题；结合truncation-free scaling和stochastic rounding控制误差；对注意力机制的query/key采用MXFP8的混合精度设计。

Result: 在7B密集LLM和30B MoE模型、100B tokens训练规模下，FP4训练稳定且与BF16性能差距小于1.3%（困惑度和下游准确率）。

Conclusion: 保证前后向缩放一致性足以实现可扩展的FP4训练，为更高效的LLM训练提供了简单有效的路径。

Abstract: Reducing training precision is a key lever for improving the e ciency of large language model (LLM) training, but pushing beyond FP8 to 4-bit oating point (FP4) remains challenging due to instability during optimization. We identify a fundamental source of this instability in existing microscaling approaches: scale inconsistency induced by tensor transposition. In conventional 1D block quantization, forward and backward passes assign di erent scaling factors to the same values after transposition, leading to biased and unstable gradient updates. To address this issue, we propose a low-precision training framework based on 2D block FP4 quantization, which enforces transposition-invariant scaling and preserves consistency between forward and backward computations. We further combine this with truncation-free scaling and stochastic rounding to control quantization error and maintain unbiased gradients. To handle the sensitivity of attention mechanisms, we adopt MXFP8 quantization for query and key projections, yielding a practical mixed-precision design. We evaluate our method on dense LLMs up to 7B parameters and a 30B Mixture-of-Experts model, trained on up to 100B tokens. Across all settings, our approach achieves stable end-to-end FP4 training and closely matches BF16 performance, with less than 1.3% degradation in perplexity and downstream accuracy. These results demonstrate that enforcing forwardbackward scaling consistency is su cient to enable practical FP4 training at scale, providing a simple and e ective pathway toward more e cient LLM training.

</details>


### [26] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization and Muon: Robustness under the Spectral Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26001)
*Wenzhi Zhong,Edward Milsom,Michael Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种矩阵感知的几何方法应用于SAM（锐度感知最小化）的两个阶段：在内部扰动阶段引入逐层谱内扰动，在外部更新阶段结合Muon优化器。在ImageNet-1K上对ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50的实验表明，谱内扰动+Muon外更新的组合在两个模型上均取得最佳验证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM变体探索了多种几何选择，但哪些几何在实际中最有效缺乏清晰视角；受矩阵感知优化（尤其是Muon优化器）在隐层权重矩阵结构方面取得强实证表现的启发，作者希望将矩阵感知几何引入SAM的两个阶段以进一步提升泛化性能。

Method: 提出矩阵感知的SAM方法：(1) 内部扰动阶段，对矩阵值隐层参数采用逐层谱内扰动（spectral inner perturbation），而非传统的各向同性或各向异性扰动；(2) 外部更新阶段，将谱内扰动与AdamW/SGDW或Muon优化器结合，形成完整的优化流程。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K数据集上对ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50进行实验，谱内扰动+Muon外更新的组合在两种模型上均取得最高的验证准确率，优于其他评估方法。

Conclusion: 矩阵感知几何（尤其是谱内扰动+Muon外更新）在SAM框架中具有显著优势，为设计更有效的几何感知优化器提供了新方向。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) aims to improve generalization by encouraging insensitivity to small, worst-case parameter perturbations. However, the notion of a "small" perturbation is inherently geometry-dependent: while existing SAM variants have explored a wide range of choices, a clear perspective on which geometries are most effective in practice remains elusive. Recent work on matrix-aware optimization, particularly the Muon optimizer, suggests that respecting the matrix structure of hidden-layer weights can lead to strong empirical performance. Motivated by this, we study matrix-aware geometry in both stages of SAM: we introduce a layerwise spectral inner perturbation for matrix-valued hidden-layer parameters and combine it with either AdamW/SGDW or Muon in the outer update. Across ImageNet-1K experiments on ViT-Small/16 and ResNet-50, we find that the combination of a spectral inner step with a Muon outer step performs consistently strongly, achieving the best validation accuracy on both models among the evaluated methods.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了Reinformed Dreamer算法，通过潜在引导的异步表示学习目标，改进了异步模型-Based强化学习中特权信息表示的学习，在多个基准测试中比之前的异步方法更一致地提升了Dreamer的性能。

Abstract: Much like humans benefit from guidance while learning, reinforcement learning algorithms may benefit from additional supervision beyond rewards. Leveraging additional information during training to learn better representations and behaviors has been the focus of asymmetric reinforcement learning. This learning paradigm has proven effective under partial observability when additional state information is available, but also under full observability when more refined state information is available. Focusing on model-based reinforcement learning, we study the effect of asymmetric learning on observation representations and on privileged information representations. First, we identify a limitation in the privileged information representations learned by an asymmetric model-based algorithm known as the Informed Dreamer. Then, we propose a novel asymmetric representation learning objective using latent guidance, resulting in a new algorithm called the Reinformed Dreamer. Experiments across several benchmarks show a more consistent improvement over Dreamer than previous asymmetric approaches.

</details>

*Luc McCutcheon,Evangelos Chatzaroulas,Saber Fallah*

TL;DR: 本文提出校准部分重置（CPR）优化器，通过对低效用神经元进行按效用缩放的部分初始化重置，在保持训练稳定性的同时恢复可塑性，在持续学习基准中优于现有衰减和重置方法，且避免了策略崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在非平稳数据环境（如持续监督学习和强化学习）中会积累休眠神经元并丧失表达性，现有神经元重置方法虽可恢复可塑性，但完全重置会牺牲峰值性能并导致训练不稳定甚至策略崩溃。

Method: 提出Calibrated Partial Resets (CPR)优化器，周期性将低效用神经元向其初始化方向拉动，拉动强度按每个神经元的效用进行缩放校准。这是一种介于二进制重置（完全重置）与均匀衰减之间的部分重置方法。

Result: 在SlipperyAnt任务中，CPR是唯一在4亿训练步内避免策略崩溃的方法；在Continual MetaWorld和Continual MinAtar基准上优于先前的衰减和重置方法。消融实验揭示了可塑性-峰值性能之间的可调节权衡。

Conclusion: 效用缩放的校准部分重置是持续学习中一个富有前景的方向，能够在保持训练稳定性的同时有效维护网络可塑性。

Abstract: Neural networks are hindered by accumulating dormant neurons and loss of expressivity throughout training, particularly in non-stationary data settings, such as continual supervised and reinforcement learning. Recently, neuron resets have been used to maintain gradient flow and restore plasticity. However, full unit reinitialization often sacrifices peak performance and can destabilize training, leading to policy collapse.
  To preserve plasticity without destabilizing training, we propose Calibrated Partial Resets (CPR), an optimizer that periodically pulls low-utility neurons toward their initialization, with pull strength scaled by each neuron's utility. Unlike binary reset methods, partial resets avoid brittleness; unlike uniform decay, calibrated utility-scaling concentrates adjustment on the units that need it most.
  Among compared methods, only CPR avoids policy collapse over 400M training steps in SlipperyAnt, and it outperforms prior decay and reset-based methods on Continual MetaWorld and Continual MinAtar benchmarks. Ablations reveal a tunable trade-off between plasticity and peak performance, highlighting utility-scaled reinitialization as a promising direction for continual learning.

</details>


### [29] [Conformal Cascade: Distribution-Free Accuracy Guarantees for Multi-Tier LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25018)
*Yifan Dou,Shikan Fang,Shibo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Cascade (CC)，一种利用共形预测集大小作为延迟规则的多层推理框架，为LLM级联提供无分布、有限样本的准确性保证，在18个多项选择基准测试中优于基于校准的启发式级联方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM级联方法使用置信度阈值来决定查询是否延迟到更大模型，但LLM置信度分数校准不良、阈值需针对每对模型和每个领域调整，且无法提供级联准确性的形式化保证。

Method: 提出Conformal Cascade (CC)框架，使用共形预测集大小作为延迟规则：当校准后的预测集坍缩为单一答案时接受，否则延迟。通过逐层联合界提供无分布、有限样本的准确率保证，并刻画了期望级联成本与α和校准集接受率的显式函数关系。

Result: 在18个科学、医学、常识和标准化考试的多项选择基准测试上，使用四类开源模型系列的两层级联进行评估，CC在大多数模型-基准测试对上严格优于最强的校准调优启发式级联，在推理密集型基准上提升最大，在简单基准上大部分查询分配给小模型且无准确率损失。

Conclusion: CC无需模型训练，仅需黑盒API访问，为LLM级联提供了理论保证；扩展到开放文本生成需要答案聚类步骤，留待未来工作。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) cascades reduce inference cost by routing easy queries to a small model and deferring hard queries to a larger one. Production cascades govern this deferral through a confidence threshold, but LLM confidence scores are miscalibrated, the threshold must be tuned per model pair and per domain, and no setting yields a formal bound on cascade accuracy. We introduce \textbf{Conformal Cascade} (CC), a multi-tier inference framework that uses conformal prediction set size as the deferral rule: accept when the calibrated set collapses to a single answer, defer otherwise. The procedure delivers a distribution-free, finite-sample accuracy guarantee. By a per-tier union bound, the prediction set at the accepting tier covers the correct answer with probability at least $1 - Kα$ for any user-specified $α$; under a selection-preservation condition (consistent with, but not strictly implied by, our marginal coverage results), the bound tightens to $1 - α$. We further characterise expected cascade cost as an explicit function of $α$ and the calibration-set acceptance rate. Across 18 multiple-choice benchmarks spanning science, medicine, commonsense, and standardized exams, evaluated on two-tier cascades drawn from four open-weight model families, CC strictly improves over the strongest calibration-tuned heuristic cascade on the majority of family--benchmark pairs, with the largest gains on reasoning-heavy benchmarks where majority vote is unreliable; on easier benchmarks the cascade commits the vast majority of queries to the small model at no accuracy cost. Extension to open-ended generation requires an answer-clustering step that we leave for future work. The method requires no model training and only black-box API access.

</details>

*Eshed Gal,Eldad Haber,Uri Ascher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于评分模型的数值稳定化框架，通过学习评分模型定义稳定化算子，对偏微分方程数值求解过程中的临时更新进行校正，使其收敛到允许状态流形，从而增强时间推进格式的稳定性和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数值求解偏微分方程时，标准时间推进格式可能产生非物理的不稳定性或违反物理一致性。需要一种能够自动校正数值解、使其满足物理约束的通用稳定化方法。

Method: 利用学习得到的评分模型定义一个稳定化算子，在标准时间步进更新之后施加该算子，通过校正驱动迭代解向允许状态流形收缩。该算子被证明具有相对于该流形的压缩性质，从而实现盆地条件稳定性。

Result: 在Advection、Korteweg-de Vries (KdV)、Nonlinear Schrodinger (NLS)和Burgers方程上的数值实验表明，该方法提升了鲁棒性，抑制了非物理不稳定性，并保留了定性动力学行为。

Conclusion: 基于评分模型的稳定化框架能有效增强偏微分方程数值求解的稳定性和物理一致性，是一种具有通用性的校正机制。

Abstract: We propose a score-based stabilization framework for numerical simulation of partial differential equations, in which a learned score model defines a stabilization operator applied to provisional numerical updates. This operator augments standard time-stepping schemes by enforcing structure and physical consistency through a correction that drives iterates toward the manifold of admissible states. We show that the stabilization operator acts as a contraction toward this manifold, yielding a correction mechanism with basin-conditional stability. Numerical experiments on Advection, Korteweg-de Vries (KdV), Nonlinear Schrodinger (NLS), and Burgers' equations demonstrate improved robustness, suppression of nonphysical instabilities, and preservation of qualitative dynamics.

</details>


### [31] [Lantern: Conflict-Aware Gradient Blending for Physics-Guided Diffusion Models in Calorimeter Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25060)
*Farzana Yasmin Ahmad,Vanamala Venkataswamy,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lantern，一种物理引导的扩散模型，用于高能物理量热计簇射模拟。针对现有扩散模型纯统计目标与物理真实性的差距，引入相关Frobenius距离(CFD)作为相关性度量，并提出两种物理感知辅助损失：基于计数统计的方差稳定化体素残差损失和基于探测器几何的图拉普拉斯损失。通过GradBlend方法将辅助损失与去噪梯度结合，在CaloChallenge数据集2上相比其他梯度平衡方法显著提升FPD和CFD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛模拟量热计簇射是高亮度LHC的主要瓶颈，扩散模型虽能快速生成高保真替代结果，但其去噪目标纯统计性导致物理错误。现有物理信息生成方法假设闭合形式定律或偏微分方程残差，而簇射缺乏逐样本的PDE约束，能量守恒仅提供一个标量。标准评估指标忽略层间和体素间的相关结构。

Method: 提出相关Frobenius距离(CFD)作为层和体素尺度相关保真度的归一化评分；设计两种物理感知辅助损失：方差稳定化体素残差损失（基于计数统计）和图拉普拉斯损失（基于探测器几何）；通过GradBlend方法将辅助损失方向与去噪梯度结合，在保持步长大小的同时引导方向。

Result: 在CaloChallenge数据集2上，PCGrad、GradNorm等任务对称规则使FPD膨胀2-100倍，而GradBlend无回归地引入物理信号。结合图拉普拉斯损失的Lantern同时改善了FPD和CFD。体素残差损失与去噪梯度冲突，需要末段纯去噪阶段；非冲突的图拉普拉斯损失对调度不敏感。

Conclusion: Lantern证明了物理引导的扩散模型在量热计簇射模拟中的有效性，GradBlend方法在引入物理先验的同时避免了任务冲突导致的性能退化，为高能物理模拟提供了兼具统计保真度和物理正确性的生成方案。

Abstract: Monte Carlo simulation of calorimeter showers is a principal bottleneck for the High-Luminosity LHC, and diffusion models have emerged as fast, high-fidelity surrogates. Their denoising objective is purely statistical, however: a model can minimize it while placing the physics wrong. Existing physics-informed generative methods cannot close this gap, because they assume a closed-form law, a governing PDE residual or a hard per-sample constraint, that a shower does not supply: no per-sample PDE governs a stochastic cascade, and energy conservation fixes only one scalar per shower. Standard metrics ignore the correlation structure across calorimeter layers and voxels, comparing showers only in a physics feature space. We address both gaps. We introduce the Correlation Frobenius Distance (CFD), a single normalized score for correlation fidelity at layer-wise and voxel-wise scales. We then encode the soft per-sample structure available in a shower as two physics-aware auxiliary losses: a variance-stabilized voxel residual loss grounded in counting statistics, and a graph Laplacian loss over the detector geometry. We combine both with denoising through GradBlend, which anchors the step magnitude to the denoising gradient while letting the auxiliary steer its direction, yielding Lantern, a physics-guided diffusion surrogate. On CaloChallenge Dataset 2, injecting the physics losses through task-symmetric rules such as PCGrad, GradNorm, IMTL-G, and ConFIG inflates FPD by 2-100x relative to denoising alone, whereas GradBlend admits the same signal without regression and, with the Laplacian loss, Lantern improves both FPD and CFD. Our ablation on the auxiliary loss scheduler shows that the voxel residual loss, whose gradient conflicts with denoising, requires a terminal denoising-only phase to preserve shower fidelity, whereas the non-conflicting Laplacian loss is insensitive to the schedule.

</details>


### [32] [Semantic Space Search Trajectory Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25122)
*Julian Agudelo,Alberto Tonda,Gabriela Ochoa,Vincent Guigue,Cristina Manfredotti,Evelyne Lutton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在语义空间（模型在固定样本集上的预测空间）中构建搜索轨迹网络（STN）的方法，通过离散化语义向量并采用基于归一化汉明距离的完全链接凝聚聚类聚合为网络节点，从而实现对不同机器学习算法（包括分类与回归任务）以及不同训练范式（如标准训练与标签随机化）的学习动态进行可视化和比较。实验表明，该语义空间STN能够捕捉训练过程中算法与数据交互产生的功能动态差异，例如真实标签训练产生的网络更密集、更高效、更中心化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统STN依赖于搜索空间的离散化，主要适用于低维或组合优化问题，难以应用于机器学习中复杂的高维参数空间。作者希望将STN扩展到能够表征和比较不同机器学习模型学习动态的通用框架中。

Method: 在语义空间（模型对固定样本集的预测向量）中构建STN：首先离散化语义向量，然后使用基于归一化汉明距离的完全链接凝聚聚类将相似语义向量聚合成网络节点，节点之间根据遍历顺序构建有向边，形成搜索轨迹网络。

Result: 语义空间STN成功恢复了不同机器学习算法之间已知的定性差异；在神经网络泛化研究中，真实标签训练产生的STN相比随机标签训练具有更密集、更高效、更中心化的图结构特征。

Conclusion: 语义空间STN能够有效捕捉训练过程中算法与数据交互产生的功能动态，为跨模型、跨训练范式的学习动态分析和比较提供了一种通用工具。

Abstract: Search Trajectory Networks (STNs) are a graph-based tool for visualizing and characterizing the behavior of optimization algorithms. STNs' reliance on discretization of the search space has largely confined them to low-dimensional or combinatorial settings. We introduce a methodology for constructing STNs in semantic spaces, defined as the space of a model's predictions on a fixed sample set. Our approach discretizes semantic vectors and aggregates them into network nodes via agglomerative clustering with complete linkage under a normalized Hamming distance. Since any predictor can be summarized by its semantic vector, this method enables comparison of learning dynamics across otherwise incomparable algorithm families. We apply semantic space STNs to classification and regression tasks solved using different machine learning algorithms, recovering known qualitative differences between them. Additionally, we use semantic space STNs to study neural network generalization by contrasting standard training with the label randomization regime of Zhang et al. (2017). The resulting STNs exhibit consistent structural differences, training on real labels produces denser, more efficient and more centralized graphs than training on shuffled labels. Together, our results show that semantic space STNs capture functional training dynamics arising from the interaction between learning algorithms and data, providing a tool for analyzing and comparing learning dynamics across machine learning models and training regimes.

</details>


### [33] [Endpoint Replay: Compressing the Recency Buffer in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25123)
*Parham Mohammad Panahi,Armin Ashrafi,Haoyu Du,Andrew Patterson,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于n步序列端点压缩的经验回放方法，通过存储链式n步序列的端点而非全部经验，在保持与大缓冲区相当性能的同时，将存储需求降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DRL使用的大型均匀采样回放缓冲区存在数据冗余和存储效率低下的问题，且大多基于启发式设置（如大小为1M），缺乏对如何更有效压缩和利用经验的系统性研究。

Method: 构建n步序列的链式连接，仅存储每条链的端点作为代表性转换，放入较小的近期缓冲区中，从而在维持有效记忆范围的同时大幅压缩存储。

Result: 在Pinball环境和Atari 2600基准测试中，该方法避免了朴素压缩策略的系统性偏差，匹配了传统大缓冲区的性能表现，且存储需求降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的端点存储策略，可以有效压缩经验回放缓冲区而不损失性能，为DRL中更高效的经验存储提供了新思路。

Abstract: Experience replay remains one of the most practical and useful algorithmic tools in the deep reinforcement learning (DRL) toolbox. Aside from the limited success of prioritized replay and specialized approaches for large asynchronous systems, most DRL algorithms make use of a large, uniformly sampled recency buffer---even the size, one million, remains unchanged. Could we store less data, reduce redundancy, or more effectively chain experience together to speed up value propagation and still retain the performance of large buffers? In this paper, we investigate a simple compression approach that stores representative transitions derived from the end-points of a chain of connected $n$-step sequences. By curating these end-points in a smaller recency buffer, our method maintains an effective memory horizon comparable to a standard large buffer while requiring an order of magnitude less storage. Through empirical evaluation, we demonstrate that this approach prevents the systematic bias inherent in naive compression strategies and matches the performance of traditional large buffers in the Pinball environment and the Atari 2600 benchmark.

</details>


### [34] [Interpretable GOHR Agents via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25132)
*Shiwei Tan,Yusong Zhao,Weiyi Qin,Wentian Wang,Jacob Feldman,Lazaros K. Gallos,Paul B. Kantor,Vladimir Menkov,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）分析Transformer智能体在隐藏规则游戏（GOHR）中的内部表征，发现其决策token嵌入中的SAE维度能够恢复隐藏规则结构，并与可解释策略（如试探规则假设、负反馈后切换）相对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解学习型决策系统的内部表征是否包含有助于解释其行为的概念，尤其是在规则信息必须从交互历史中隐式推断、且正确规则在信息性动作之前不可识别的设置下。

Method: 使用token化自回归Transformer智能体在GOHR的两规则任务中训练策略，在固定权重下评估；在决策token嵌入上训练稀疏自编码器（SAEs），分析其维度与形状、桶等简单概念的对应关系。

Result: SAE中高度选择特定概念的维度覆盖了该概念出现的大部分决策；单个SAE维度对应于可解释策略，如试探一个规则假设并在负反馈后切换。

Conclusion: 稀疏自编码器能够从Transformer智能体的隐式推理中恢复隐藏规则的结构化信息，揭示其内部概念与行为策略之间的对应关系。

Abstract: A central challenge in interpreting learned decision-making systems is to determine whether their internal representations contain concepts that help explain their behavior. We report interpretability experiments for a tokenized autoregressive Transformer agent in the Game of Hidden Rules (GOHR). We focus on a compact two-rule task in which both hidden rules map object shapes to target buckets, but with different permutations. The policy is trained on episodes sampled from these two hidden rules and then evaluated with fixed weights. It is never given a rule label and does not use an explicit rule classifier; any rule information must be inferred implicitly from interaction history. In this setting, the correct rule is not identifiable before the agent tries an informative move and observes accept/reject feedback. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) trained on the agent's decision-token embeddings recover this structure. When held-out decisions are labeled by simple concepts such as the chosen shape or bucket, SAE dimensions that are highly selective for a concept cover most decisions where that concept is present. Individual SAE dimensions also correspond to interpretable strategies such as probing one rule hypothesis and switching after negative feedback.

</details>


### [35] [Physics-Informed CNN-LSTM for Street-Scale Urban Flood Prediction: Reconciling Aggregate Accuracy and Street-Level Plausibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25148)
*Luc DCosta,Yidi Wang,Jonathan L. Goodall,Rohan Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息约束的训练框架，将重力损失、连续性损失和地形感知误报惩罚嵌入CNN-LSTM模型的损失函数中，用于城市洪水深度预测。该方法在诺福克洪水数据集上实现近零重力违例（10⁻⁶量级），街道级召回率达0.77（基线为0.44），并通过地形湿度指数（TWI）调制有效平衡了聚合像素误差与物理合理性之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习代理模型使用均方误差损失训练，虽然统计上准确，但产生的洪水预测在物理上不可行（如水向上流、自发出现、街道级通道被平滑），导致预测结果无法满足交通路由等实际应用需求。

Method: 在CNN-LSTM模型的损失函数中嵌入三个可微惩罚项：(i) 重力损失，惩罚逆水表面高程梯度增加水深的行为；(ii) 连续性损失，使用雨量自适应阈值强制局部质量守恒；(iii) 地形感知误报惩罚，通过地形湿度指数（TWI）调制。模型在128×128空间网格上以15分钟间隔预测，使用诺福克洪水数据集（两次风暴事件、300个样本）进行评估。

Result: 物理约束模型实现近零重力违例（10⁻⁶量级），街道级召回率0.77±0.09（基线0.44±0.10），在留出风暴测试中优势翻倍以上。均匀误报惩罚变体MAE降低16%但街道召回率降至0.25。TWI调制惩罚在所有指标上均优于均匀变体，在约束变体中MAE最低，街道召回率恢复60%，街道F1最优。

Conclusion: 聚合像素级误差与应用特定物理合理性之间存在根本性张力，基于地形的损失调制为解决这一矛盾提供了原则性方案。

Abstract: Deep learning surrogate models trained with mean-squared-error loss produce statistically accurate but physically unconstrained flood predictions: water may flow uphill, appear spontaneously, or smooth over street-level corridors. We develop a physics-informed training framework for CNN-LSTM models that predict urban flood depths at 15 min intervals over a 128x128 spatial grid. Three differentiable penalty terms are embedded into the loss: (i) a gravity loss penalizing depth increases against the water-surface-elevation gradient, (ii) a continuity loss enforcing local mass conservation with rainfall-adaptive thresholds, and (iii) a topography-aware false-alarm penalty modulated by the topographic wetness index (TWI). We evaluate on the Norfolk, Virginia flood dataset spanning two storm events (August 2017 and September 2022, 300 samples), with all variants trained on identical splits and robustness assessed over repeated random splits and leave-one-storm-out tests. A road-proximal evaluation restricted to a TWI-derived street mask quantifies street-level skill. The physics-constrained model achieves near-zero gravity violations (order 1e-6) and the highest street-channel recall (0.77 +/- 0.09 vs 0.44 +/- 0.10 for the unconstrained baseline), the capability most relevant to traffic routing, and its advantage more than doubles on a held-out storm; a uniform false-alarm variant attains 16% lower mean absolute error but suppresses street recall to 0.25. The TWI-modulated penalty reconciles this trade-off: it improves on the uniform variant on every metric, recovering 60% higher street recall at the lowest MAE among constrained variants and the best street-level F1. These results expose a fundamental tension between aggregate pixel-level error and application-specific physical plausibility, and show that terrain-aware loss modulation offers a principled resolution.

</details>

*Vilya Research,:,Pascal Sturmfels,Naozumi Hiranuma,Milad Salem,Benjamin D. Sellers,Stephen Rettie,CJ San Felipe,Chase A. P. Wood,Jeffrey K. Holden,Adam P. Moyer,Patrick J. Salveson,Ivan Anishchanka*

TL;DR: Vilya-2是一个基于扩散变换器的全原子结构预测模型，能从单个分子扩展到蛋白质-分子相互作用建模，在肽类、小分子、大环化合物和微型蛋白的结构预测上表现卓越，并可作为基础模型用于药物发现。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: Vilya-2采用扩散变换器架构，将Vilya-1的全原子表示从单分子建模扩展到分子与蛋白质靶标的相互作用建模。通过全原子表示实现不同分子类型间的迁移学习，生成多样化结构集成并通过校准置信度进行排序。

Result: Vilya-2恢复59.1%的肽界面至亚2Å主链RMSD，远超共折叠模型（即使提供了结合受体模板）；在小分子对接上达到SOTA；泛化至训练中未见的新型蛋白质-小分子复合物；可建模比训练分子大数倍的大环化合物和二硫键稳定微型蛋白。

Conclusion: Vilya-2建立的全原子方法可作为从头肽类治疗药物设计与评估的通用基础，统一了预测准确性与化学空间泛化能力，是推动从头肽类设计管线所需的结构预测核心工具。

Abstract: Structure-prediction networks built on co-evolutionary statistics have transformed protein-based drug discovery, yet their accuracy does not extend to peptide therapeutics--an increasingly important modality defined by non-canonical residues, macrocyclization, and complex topologies. We introduce Vilya-2, a diffusion transformer that extends the all-atom representation of Vilya-1 from modeling individual molecules to modeling their interactions with protein targets. This all-atom representation enables transfer learning between different molecular types, and delivers highly accurate structural modeling of peptides across sizes, classes, and compositions bound to therapeutically relevant targets. By generating diverse structural ensembles and ranking them with calibrated confidence, Vilya-2 recovers 59.1% of peptide interfaces to sub-2 Å backbone RMSD, far exceeding the performance of a representative co-folding model even when that model is given the bound receptor as a template. In addition, Vilya-2 is state-of-the-art at small-molecule docking, and generalizes to novel protein-small molecule complexes unlike those seen in training. It also generalizes to modeling molecular conformations of diverse macrocycles and disulfide-stapled miniproteins several-fold larger than any molecule seen in training. Finally, Vilya-2 can be used as a foundation model, and fine-tuned to enrich for active compounds in hit-to-lead campaigns. By unifying predictive accuracy with broad generalizability across chemical space, Vilya-2 is the structure-prediction oracle that de novo peptide design pipelines require--establishing the all-atom approach as a general foundation for the design and evaluation of de novo peptide therapeutics.

</details>


### [37] [CondPSE: A Polynomial-Filtered Structural Encoder with Conditional Modulation for Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25169)
*Woohyun Lee,Hogun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CondPSE是一种通过可学习多项式滤波器组和图条件调制增强的图位置/结构编码器，在合成图结构区分任务上显著优于GPSE（CSL准确率从42.9%提升至97.3%，EXP从68.3%提升至99.9%），但在真实分子性质预测任务中表现与GPSE相当，表明强合成结构区分能力并不直接转化为下游任务的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递图神经网络受限于1-WL测试，无法区分某些非同构图。现有位置/结构编码（PSE）虽能注入拓扑信息，但GPSE等学习型编码器在结构区分能力上仍有不足。作者旨在设计一种更强的学习型PSE编码器，能够在合成结构区分基准上超越1-WL界限，并探究其在下游任务中的实际效用。

Method: CondPSE包含三部分：(1) 可学习多项式图滤波器组，对标准高斯节点探针进行滤波，产生多分支结构响应；(2) FiLM-style条件调制机制，结合跨滤波器、局部消息传递和图级信号对分支进行精细化调节；(3) 预训练阶段重建节点级位置/结构目标及图级不变量。预训练后冻结编码器，作为下游模型的输入编码。

Result: (1) 合成结构区分：CSL准确率42.9%→97.3%，EXP准确率68.3%→99.9%，消融实验表明多项式滤波器组贡献最大。(2) 真实分子性质预测：在混合局部消息传递/全局注意力骨干下，CondPSE与GPSE性能相当；在ZINC骨干上未呈现一致的优势排序。(3) 强合成结构区分能力并未直接转化为下游任务优势。

Conclusion: 学习型PSE编码器在合成结构区分任务上的显著提升不一定能迁移至真实下游任务，原因可能包括：下游集成方式的限制、结构预训练目标与分子性质标签之间的不匹配。需要进一步研究PSE编码器与下游模型的有效集成策略。

Abstract: Message-passing graph neural networks are bounded by the 1-WL test and can miss topological structure that distinguishes non-isomorphic graphs. Positional and structural encodings (PSE) inject such topology-derived signals, and learned PSE encoders such as GPSE pretrain a single encoder to produce these signals from random node probes, which can then be frozen and reused as inputs across downstream graph models. We present CondPSE, a learned PSE encoder that applies a learnable polynomial graph filter bank to standard Gaussian node probes and refines the resulting structural-response branches through FiLM-style modulation conditioned on cross-filter, local message-passing, and graph-level signals. CondPSE is pretrained to reconstruct node-level positional/structural targets and graph-level invariants, and is then frozen for use as a downstream input encoding. On synthetic structural-discrimination benchmarks, CondPSE separates graph structures that 1-WL-bounded message passing cannot: it raises CSL accuracy from 42.9% to 97.3% and EXP accuracy from 68.3% to 99.9% relative to GPSE, and ablations show that the polynomial filter bank accounts for most of this gain. On real molecular property prediction, the picture is more limited. With a hybrid local-message-passing/global-attention backbone, CondPSE performs comparably to GPSE without surpassing it, and a ZINC backbone sweep shows no consistent ordering between the two encoders. We report these results and discuss why strong synthetic structural discrimination does not, on its own, yield a downstream advantage for frozen learned PSE encoders, including the role of downstream integration and possible mismatch between structural pretraining targets and molecular property labels.

</details>


### [38] [Rethinking CD: A Reproducibility Study and Extension on the Ineffectiveness of Contrastive Decoding at Mitigating Object Hallucinations in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25196)
*Arnav Bendre,Guneesh Gupta,Kavish Grover,Chayan Aggarwal,Shreyansh Modi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展了"对比解码在MLLM中无法缓解对象幻觉"的研究，验证了CD导致单向输出分布偏移、自适应合理性约束将采样退化为贪心搜索等主张，并通过多项实验证明CD带来的改进是虚假的，不能一致地转化为更强的视觉接地能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑对比解码（CD）在多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对象幻觉缓解任务中的有效性问题。尽管CD在POPE等基准上报告了性能提升，但近期研究质疑这些提升是否真正反映了视觉接地能力的改进，因此需要系统性地复现检验并深入探究CD的真实效果。

Method: 复现并扩展前人工作，具体包括：1) 验证CD在判别式数据集上诱导单向输出分布偏移的主张及其跨数据集泛化性；2) 验证自适应合理性约束（APC）在判别式和生成式基准上将采样退化为贪心搜索；3) 在生成式和判别式数据集上系统研究CD效果；4) 分析生成式数据集上不同CD策略诱导的logit分布；5) 提出代理方法并与CD技术对比；6) 研究幻觉信号在专家模型和业余模型各层中的传播情况。

Result: 在MME、POPE和CHAIR基准上使用LLaVA和Qwen的实验结果验证了原始主张：CD带来的表面改进往往是虚假的，并不能一致地转化为更强的视觉接地能力以缓解幻觉。

Conclusion: 当前对比解码策略的有效性受到挑战，需要开发更可靠的方法来缓解MLLM中的对象幻觉。

Abstract: Contrastive decoding (CD) has been proposed as a training-free strategy for mitigating object hallucinations in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), with reported gains on benchmarks such as POPE. However, recent work has questioned whether these gains reflect genuine improvements in visual grounding. In this study, we reproduce and extend the findings of "The Mirage of Performance Gains: Why Contrastive Decoding Fails to Mitigate Object Hallucinations in MLLMs." Specifically, we test the claim that CD induces a unidirectional output distribution shift in discriminative datasets and examine its generalizability across datasets. We also verify that the adaptive plausibility constraint (APC) reduces sampling to greedy search on both discriminative and generative benchmarks. Beyond reproduction, we rigorously study the effects of CD across generative and discriminative datasets. We conduct several experiments that provide additional insights: we analyze the logit distributions induced by different CD strategies on generative datasets, propose a proxy method and compare its performance against CD techniques, and investigate how hallucination signals propagate through each layer of the expert and amateur models. Experimental results across MME, POPE, and CHAIR using LLaVA and Qwen validate the original claims and show that the apparent improvements from CD are often spurious and do not consistently translate into stronger visual grounding for reducing hallucinations. These findings challenge the effectiveness of current contrastive decoding strategies and motivate the development of more reliable approaches for mitigating hallucinations in MLLMs.

</details>


### [39] [A Unified Algorithmic Framework for Hybrid Reinforcement Learning in Tabular MDPs with Shifted Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25207)
*Zheshun Wu,Renjie Zheng,Jinhang Zuo,Zenglin Xu,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合强化学习框架，通过MIN-UCB-VI和MAX-LCB-VI两种算法，在表格型MDP中有效利用来自动态转移环境的历史离线数据，并提供了理论最优性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据可能来自具有转移动态偏移的过时环境，简单整合历史数据效果不佳，因此需要一种能够应对转移偏移的混合强化学习框架。

Method: 提出统一算法框架，包含MIN-UCB-VI（遗憾最小化）和MAX-LCB-VI（最优策略识别）两种算法，利用细粒度偏差信息来更有效地利用具有一般转移偏移的离线数据。

Result: 提供了包括实例相关和实例无关的遗憾界与次优间隙上界，建立了匹配的下界以证明算法的最优性，并通过实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合强化学习框架在转移动态偏移下能够实现最优性能，为利用过时离线数据提供了理论保障。

Abstract: This paper investigates a hybrid reinforcement learning setting in tabular Markov Decision Processes (MDPs), where an agent aims to learn an optimal policy by combining online interactions with a target environment and offline data from a source environment. A central challenge is that offline data may be collected from outdated environments with shifted transition dynamics, making naive integration of historical data ineffective. To address this, we propose a unified algorithmic framework featuring two algorithms: MIN-UCB-VI for regret minimization and MAX-LCB-VI for best policy identification. Both algorithms leverage fine-grained bias information to more effectively exploit offline data under general transition shifts. We provide theoretical guarantees for our framework, including both instance-dependent and independent upper bounds on regret and sub-optimality gap. Furthermore, we establish matching lower bounds to demonstrate the optimality of our approach and validate our theoretical findings through extensive experiments.

</details>


### [40] [Neurai-VN Benchmark: Standardized Machine Learning Models for Multimodal Digital Phenotyping in Mental Health Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25232)
*Quoc-Cuong Pham,Hoang-Thuy-Duong Vu,Thi-Thanh-Huong Ha,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于Neurai-VN多模态数据集(100名越南成年人两周的智能手机和可穿戴设备数据)，构建了数字表型分析的可复现基准，针对四个临床相关二分类任务评估了线性、树模型和神经网络基线，结果显示健康对照vs抑郁和健康对照vs临床的F1分数达0.71，焦虑相关任务分别为0.69和0.56。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字表型分析在心理健康监测方面潜力巨大，但由于数据集异质性和预处理流程不一致，研究进展难以评估，亟需标准化的可复现基准。

Method: 基于Neurai-VN高分辨率多模态数据集(100名越南成年人两周数据)，定义四个临床相关的二分类任务，采用标准化subject-wise交叉验证，评估线性模型、树模型和神经网络基线在预定义特征配置下的表现。

Result: 健康对照vs抑郁和健康对照vs临床的F1分数达0.71，健康对照vs焦虑为0.69，抑郁vs焦虑为0.56。

Conclusion: 该基准为未来多模态数字表型分析在心理健康分类任务中的研究提供了可复现的基线。

Abstract: Digital phenotyping (DP) using smartphones and wearable devices has shown considerable potential for mental health monitoring. However, progress remains difficult to evaluate due to heterogeneous datasets, inconsistent preprocessing pipelines. In this study, we present a reproducible benchmark built upon the Neurai-VN dataset, a high-resolution, multimodal dataset comprising passive sensing and active assessment from wearable and smartphone devices, collected from 100 Vietnamese adults over two weeks.
