We propose two novel recognition approaches, Compositional Prototype Interpolation (CPI) and Synthetic Adaptation for Prototypes (SAP), that enable zero-shot learning of combined, novel and unseen movements in Prototype Networks after training only with basic movements. Our methods rest on a linear interpolation assumption in the embedding space, which we study by inspecting the geometry of combined motions in signal and embedding space. In experiments on the NearLab and NinaPro DB3 data sets as well as our newly recorded BasCom dataset, our proposed SAP outperforms prior zero-shot learning methods with accuracy improvements on combined movements of more than 20%. This advantage is maintained in online inference experiments in a user study.

</details>


### [78] [DRIFT: Direct-Recursive Intervention-Conditioned Forecasting of ICU Physiological Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25864)
*Weixin Liu,Juming Xiong,Congning Ni,Yanfan Zhu,Xingtao Lin,Bradley A. Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT是一个混合时间序列预测框架，结合直接预测和递归动作条件校正，在ICU患者血压预测中相比纯动作条件模型有适度但稳健的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型要么忽略ICU中的治疗干预（如血管升压药），要么采用自回归方式导致误差累积，无法有效利用治疗信息进行准确预测。

Method: DRIFT采用混合框架：直接模型生成主预测，递归的动作条件模型提供受限校正，平衡了直接预测的稳定性和动作条件建模的灵活性。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU-CRD数据集上，DRIFT在MAP预测的8、24、48小时端点平均MAE优于TFT-action；在治疗序列改变的子集分析中，DRIFT在8和24小时表现更佳；鲁棒性实验中优势持续存在。

Conclusion: 混合直接-递归框架DRIFT能更有效地利用治疗干预信息进行ICU时序预测，整体精度提升适中但在治疗变化场景下更为稳健。

Abstract: Many time-series forecasts depend not only on prior observations but also on actions specified during the forecast period. In intensive care units (ICUs), future vital signs and laboratory values are influenced by treatments such as vasopressors. However, models that predict the full future sequence all at once make little use of these treatments, whereas autoregressive models can accumulate errors. We introduce DRIFT, a hybrid framework in which a direct model produces the primary forecast and a recursive, action-conditioned model contributes constrained corrections. We evaluate DRIFT on 6,046 admissions from MIMIC-IV and 8,345 admissions from eICU-CRD. Averaged across the 8-, 24-, and 48-hour forecast endpoints, DRIFT reduces mean absolute error for mean arterial pressure (MAP) by 0.673% relative to an action-conditioned Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT-action) on MIMIC-IV and achieves the lowest corresponding error among the compared models on eICU-CRD. Although the overall accuracy improvement is modest, a MIMIC-IV audit restricted to windows in which the supplied treatment sequence was altered showed that DRIFT achieved lower observed-target MAP error than TFT-action at 8 and 24 hours. Treatment-sequence alteration increased DRIFT's MAP error by 0.21-0.26 mmHg more than it increased TFT-action's error, with prediction changes occurring primarily after the supplied paths diverged. In a separate robustness experiment, the MAP advantage persisted under three shared checkpoint-selection rules emphasizing overall endpoint error, MAP error, or both equally.

</details>


### [79] [A2TTA: Anchored-and-Agile Test-Time Adaptation for Evolving Traffic Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25875)
*Du Yin,Xiachong Lin,Yue Tan,Jinliang Deng,Estrid He,Hao Xue,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A2TTA框架，将拓扑演化引起的预测误差转化为可扩展的输出校准问题，并分离时域适应为持久全局校正和敏捷上下文特化，以解决动态演化交通网络中测试时适应的两大挑战：拓扑扩展和多尺度时间偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测研究通常假设传感器图固定不变，忽略了真实交通网络的持续演化（如道路建设、人类移动模式变化），导致传统预测模型性能显著下降。亟需高效的测试时适应方法来应对动态变化。

Method: 提出Anchored-and-Agile TTA框架：(1) 将拓扑演化引起的预测误差转化为可扩展的输出校准问题；(2) 将时域适应分离为持久全局校正（长期偏移）和敏捷上下文特化（短期偏移）两部分。

Result: 在10个真实交通网络数据集上的大量实验表明，A2TTA在不同骨干网络、数据集和预测范围上一致提升了预测性能。

Conclusion: A2TTA通过联合处理拓扑演化和多尺度时间偏移，实现了对持续演化交通环境的高效鲁棒适应，代码已开源。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is important for efficient traffic management and route planning in smart cities. Existing traffic forecasting studies typically assume fixed sensor graphs, overlooking the continuous evolution of real-world traffic networks, e.g., ongoing road network construction and evolving human mobility patterns. These dynamic changes can substantially degrade conventional forecasting models, motivating test-time adaptation (TTA) to efficiently adapt pretrained models during deployment. However, applying TTA to evolving traffic sensor networks remains challenging in two aspects. First, topology expansion introduces new sensors and connections, continuously reshaping the sensor graph. Second, tem- poral shifts vary in time scale and stability, requiring differentiated adaptation to long-term and short-term shifts. In this study, we address these challenges by proposing A2TTA, an Anchored-and-Agile Test-Time Adaptation framework for evolving traffic sensor networks, which transforms topology-induced forecasting errors into an expandable output calibration problem and separates tem- poral adaptation into persistent global correction and agile context-specific specialization. By jointly addressing topology evolution and multi-scale temporal shifts, A2TTA enables efficient and robust adaptation to continuously evolving traffic environments. Extensive experiments on ten real-world traffic networks demonstrate that A2TTA consistently improves forecasting performance across different backbones, datasets, and prediction horizons. Our code is available in https://github.com/lixus7/A2TTA.

</details>


### [80] [A Machine-Learning-Based Gas Lift Optimization Workflow for Unconventional Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25885)
*Sha,Miao,Alexandra Vendetti,Logan Smart,Gunta Chomchalerm,Yang Chen,Christopher Frazier,Dustin Haralson,Jeremy Sorenson,Xiao Ma,Huafei Sun,Aaron Shinn,Haining Zheng,Xiao-Hui Wu,Peng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的自动化数据驱动工作流，用于非常规气田的气举优化。该工作流集成了ML模型（准确预测气举性能曲线）和贝叶斯优化框架（在设施容量约束下求解最优注气速率），无需井下压力计或多速率测试。在Bakken油田30口井的试点中获得>5%平均增产，现已全面部署至200余口气举井，并为其他缺乏井下数据的资产提供了经济有效的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规气田的气举优化面临井下数据缺乏、多速率测试成本高或受设施条件限制的挑战，传统方法难以有效实施，亟需一种不依赖井下数据且经济高效的自动化优化方案。

Method: 采用机器学习模型利用历史生产时间序列数据预测气举性能曲线（Gas Lift Performance Curve），结合贝叶斯优化框架在设施容量约束下求解最优注气速率，整个流程为自动化数据驱动工作流。

Result: 在Bakken油田30口井（5个井组）的试点中获得平均>5%的产量提升，随后成功将工作流全面部署至200余口气举井和柱塞辅助气举井。

Conclusion: 该ML-based气举优化工作流是一种有效且经济的解决方案，尤其适用于因成本或设施限制而无法获取井下数据或多速率测试的资产，具有广泛推广价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an automated data-driven workflow using Machine Learning (ML) for gas lift optimization in unconventional fields. This workflow integrates a ML model that accurately forecasts the Gas Lift Performance Curve, and a Bayesian Optimization Framework to solve for the optimal gas injection rates under the constraints of facility capacity. The ML model leverages the historical production time series data without requiring downhole gauges or multi-rate well tests. We piloted this workflow on 30 wells across 5 well pads in Bakken and obtained >5% production uplift on average. With the success of the pilot, we have now fully-deployed this workflow in Bakken across 200+ gas lift and plunger-assisted gas lift (PAGL) wells. Moreover, the ML-based gas lift optimization workflow presented in this paper is an effective and economic solution for other assets where downhole data or multi-rate testing are not available/feasible due to cost or facility constraints.

</details>


### [81] [Minimizing Targeted Activations: Input-Only Suppression of Evaluation-Awareness Latents in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25907)
*Deepanshu Mody,Samarth Agarwal,Utkarsh Mittal,Dipesh Mahato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究激活引导的输入侧对偶方法：通过优化流畅提示词将内部潜在表征推向零，无需推理时模型访问。目标是一种"评估意识"潜在表征——当模型检测到被测试时行为可能改变，威胁安全评估有效性。实验在Llama-3.2-3B和Llama-3.1-8B上对五种目标构造（CAA方向、子空间范数、SAE特征、单MLP神经元、行为logit）进行抑制。发现：该潜在表征可被稳健抑制（z≈-7），且因果验证的Llama Scope SAE特征可被完全选择性关闭。但CAA方向存在安慰剂效应——随机方向可被同等程度抑制并改变行为；当在上下文中持有真实评估段落并仅优化前缀时，抑制评估方向未能降低反而略微提高了模型的评估判断。结论：激活可读性不等于行为可控性。单MLP神经元在两种规模上均与评估相关但不因果；扫描真实Pile数据为内部方向提供了与优化器竞争的自然文本基线。阳性对照验证了擦除检测器，界定了先前未解决的擦除与旋转问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究"评估意识"潜在表征的可控性——如果模型在检测到被测试时改变行为，将威胁AI安全评估的有效性。本文试图回答：能否通过输入侧的提示优化来抑制这种内部潜在表征？以及这种抑制是否能真正控制模型行为？

Method: 采用Fluent Dreaming/EPO框架，结合GCG风格的token优化和自交叉熵流畅性正则化器（对流畅性权重进行扫描），使用负特征项来抑制内部潜在表征。在Llama-3.2-3B和Llama-3.1-8B上对五种目标构造（CAA方向、子空间范数、SAE特征、单MLP神经元、行为logit）进行实验。同时引入随机方向作为安慰剂对照，并在真实评估段落上下文下测试前缀优化效果。

Result: 1) 潜在表征可被稳健抑制（z≈-7），因果验证的SAE特征可被完全选择性关闭；2) CAA方向存在严重的安慰剂效应——随机方向同样可被抑制并改变行为；3) 在真实评估上下文中，抑制评估方向反而略微提高了模型的评估判断；4) 单MLP神经元与评估相关但不具因果性；5) 自然Pile数据可提供与优化器竞争的基线。

Conclusion: 激活可读性不等于行为可控性。对CAA方向的控制存在安慰剂效应，且对内部潜在表征的抑制并不总能转化为期望的行为改变。研究还验证了擦除检测器的有效性，界定了擦除与旋转问题。提示：不当使用可能导致模型行为异常而非安全改善。

Abstract: Activation steering controls model behavior by editing internal activations at inference time. We study its input-side dual: optimizing a fluent prompt so that a chosen internal latent is driven toward zero, with no inference-time model access. Our target is an "evaluation-awareness" latent-linearly readable and steerable in recent work-whose control would threaten the validity of safety evaluations if models behave differently when they detect being tested. Adapting Fluent Dreaming / EPO with a negated feature term (GCG-style token optimization plus a self-cross-entropy fluency regularizer, swept over a fluency weight), we suppress the latent under five target constructions-a CAA direction, a subspace norm, an SAE feature, a single MLP neuron, and a behavioral logit-on Llama-3.2-3B and Llama-3.1-8B. The latent is robustly suppressible ($z\approx-7$), and a causally-validated Llama Scope SAE feature can be fully and selectively turned off. But our controls tell a cautionary story about the CAA direction: a placebo random direction is suppressed just as hard and shifts behavior just as far, and when we hold a real eval passage in context and optimize only a prefix, suppressing the eval-direction fails to reduce-and slightly increases-the model's behavioral eval judgment. Activation-readability, in short, is not behavioral controllability. We further find that a single MLP neuron is eval-correlated but not causal at both scales, and that scanning the real Pile yields a natural-text baseline competitive with the optimizer for the internal direction. A positive control validates our erasure detector, bounding an erasure-vs-rotation question earlier left open.

</details>


### [82] [Reinforcement Learning for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25970)
*Pierre Chambon,Kunhao Zheng,Juliette Decugis,Benoit Sagot,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套三阶段方法（DMC-Optim测试集、速度奖励设计、GRPO适配），解决了代码优化RL中因测量噪声、奖励稀疏和GRPO不稳定导致的训练失败问题，在Qwen 2.5 7B和CWM 32B上显著提升了优化感知的pass@1指标，并保持纯正确性得分不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将RL从代码正确性扩展到代码优化时，执行时间作为奖励信号会引入测量噪声、奖励稀疏和GRPO不稳定性，导致生成的代码几乎不更快且更多失败。需要系统性地解决这三个问题，使执行时间变得可学习。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1) DMC-Optim测试集：构建大规模优化测试和校准沙箱；(2) 速度奖励设计：在RL环境中组合正确性与速度，使用离线模拟器预测最优配置；(3) GRPO适配：针对更稀疏、噪声更大的定时执行场景调整GRPO和评估方式。

Result: 在DMC-Optim上，top-50% pass@1从18.0%提升至31.3%（Qwen 2.5 7B），从30.7%提升至50.4%（CWM 32B）；top-30%提升125%（CWM 32B）；纯正确性得分不变。沙箱退化时，鲁棒优化RL比标准RLVR提升100%-200%。LCB上CWM 32B在83%的中位数样本速度比较中胜出。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地解决代码优化RL中的测量噪声、奖励稀疏和训练不稳定问题，可以使模型在保持正确性的同时显著提升代码执行速度，达到人类复杂度改进速率的一半左右。

Abstract: RL for code correctness is now established: have the model generate a program, run it against hidden test cases, and reward solutions that pass. Extending this to code optimization seems straightforward: just add execution time to the reward. But in practice, once timing drives the reward, small problems in measurement noise, reward sparsity, or GRPO instability overwhelm the signal and make RL fail: generated solutions are barely faster, and more of them can fail. We make execution time learnable through three stages: (1) how code is tested, by building DMC-Optim with large optimization tests and a calibrated sandbox; (2) how speed is turned into reward, by composing correctness and speed in the RL environment and using an offline simulator to predict the most promising configurations; and (3) how the model learns from that reward, by adapting GRPO and evaluation to the sparser, noisier timed-execution setting. On DMC-Optim, the strongest optimization-aware configurations improve strict top-50% pass@1 from 18.0% to 31.3% on Qwen 2.5 7B and from 30.7% to 50.4% on CWM 32B. These gains further increase at stricter percentiles such as top-30%, with 125% relative improvement for CWM 32B, while preserving pure-correctness scores. When the timing sandbox is degraded, robust optimization RL reaches 100% to 200% improvement over standard RLVR, depending on the evaluation criterion. On LCB, CWM 32B wins up to 83% of median-sample speed comparisons against standard RLVR. Relative to the fastest correct human submissions per problem, it reaches about half the human rate of complexity-class improvements (14% vs. 28%).

</details>


### [83] [Generator-Aligned Representation Interfaces for Diagnostic Soft Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25988)
*Weitao Li,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成器对齐表示接口（GARI），一种将变换生成器暴露给通用序列骨干的表示级设计原则，通过对齐的规范视图与生成器诱导视图实现，并在基因组序列、图像和三维点云上验证了其任务相关的变换一致性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确等变架构通常将特定群作用编码到专门算子中，这限制了其在不同骨干网络和数据模态间的复用性。作者旨在设计一种通用的、与骨干网络无关的表示级接口，使变换生成器的结构可访问、可学习、可测量，同时无需针对群进行专门的重设计。

Method: GARI-Net：构建生成器索引的流，将其转换为公共交互帧，使用共享参数处理，修复排序引起的上下文失配，实现跨流信息交换，并利用流间差异进行聚合。同时提出直接等变误差（DEE）作为冻结检查点的诊断指标。

Result: 在基因组序列（序列反转）、图像（平面旋转与反射）和三维点云（受控轴向传递）上，同一接口原理支持任务相关的变换一致性和对声明保留探针的泛化，无需对序列骨干进行群特定的重新设计。

Conclusion: GARI为硬等变架构提供了一种可移植的诊断补充：它使生成器结构可访问、可学习、可测量，同时有限探针证据与连续群上精确等变的认证保持区别。

Abstract: Exact-equivariant architectures typically encode prescribed group actions in specialized operators, which can complicate their reuse with generic backbones and across data modalities. We introduce the Generator-Aligned Representation Interface (GARI), a representation-level design principle that exposes selected transformation generators to a generic sequence backbone through aligned canonical and generator-induced views. We formalize the resulting behavior using a probe-specific soft-equivariance residual defined over declared data and transformation distributions. This framework distinguishes representation consistency from task robustness and exact equivariance, and localizes residual mismatch to interface construction, shared stream processing, and terminal fusion. We instantiate the interface as GARI-Net, which constructs generator-indexed streams, converts them into a common interaction frame, processes them with shared parameters, repairs ordering-induced context mismatch, enables cross-stream information exchange, and aggregates them using inter-stream discrepancy. Direct Equivariance Error (DEE) provides a frozen-checkpoint diagnostic of the prescribed representation relation under known token or voxel actions. Experiments on genomic sequences, images, and three-dimensional point clouds examine sequence reversal, planar rotations and reflections, and controlled axial transfer. Across these settings, the same interface principle supports task-relevant transformation consistency and generalization to declared held-out probes without requiring group-specific redesign of the sequence backbone. GARI therefore provides a portable diagnostic complement to hard-equivariant architectures: it makes generator structure accessible, learnable, and measurable, while finite-probe evidence remains distinct from certification of exact equivariance over a continuous group.

</details>


### [84] [Empirical Evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution Performance of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26000)
*Malena Loza,David Chushig-Muzo,Eva Milara,Luis Bote-Curiel,Luis Estrada-Petrocelli,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了九种表格基础模型（TFMs）在分布偏移下的泛化能力，发现所有模型均存在系统性性能退化（偏移差距0.003-0.060），且高性能模型存在显著的计算资源可扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TFMs大多在独立同分布假设下训练和评估，但真实场景普遍存在分布偏移，而针对TFMs在分布偏移下鲁棒性的研究十分有限。

Method: 选取九种涵盖不同预训练策略和架构的TFMs（TabPFNv2/v2.5/v2.6/v3、TabICL/v2、Mitra、LimiX、TabFM），使用TableShift研究中的三个真实数据集（HELOC、Voting、Childhood Lead），覆盖标签偏移、社会经济偏移和地理偏移三种类型，系统评估其OOD性能。

Result: 所有TFMs在分布偏移下均出现系统性性能退化，偏移差距因偏移类型而异（0.003-0.060）；ID性能与OOD性能之间的关系延续了经典表格模型的规律；高性能模型的内存和计算资源需求超出标准部署基础设施的承载能力。

Conclusion: 该研究拓展了表格数据OOD评估基准，为在高风险领域采用TFMs应对结构性分布偏移提供了实证依据，同时揭示了可扩展性差距这一实际部署障碍。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as novel approaches for tabular predictive tasks, demonstrating competitive predictive performance to ensemble tree-based models. Most TFMs are trained and evaluated on independent and identically distributed data, but this assumption changes in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts, which compromise the robustness of models. Limited research has been conducted of TFMs under distribution shifts. We present an empirical evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) performance of nine TFMs, spanning diverse pre-training strategies and architectures: TabPFNv2, TabPFNv2.5, TabPFNv2.6, TabPFNv3, TabICL, TabICLv2, Mitra, LimiX and TabFM. Three real-world datasets from the TableShift study were considered (HELOC, Voting, Childhood Lead), covering label, socioeconomic, and geographic shift types. Our results show that all evaluated TFMs degrade systematically under distribution shift regardless of pre-training strategy, with shift gaps ranging from 0.003 to 0.060 depending on shift type. The relationship between in-distribution and OOD predictive performance documented for classical tabular models extends into TFMs. We also identified a scalability gap, as high-performing models demand significant memory and computational resources beyond what standard deployment infrastructure can support. This study extends existing benchmarks for OOD in tabular data, providing evidence to support their adoption in high-stakes domains characterized by structural distribution shifts.

</details>


### [85] [Re-thinking Mammography Transfer Learning: The Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) Framework for Breast Cancer Screening and Lesion Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26043)
*Adarsh Bhandary Panambur,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL)框架，通过集成数据集导出的难度信号与邻域三元组监督，在无需超参数调优的情况下，在乳腺X线摄影分类任务中显著超越现有方法，并在大规模临床数据集和小型ROI数据集上均取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺X线摄影分类任务中，传统迁移学习忽略数据集特定特征，而现有邻域方法受限于狭窄任务和刚性公式，难以扩展至大规模人群数据集。作者旨在提出一种兼具可扩展性、鲁棒性和无需超参数调优的统一框架。

Method: DITL框架包含两个自适应组件：(i) 自适应难度加权交叉熵(A-DWCE)，基于自监督特征空间中的k近邻标签纯度分配逐样本权重；(ii) 自适应邻域表示三元组损失(A-NR-Triplet)，使用可学习边距增强类内紧凑性和类间分离性。两者在统一目标中联合优化。

Result: 在VinDR-Mammo大规模数据集上，全图像乳腺密度分类任务取得SOTA性能，准确率、F1分数和AUC均有显著提升(p<0.0001)；在小规模ROI数据集上也取得一致且统计显著的性能增益(p<0.0001)。

Conclusion: DITL通过连接小规模病灶分析与大规模密度估计，建立了临床相关、可扩展且泛化性强的乳腺X线摄影分类框架，覆盖从乳腺癌筛查到诊断的完整谱系。

Abstract: Enhancing classification performance in mammography remains a persistent challenge across both small curated datasets and large-scale clinical cohorts. Conventional transfer learning approaches often neglect dataset-specific characteristics, while recent neighborhood-informed methods have been restricted to narrow tasks with rigid formulations, limiting their scalability to population-level datasets. To address these challenges, we propose the Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) framework, which integrates dataset-derived difficulty signals with neighborhood-based triplet supervision in a unified objective. DITL introduces two adaptive components: (i) Adaptive Difficulty-Weighted Cross-Entropy (A-DWCE), which assigns per-sample weights based on k-nearest neighbor label purity in a self-supervised feature space, and (ii) Adaptive Neighborhood Representation Triplet (A-NR-Triplet), which enforces intra-class compactness and inter-class separation using a learnable margin. Unlike focal loss, DITL requires no hyperparameter tuning, removes heuristic weighting and fixed margins, and incurs negligible computational overhead, yielding a robust and scalable optimization strategy. On the large-scale VinDR-Mammo dataset, DITL achieves state-of-the-art performance for whole-image breast density classification, with significant improvements across accuracy, F1-score, and AUC (p < 0.0001). Beyond large cohorts, DITL also delivers consistent, statistically significant gains on small ROI datasets (p < 0.0001). By bridging small-scale lesion analysis with large-scale density estimation, DITL establishes a clinically relevant, scalable, and generalizable framework for mammography classification, spanning the full breast cancer screening-to-diagnosis spectrum.

</details>


### [86] [Spend Experts Where You Are Unsure: Confidence-Adaptive Routing for Mixture-of-Experts LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26052)
*Tom Saliencro,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE是一种基于置信度自适应的专家路由方法，通过核采样方式动态激活专家数量，在匹配计算量的情况下优于固定top-k MoE-LoRA，并在OOD检测上也有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定数量的top-k专家路由对简单token过度分配资源，对困难token分配不足，因为不同token的模型不确定性不同。

Method: CARE利用路由器输出的分布作为每个token的不确定性信号，采用核采样方式激活专家：按路由器权重降序激活专家直到累积质量达到阈值，当已激活专家之间存在分歧时做少量扩展。通过预算恒温器校准阈值，使平均激活专家数匹配目标值。单次前向传播、无额外参数。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B的8个常识基准、数学、代码和知识任务上，CARE在匹配计算量时优于固定top-k MoE-LoRA，在匹配固定k=4基线时激活更少的专家。置信度和分歧信号还改进了OOD检测性能。

Conclusion: 核采样保真度、预算最优性以及对分歧的认知论解读支持了CARE的设计有效性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) variants of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) route every token to a fixed number of experts $k$. Tokens differ in how uncertain the model is about them, so a single k over-spends on easy tokens and under-serves hard ones. We observe that the router's output distribution is already a per-token uncertainty signal: peaked mass indicates confidence, while a flat distribution indicates ambiguity. We introduce CARE (Confidence-Adaptive Routing of Experts), which admits experts in a nucleus fashion. Experts are activated in decreasing router weight until their cumulative mass reaches a threshold, with a small extension when the admitted experts disagree. A budget thermostat calibrates the threshold so that the average number of active experts matches any target. CARE is a drop-in, single-forward-pass rule with no extra parameters. Across eight commonsense benchmarks on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, as well as math, code, and knowledge tasks, CARE improves over fixed top-k MoE-LoRA at matched compute and matches the fixed-k=4 baseline while activating fewer experts. The same confidence and disagreement signals also improve out-of-distribution detection over MSP, entropy, and multi-pass proxies. We support the design with nucleus fidelity, budget optimality, and an epistemic reading of disagreement, and we release code.

</details>

### [87] [Lloyd's $K$-Means Clustering Algorithm Is Frank-Wolfe in Disguise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25190)
*Michael Pokojovy,J. Marcus Jobe,Simon Lacoste-Julien*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了Lloyd's K-means算法与Frank-Wolfe算法之间的理论联系，证明Lloyd算法是FW算法的特例，并基于FW在凹目标上的最新进展推导出SSE目标局部最小值的非渐近O(1/t)收敛率。同时针对空簇问题，提出了半光滑目标的FW变体，并保持了相同的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Lloyd's K-means算法（一种广泛使用但缺乏严格理论保证的启发式优化方法）与Frank-Wolfe算法（一种成熟的投影自由一阶优化方法）之间的理论联系，从而为K-means算法提供更严谨的收敛性分析。

Method: 1) 证明Lloyd's K-means算法是Frank-Wolfe算法的一个特例；2) 利用FW算法在凹目标函数上的最新理论成果，推导出Lloyd算法在SSE目标下的非渐近收敛率O(1/t)；3) 针对Lloyd算法中可能出现的空簇问题（由贪心分配导致），提出一种适用于半光滑目标的FW变体，并保持相同的收敛率；4) 通过球面高斯混合模拟实验和真实图像分割数据集进行验证。

Result: 建立了Lloyd's K-means与Frank-Wolfe算法的等价关系，推导出O(1/t)的非渐近收敛率到SSE目标的局部最小值，该收敛率仅由初始SSE值控制。提出的FW变体能够处理空簇问题并保持相同的收敛率。

Conclusion: Lloyd's K-means算法可以被严格解释为Frank-Wolfe算法的特例，从而获得理论上的收敛保证。这一发现不仅为K-means提供了新的理论视角，也为进一步改进该经典算法开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Lloyd's $K$-means algorithm, also known as naïve $K$-means, is a widely used ad hoc optimization heuristic, designed to minimize the sum of squared errors (SSE) across all $K$-partitions of a dataset via iterative cluster refinement. In this work, we establish a novel connection between Lloyd's algorithm and the Frank-Wolfe (FW) algorithm, a prominent first-order method for projection-free optimization. We demonstrate that Lloyd's algorithm is a special case of FW. Leveraging recent advances in FW methods for concave objectives, we derive a non-asymptotic $\mathcal{O}(1/t)$ convergence rate to a local minimum of the SSE objective. To account for empty clusters, an outcome possible under Lloyd's greedy assignment, we develop an FW variant for semismooth objectives while retaining the same convergence rate that is solely controlled by the initial SSE value. We illustrate our findings with a simulation study for spherical Gaussian mixtures and a real-world image segmentation dataset.

</details>


### [88] [Learning from the Unseen: Offline Reinforcement Learning with Hidden Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25241)
*Zeyu Bian,Ying Zhou,Yifan Cui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了离线强化学习中动作未观测（仅存在带噪代理变量）的问题，提出基于影响函数的乘性稳健估计器LURE，利用下一状态变量作为未观测动作的自然代理，实现策略价值的识别与有效统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离线强化学习假设数据集中的动作是准确观测的，但实际应用中真实动作往往不可观测，仅有带噪代理变量可用，这导致现有RL方法产生有偏甚至误导性的结论。本文旨在解决这一隐藏动作场景下的离线策略评估问题。

Method: 在无限时域折扣MDP中，利用下一状态变量作为未观测动作的自然代理，建立策略价值的可识别性；提出基于影响函数的估计器LURE，具备乘性稳健性（多种正确设定组合下保持一致性）和渐近正态性。

Result: 通过仿真实验和基于MIMIC-III数据库的脓毒症管理应用验证了LURE的有效性。

Conclusion: LURE是首个针对离线强化学习中隐藏动作问题的解决方案，能提供有效的统计推断。

Abstract: Standard offline reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms typically assume that the actions in the dataset are observed without error. However, in many real-world applications, the true actions are unobserved and only noisy proxies are available, causing existing RL methods to yield biased and potentially misleading conclusions. We study off-policy evaluation in infinite-horizon discounted Markov decision processes with hidden actions. By leveraging the next-state variable as a natural proxy for the unobserved action, we establish identification of the policy value and propose an influence-function-based estimator called LURE (Learning from the Unseen: Robust Estimator). LURE is multiply robust, remaining consistent under several combinations of correctly specified nuisance components, and is asymptotically normal, enabling valid statistical inference. To our knowledge, this is the first work to address offline RL with hidden actions. We demonstrate LURE's effectiveness through simulations and a sepsis management application using the MIMIC-III database.

</details>


### [89] [Can Deep Generative Models Reproduce Non-Stationary Gaussian Random Fields?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25929)
*Daniel Kua,Yan Song*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种深度生成模型（FM、DDPM、score-SDE、VAE）在已知非平稳高斯随机场上的表现，发现所有模型均能恢复均值结构，但协方差恢复能力差异显著：DDPM和score-SDE表现较好，FM略有衰减，VAE困难。ERA5温度异常实验验证了该评估框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型在空间/时空建模中广泛应用，但因其生成样本无真实参照，难以评估模型是否真正学到了数据背后的真实生成过程。传统评估依赖观测数据本身，缺乏对模型恢复真实统计结构（如均值、协方差）能力的系统性验证方法。

Method: 使用已知非平稳高斯随机场作为基准，以oracle样本和平稳控制作为参考，设计综合指标评估模型恢复真实均值和协方差结构的能力。评估对象包括流匹配（FM）、DDPM、score-SDE和VAE四种代表性深度生成模型，并在ERA5温度异常数据上进行应用验证。

Result: 所有四种模型均能较好恢复均值曲面；协方差恢复方面，DDPM和score-SDE表现合理，FM呈现轻度非平稳性衰减和方差欠分散，VAE难以恢复协方差结构。ERA5实验进一步验证了该评估框架对复杂时空数据的适用性。

Conclusion: 该评估框架能够有效区分不同深度生成模型在时空建模中的统计性能差异，为开发适用于复杂真实世界时空数据的生成模型提供验证支持。

Abstract: Deep generative models (DGMs) are widely used for complex high-dimensional data and increasingly applied to spatial and spatio-temporal modeling. Their generated samples implicitly represent the learned data distribution and associated uncertainty. However, for real-world data, assessing whether DGMs have learned the underlying process is difficult because the ground truth is unknown and evaluation often relies on observations alone. We evaluate representative DGMs, flow matching (FM), DDPM, score-SDE, and VAE, on a known non-stationary Gaussian random field. This paper provides comprehensive metrics to assess recovery of the ground-truth mean and covariance structures, with oracle samples and a stationary control as references. All four models recover the mean surface, while their covariance recovery differs across model families: DDPM and score-SDE recover the covariance structure reasonably well, FM exhibits mildly attenuated non-stationarity and slight variance under-dispersion, and VAE has difficulty recovering the covariance structure. An experiment on ERA5 temperature anomalies further demonstrates how the framework can support the validation and development of DGMs for complex real-world spatio-temporal data.

</details>

### [90] [Parallelisation of Discrete Exterior Calculus via Representation Theory on Curved and Three-Dimensional Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25192)
*Leon D. da Silva,Marcelo P. Santos,José D. da Silva,Gilson Ferreira*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了离散外微积分（DEC）算子在对称网格上的通用块对角化框架，通过证明离散外导数d和Hodge星算子★在等距有限群作用下的等变性，使得任何由d和★组装的算子在单一对称适应基下具有块对角结构。数值实验在测地球面（I_h对称）、六边形环面（D_{6h}对称）和体心立方（BCC）网格上实现了高达182倍的FLOP并行加速比和约9.07倍的顺序加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEC算子在结构化网格上的计算成本高，传统谱方法局限于平坦柏拉图区域，缺乏适用于弯曲流形和三维对称网格的通用块对角化方法。

Method: 利用有限群作用在余链空间上诱导的置换表示，证明d和★在等距有限群作用下等变，从而在单一对称适应基下获得任何由二者组装的算子的块对角结构。

Result: 在I_h对称测地球面上FLOP并行加速比达62倍，D_{6h}对称六边形环面达182倍；BCC三维环面网格上d、★和Hodge Laplacian在机器精度下验证等变性，顺序加速比逼近理论渐近值约9.07倍。

Conclusion: 单一对称适应基能显著降低弯曲和三维网格上结构保持DEC计算的线性求解成本，该框架适用于计算电磁学和对称域上的几何流体模拟。

Abstract: We establish a universal block-diagonalization framework for Discrete Exterior Calculus (DEC) operators on symmetric meshes, enabling embarrassingly parallel solvers with provable FLOP reductions. We prove that the two fundamental DEC operators, the discrete exterior derivative $d$ and the Hodge star $\star$, are equivariant under isometric finite group actions on simplicial complexes. The proof exploits the permutation representation induced on cochain spaces by the group action. As a consequence, any operator assembled from $d$ and $\star$ (including the Hodge Laplacian, the codifferential, Maxwell-type operators, and elasticity operators) inherits a block-diagonal structure in a single symmetry-adapted basis, which is computed only once per mesh. Unlike spectral methods restricted to flat Platonic domains, the framework applies natively to curved manifolds and is applicable in principle to computational electromagnetism and geometric fluid simulation on symmetric domains. Numerical experiments on a geodesic sphere ($I_h$ symmetry) and a hexagonal torus ($D_{6h}$ symmetry) yield FLOP-based parallel speedups, relative to a dense direct factorization, of up to $62\times$ and $182\times$, respectively. A further experiment on a body-centred-cubic (BCC) tessellation of the flat 3-torus $T^3$ with $T_d$ symmetry confirms equivariance of the exterior derivative, Hodge star, and Hodge Laplacian at machine precision for form degrees $k=0,1,2$ across three mesh resolutions. The FLOP-based sequential speedup approaches its theoretical asymptote of $\approx 9.07\times$, which a standard Schur-multiplicity reduction deepens by a further factor of order $|G|$. These results show that a single symmetry-adapted basis reduces the linear-solve cost of structure-preserving DEC computations on curved and three-dimensional meshes.

</details>


### [91] [A Hybrid Physics-Informed Neural Network Framework for Subcritical and Supercritical Dynamics in Multi-Species Chemotaxis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24949)
*Hamid El Bahja,Jan C. Riedel,Peter Jung*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究两物种趋化系统的亚临界（解光滑）和超临界（有限时间爆破）两种动力学行为，提出混合PINN框架：亚临界使用标准连续时间PINN高效求解，超临界引入基于后向欧拉时间离散、对数变换和残差自适应配点的离散时间PINN，数值实验验证了该方法的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准连续时间PINN在超临界区域无法鲁棒地解析高度局部化结构和陡峭梯度（与爆破相关），需要开发更稳健的数值方法。

Method: 提出混合PINN框架：1）亚临界区域使用标准连续时间PINN（交替训练耦合场）；2）超临界区域引入离散时间PINN（后向欧拉时间步进），结合对数变换（稳定大解值）和残差自适应配点策略（在活跃区域集中训练点）。

Result: 数值实验表明：连续时间PINN准确解析亚临界动力学；离散时间PINN可靠捕获超临界区域的奇异聚集和有限时间爆破。

Conclusion: 混合框架兼顾了计算效率与鲁棒性：解光滑时使用简单经济的连续时间PINN，奇异动力学出现时切换到更稳健的离散格式。

Abstract: We study a two-species chemotaxis system that exhibits two qualitatively different regimes: subcritical dynamics, in which solutions remain smooth, and supercritical dynamics, in which strong aggregation may lead to finite-time blow-up. In the subcritical regime, we use a standard continuous-time physics-informed neural network (PINN) with alternating training for the coupled fields and show that it provides accurate and efficient approximations. In the supercritical regime, however, this formulation is not sufficiently robust to resolve the highly localized structures and steep gradients associated with blow-up. To address this limitation, we introduce a discrete-time PINN based on backward Euler time stepping, combined with a logarithmic transformation to stabilize large solution values and a residual-adaptive collocation strategy that concentrates training points near regions of high activity. This hybrid framework allows us to use a simple and computationally inexpensive PINN when the solution is smooth, while switching to a more robust discrete formulation when singular dynamics emerge. Numerical experiments confirm that the continuous-time formulation accurately resolves subcritical dynamics, while the discrete-time formulation reliably captures singular aggregation and finite-time blow-up in the supercritical regime.

</details>


### [92] [On residual bounds, backward shadowing stability of the Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition solution to the eigenvalue problem for the Koopman operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25086)
*Zlatko Drmač*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于阴影理论的向后稳定性评估方法，用于Koopman组合算子和扩展动态模态分解框架下的离散动力系统数据驱动计算分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据驱动场景下，噪声污染数据导致Koopman算子的谱分析存在误差，需要一种系统性的误差评估方法将计算误差解释为原始系统的伪轨迹，并确保其能被真实轨迹阴影覆盖。

Method: 1) 将近似特征对的残差聚合并构造Koopman算子的向后扰动；2) 将算子层面的扰动传递回原始系统的映射和初始条件；3) 证明计算近似对应系统的一个伪轨迹；4) 通过阴影理论证明该伪轨迹被系统的真实轨迹所阴影。

Result: 计算得到的特征对精确对应一个扰动后的非组合算子，该扰动可映射回原始系统产生伪轨迹，且该伪轨迹可被真实轨迹阴影。

Conclusion: 该方法为数据驱动的Koopman分析提供了向后误差分析的框架，特别适用于噪声污染的数据场景，并为数值阴影提供了新见解。

Abstract: This paper introduces shadowing theory based backward stability assessment of data driven computational analysis of discrete dynamical systems in the framework of the Koopman composition operator and the Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition. Data driven spectral analysis of the dynamics using the approximate (computed) eigenpairs of the Koopman operator is cast in terms of the backward error analysis. The individual residuals of the computed eigenpairs are aggregated in a backward perturbation of the Koopman operator, so that the computed eigenpairs are exact for a perturbed operator that is not a composition operator. Then, the impact of the perturbation in the operator is carried over to the map and the initial condition of the original system, showing that the computed approximations correspond exactly to a pseudo--trajectory of the system. In the final step, the pseudo--trajectory is shadowed by an exact trajectory of the system. This interpretation of errors is in particular suitable in data driven scenarios where the data is contaminated by noise. New insights into the numerical shadowing are provided.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场模型在模拟界面主导现象时，因需要在整个域上解析薄扩散界面，计算成本极高，限制了其在大规模问题中的应用。

Method: 将梯度平滑方法（GSM）与分层自适应移动结构化网格耦合，引入分层细化设计以保持界面区域的局部均匀分辨率，确保GSM离散化在网格非均匀条件下仍保持二阶精度。

Result: 基于Allen-Cahn和Cahn-Hilliard方程的数值实验表明，所提自适应GSM求解器在保持界面演化精度的同时，实现了O(N)近线性计算复杂度，相比现有弱形式和强形式求解器，在大规模薄界面问题中效率显著更高。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应GSM求解器通过大幅度减少总自由度数，将计算复杂度从均匀网格的二次标度降为近线性标度，为大规模相场模拟提供了一种高效且精确的强形式求解方案。

Abstract: Multiscale problems with evolving interfaces are ubiquitous in science and engineering. Phase-field models are a powerful tool for simulating interface-dominated phenomena in computational mechanics and materials modeling, but their application to large-scale problems is often constrained by the high computational cost of resolving thin diffuse interfaces over the entire domain. This paper presents an efficient strong-form phase-field solver that couples the Gradient Smoothing Method (GSM) with a hierarchical adaptive and moving structured mesh, enabling automatic localization of resolution within a narrow interfacial region while retaining coarse discretization in bulk domains. A layered refinement design is introduced to preserve locally uniform resolution across the interface, allowing the GSM discretization to maintain overall second-order accuracy despite strong mesh non-uniformity away from the interface. Although GSM incurs a higher per-degree-of-freedom cost than standard finite-difference schemes, the adaptive framework substantially reduces the total number of degrees of freedom, resulting in near-linear computational scaling compared with the quadratic scaling of uniform-grid approaches. Numerical examples based on the Allen-Cahn and Cahn-Hilliard equations demonstrate that the proposed adaptive GSM solver delivers desired accuracy for interface evolution while attaining more favorable computational complexity, O(N), than existing weak-form and strong-form solvers, becoming significantly more efficient for large-scale problems with thin interfaces or a small interfacial area fraction relative to the whole domain.

</details>

*Remi Abgrall,Yongle Liu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析Active Flux格式在双曲系统中的熵稳定性，确保数值解满足热力学第二定律的离散模拟，避免非物理解的出现。

Method: 构建整体格式：对平均值施加熵不等式（基于Tadmor框架的半离散格式），结合有界保持技术（BP_Pampa_VEM）和非振荡技术（PampaDG）；点值自由度无需处理熵条件。

Result: KPP测试表明熵修正是有效的：即使不激活有界保持和非振荡条件，也能得到正确解（仅含一些寄生波动）。格式在三角形网格上实现，可推广至多边形网格。

Conclusion: Active Flux格式的熵稳定性只需处理平均值自由度，点值自由度不影响熵条件；该方法可扩展至多边形网格，为高阶格式的熵稳定设计提供了简化路径。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the analysis of the entropy stability properties of Active Flux\yolo{-type} scheme for a hyperbolic system equipped with one entropy inequality. This type of scheme evolves two sets of degrees of freedom: point values that are chosen on the boundary of the elements that cover the computational domain, and the average of the solution in these elements. We show that the only thing to do is to get an entropy inequality for the average values, the point values degrees of freedom do not play any role. We construct a monolithic scheme which is bound preserving of \cite{BP_Pampa_VEM}, non oscillatory following \cite{PampaDG}, and entropy diminishing. The entropy condition is implemented in Tadmor's framework\cite{TadmorEntropy}, i.e. for the semi-discrete scheme only. The scheme is tested on the Kurganov-Popov-Petrova test case \cite{KPP} which is known to \yolo{be} sensitive to the satisfaction of an entropy inequality. We show that our entropy correction is effective: if we do not activate the bound-preserving nor the non oscillatory condition, we get the correct solution with some spurious wiggles, as expected. Though the development, implementation and tests are done with the triangle version of the scheme, the same method can be used for polygonal meshes, following \cite{BP_Pampa_VEM}.

</details>

*Zachary Grey*

Main category: math.NA

<details>

Method: 通过并行传输将主动子空间从欧氏空间推广到黎曼流形，形成内蕴形式的主动子空间。对比了基于嵌入的外蕴流形学习梯度平均方法，并在局部（均值中心的测地线球）范围内分析了两种方法的差异与一致性。对于扩展活动范围，提出了重新计算分解或并行传输单一中心标架两种策略。

Result: 在局部范围内，两种方法在中心切空间上的特征值达到二阶（相对于测地线半径）一致，主导特征空间达到与谱间隙相关的同阶一致。在2-球面上的数值实验验证了形式化方法，并展示了曲率限制下的二次速率脊线恢复。

Conclusion: 提出的内蕴主动子空间方法有效推广了欧氏空间中的原理到黎曼流形，特别适用于超球面（如预形状空间）上的统计形状分析应用。

Abstract: Active subspaces provide an explainable, eigenvalue-ordered principle for studying how scalar-valued quantities of interest change the most, on average, over a reduced basis of Euclidean domains. Composition with parallel transport generalizes this principle from Euclidean space to quantities of interest defined over Riemannian manifolds, and the resulting intrinsic formulation is contrasted with the extrinsic, embedding-based gradient average of manifold learning. Either strategy is studied in an intrinsically local sense, restricted to mean-centered geodesic-balls, and within that scope the two are not identical: on the central tangent space, eigenvalues agree to second order in the geodesic radius of the sampled domain, while dominant eigenspaces agree at the same order relative to the spectral gap. Extending activity beyond that central space then calls for either recomputed decompositions over changing tangent spaces or, intrinsically, parallel transport of a single central frame. Hyperspheres are emphasized throughout as a particular manifold of interest, motivated by applications over preshape spaces for statistical shape analysis. Numerical examples over the 2-sphere illustrate the formalism, including the derived ridge recovery at a curvature-limited quadratic rate.

</details>


### [96] [A Convex Splitting Spectral Method for the Phase Field Crystal Equation: Energy Stability, Computational Stability Maps, and Three-Dimensional GPU Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25177)
*Saulo Orizaga,Peimeng Yin,Deep Choudhuri*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于凸分裂框架的傅里叶谱方法用于相场晶体(PFC)方程。该一阶格式无条件能量稳定且守恒质量至机器精度。通过截断势论证建立能量稳定性定理，并推导半解析中性稳定曲线。经典条件a≥2被证明过于保守——基于4万次GPU加速PFC模拟的计算稳定图谱显示，能量稳定解在远低于该阈值的a值下仍可维持。精度分析表明稳定区内较小的a值始终产生更低的L²误差。通过长达10,000时间单位、256³网格的三维渐近应力测试进行了严格验证，在远低于经典凸分裂极限(a<2)的松弛稳定区内成功执行了10⁶个连续时间步长，同时保持严格的单调能量耗散和机器精度质量守恒。最后，在单块消费级GPU上完成了高达512³分辨率的二维和三维模拟，展示了无需HPC基础设施即可解析复杂相场动力学的可扩展性。代码已在GitHub上开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典凸分裂方法要求a≥2的充分条件过于保守，限制了计算效率。本文旨在突破该保守条件，探索在更宽松的稳定性边界下高效、高精度求解PFC方程的可能性，并降低计算资源需求。

Method: 采用基于凸分裂框架的傅里叶谱方法，构建一阶无条件能量稳定格式；使用截断势论证证明能量稳定性；通过主模态能量平衡推导半解析中性稳定曲线；利用GPU加速进行40,000次PFC模拟构建计算稳定图谱；在消费级GPU上实现高达512³分辨率的大规模模拟。

Result: 经典条件a≥2被证明过于保守，能量稳定解在a<2的松弛区域内仍可维持；稳定区内较小的a值始终产生更低的L²误差；成功在256³网格上执行10⁶步连续时间推进至T_f=10,000，保持严格的能量单调耗散和质量守恒；在单块消费级GPU上完成512³分辨率的模拟。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法突破了经典凸分裂的保守限制，在更宽泛的参数范围内实现了高效、高精度、能量稳定的PFC方程求解，且消费级GPU即可支撑大规模模拟，显著降低了计算门槛。开源代码便于社区复现和应用。

Abstract: We present an efficient Fourier spectral method based on the convex splitting framework of Eyre~\cite{eyre1998unconditionally} for the phase field crystal (PFC) equation. The proposed first-order scheme is unconditionally energy stable and conserves mass to machine precision. An energy stability theorem is established using a truncated potential argument, and a semi-analytical neutral stability curve is derived from a dominant-mode energy balance, providing a closed-form characterization of the practical stability boundary. The classical sufficient condition $a \geq 2$ is shown to be conservative: a computational stability map obtained from 40,000 GPU-accelerated PFC simulations reveals that energy-stable solutions persist for values of $a$ significantly below this threshold. Crucially, accuracy analysis demonstrates that smaller values of $a$ within the stable region consistently yield lower $L^2$ errors. This high-fidelity regime is rigorously verified through an extended asymptotic stress test consisting of a long-time three-dimensional simulation on a $256^3$ grid up to $T_f = 10,000$, successfully executing $10^6$ continuous temporal increments deep within the relaxed stability regime, well below the classical convex splitting limit ($a < 2$), while preserving strict monotonic energy dissipation and machine-precision mass conservation. Finally, two-dimensional and three-dimensional simulations at resolutions up to $512^3$ are performed on a single consumer GPU, demonstrating the scalability of the proposed framework for resolving complex phase-field dynamics without requiring HPC infrastructure. The code is made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模线性统计模型中，普通最小二乘估计的计算成本过高，现有迭代方法收敛速度慢或计算量大，亟需一种能显著降低计算开销同时保持预测精度的高效算法。

Method: SPCG方法：1）构造一系列草图大小递增的草图最小二乘子问题；2）以PCG作为内层求解器；3）每个子问题从前一个解热启动；4）最后在全尺度问题上进行精化阶段。大部分迭代在较小的子问题上执行，从而降低整体计算成本。

Result: 理论证明了SPCG收敛性并达到OLS预测精度，推导了每个子问题的迭代界限和复杂度估计。数值实验表明SPCG在达到目标预测精度所需的迭代次数和CPU时间上均优于全数据PCG和Iterative Double Sketching (IDS)。

Conclusion: SPCG是一种高效的大规模最小二乘求解方法，通过多尺度草图子问题和热启动策略在保证预测精度的前提下显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: We propose a randomized iterative method for the ordinary least-squares estimation problem in large-scale linear statistical models, namely the Sequential Preconditioned Conjugate Gradient Method (SPCG). SPCG constructs a sequence of sketched least-squares subproblems with increasing sketch sizes, applies PCG as the inner solver, and warm-starts each subproblem from the previous solution. A final refinement stage is then performed on the full-scale problem. Since most iterations are carried out on smaller subproblems, the overall computational cost is significantly reduced. We establish the convergence theory, prove that SPCG attains OLS prediction accuracy, and derive per-subproblem iteration bounds and complexity estimates. Numerical experiments show that SPCG reaches the target prediction accuracy with fewer iterations and less CPU time than full-data PCG and Iterative Double Sketching (IDS).

</details>

*Xinye Chen*

Conclusion: 该方法和工具为开发数值稳定的计算内核提供了宝贵见解，对于实现数值稳定且高效的深度学习训练和推理至关重要。

Abstract: Finite-precision arithmetic unavoidably introduces numerical approximation errors. Numerical computations may use insufficient precision or an improper formulation, which leads to numerical instability. In this paper, we introduce the first unified software tool that integrates CESTAC for detecting the numerical stability of deep learning operators. Our developed software not only enables numerical validation with a single computation pass but also detects the sources of numerical instability and provides numerical stability monitoring during deep learning training and inference. We verified its effectiveness on the detection of polluted operators with injected numerical instabilities across various tasks. We believe that our developed method and tool provide valuable insights into developing numerically stable computing kernels, which are particularly critical for numerically stable and efficient deep learning training and inference.

</details>

Abstract: The ferromagnetic magnetohydrodynamic equations are a non-linear PDE system that models the flow of an electrically conducting fluid that also has intrinsic magnetisation. *Simon Candelaresi,Benedict Geihe,Marco Artiano,Lars Christmann,Valentin Churavy,Andrés Rueda-Ramírez,Hendrik Ranocha,Gregor J. Gassner,Michael Schlottke-Lakemper*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Julia语言在串行应用中表现良好，但其是否适用于传统的大规模并行HPC工作负载尚不明确，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 通过对比分析Julia编写的数值计算流体动力学模拟代码Trixi.jl与Fortran编写的FLUXO代码的并行性能，在61440 CPU核心上进行扩展测试，并分析大规模使用Julia时的挑战。

Result: Julia代码（Trixi.jl）在多达61440个CPU核心上展示了并行扩展能力，但存在启动时代码加载与编译的挑战。

Abstract: The Julia programming language aims to provide a modern approach to develop high-performance computing (HPC) applications. It tries to achieve this by combining a high-level, dynamic interface with just-in-time compilation to native machine code, thereby facilitating high developer productivity and native code performance at the same time. While this approach has already been shown to work well for serial applications, it is not clear if it readily translates to traditional, massively parallel HPC work loads. In this paper, we fill this gap by analyzing the parallel performance of the numerical computational fluid dynamics simulation code Trixi$.$jl, written in Julia, and compare it to the Fortran code FLUXO. We show some of the challenges of using Julia at scale and discuss possible solutions, specifically with respect to code loading and compilation at startup. Finally, we demonstrate the parallel scaling of our Julia code on up to 61440 CPU cores.

</details>


### [101] [Multi-Solver Coupling for Parallel Adaptive Multi-Physics Simulations with Trixi$.$jl and deal$.$II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25871)
*Vivienne Ehlert,Gregor Gassner,Martin Kronbichler,Hendrik Ranocha,Michael Schlottke-Lakemper*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值求解器框架大多针对单一或多物理问题独立开发，耦合问题通常通过扩展现有求解器、开发全新求解器或借助耦合库接口连接两个求解器来解决，但目前缺乏一个允许轻松直接开发耦合求解器的框架。

Method: 采用Trixi.jl和deal.II构建跨语言可移植框架，原型化一个耦合求解器开发环境，通过分区强耦合多物理求解器实现牛顿自引力气体动力学模拟。

Result: 验证了耦合求解器的预期收敛阶、结果的物理合理性以及网格自适应性，并研究了其并行扩展性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为耦合并行自适应求解器的开发提供了可移植、可复现的跨语言方案，数值结果和代码已公开在可复现仓库中。

Abstract: Many standalone frameworks for numerical solvers have been developed to tackle the simulation of specific single- or multi-physics problems. For solving coupled problems, common approaches are to extend existing solvers, to develop an entirely new solver or to couple two existing solvers by using the interface provided by a coupling library or framework. However, to the best of our knowledge, there is not yet a framework that allows the easy and direct development of coupled solvers. In this work, we prototype a portable reproducible cross-language framework for the development of coupled parallel adaptive solvers using Trixi$.$jl and deal$.$II for the numerical simulation of coupled multi-physics problems. Currently, this is tightly entangled with an example coupled solver. We show its usability by developing a partitioned strongly-coupled multi-physics solver for the dynamics of Newtonian self-gravitational gases. For the coupled solver, we validate the expected order of convergence, physical sensibility of the results and mesh adaptivity. Finally, we investigate its parallel scaling. A publicly accessible reproducibility repository for the numerical results and code is available.

</details>
