# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Optimal, Data-Driven Wall Models for Efficient Large Eddy Simulations of Metastable von Kármán Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25048)
*Quentin Malé,Lucas Amoudruz,Daniel Bulgarini,Shuolin Xiao,Charles Meneveau,Fabrizio Bisetti,Petros Koumoutsakos*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的叶轮模型，通过结合实验PIV数据、扭矩测量和RANS方程的ODIL框架以及CMA-ES优化，实现了对von Kármán湍流旋流的长时间大涡模拟，成功复现了亚稳态状态切换动力学，并发现这些亚稳态由交替的四胞流型构成且缓慢绕轴旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: von Kármán湍流旋流存在大尺度亚稳态动力学（如低频状态切换），但高雷诺数下叶轮分辨的LES计算成本过高，难以捕捉这些缓慢动力学。因此需要开发一种计算高效的叶轮模型来模拟叶轮对流动的作用。

Method: 利用PIV和扭矩测量数据，通过B样条参数化叶轮区域速度场，结合RANS约束的ODIL框架推断初始B样条系数，再用CMA-ES优化系数以最小化LES时均速度/扭矩与实验值的差异，最终将优化后的模型用于长时间LES。

Result: 模拟成功复现了主体平均流，并显示出亚稳态状态切换动力学；这些亚稳态是非轴对称的，由交替的四胞流型构成并缓慢绕圆柱容器轴旋转。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动方法为研究叶轮驱动湍流中的大尺度动力学提供了一条实用且计算高效的途径。

Abstract: The von Kármán turbulent swirling flow exhibits intriguing large-scale metastable dynamics, including low-frequency state switching. The study of state switching demands long-duration high-fidelity simulations at high Reynolds numbers that capture the flow generated by the impellers. Blade-resolved Large Eddy Simulations (LES) are computationally prohibitive, limiting access to these slow dynamics. Here, we develop a model for the action of the impellers on the flow using experimental data from Particle Image Velocimetry (PIV) and torque measurements of the von Kármán flow. The impeller-region velocity is parametrized via B-splines and coupled to the LES through momentum forcing. An initial set of B-spline coefficients is inferred using the Optimizing a DIscrete Loss (ODIL) framework constrained by the Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) equations, PIV measurements in the optically accessible portion of the device, and impeller torque measurements. The coefficients are then refined by the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES), which minimizes the discrepancy between the LES time-averaged velocity and torque and their experimental counterparts. Using the data-driven impeller model, we perform long-duration LES of the von Kármán flow. We find that the simulation reproduces the mean flow in the bulk and displays metastable state-switching dynamics. We further show that these metastable states are not axisymmetric and consist of an alternating four-cell flow pattern that slowly rotates around the axis of the cylindrical vessel. The proposed approach provides a practical and computationally efficient route to investigating large-scale dynamics in impeller-driven turbulent flows.

</details>


### [103] [Vortex dynamics and air entrainment in dam break wave impacting on vertical walls: A multiphase lattice Boltzmann study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25433)
*Massimiliano Mustè,Andrea Montessori,Pietro Prestininzi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文采用基于格子玻尔兹曼方法（LBM）耦合保守型Allen-Cahn界面捕捉方程的两相数值模型，对干床溃坝波传播及撞击垂直壁面进行了高分辨率直接数值模拟。研究发现，精确解析气-水和固-水界面对局部和整体动力学及工程关键量（如压力峰值大小和位置）有显著影响，压力峰值受波前偏转和小角涡引起的局部掺气强烈影响。此外，无滑移与自由滑移条件的对比表明，反射射流回落时捕获的大空腔形成可能源于建模假设而非固有流动物理，突出了近壁剪切在射流破碎动力学中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气卷吸在自由表面波流与结构相互作用中决定冲击载荷，但现有数值方法对其建模往往过于简化，缺乏高分辨率精确解析。

Method: 采用基于格子玻尔兹曼方法（LBM）耦合保守型Allen-Cahn界面捕捉方程的两相数值模型，对干床溃坝波传播及垂直壁面撞击进行高分辨率直接数值模拟。

Result: 压力峰值的大小和位置受波前偏转及小角涡引起的局部掺气强烈影响；无滑移与自由滑移条件对比表明，反射射流中捕获的大空腔形成可能源于建模假设而非固有流动物理。

Conclusion: 精确解析气-水和固-水界面对冲击动力学至关重要，近壁剪切在射流破碎和空腔形成中起关键作用，建模假设可能误导对物理机制的理解。

Abstract: Air entrainment often plays a crucial role in determining impact loads exerted by free-surface wave flows interacting with structures, yet its modelling is often oversimplified in numerical approaches.
  In this study a two-phase numerical model, based on the Lattice Boltzmann Method coupled to a conservative Allen--Cahn interface-capturing equation is employed to perform direct numerical simulations of dam-break waves propagating over a dry bed and impacting on vertical walls.
  Access to high-resolution simulations enables a detailed assessment of how accurately resolving both air--water and solid--water interfaces affects local and overall dynamics, as well as quantities of extreme engineering interest. Indeed, the magnitudes and locations of the pressure peaks are strongly affected by wave front deflection and local aeration induced by a small corner vortex.
  Additionally, comparisons between no-slip and free-slip implementations suggest that the large air cavity formation, commonly observed as trapped inside the reflected jet falling back onto the incoming flow, may be the result of modeling assumptions rather than intrinsic flow physics, again highlighting the key role of near-wall shear in jet breakup dynamics.

</details>


### [104] [Numerical simulations and universal saturation profiles for viscous fingering patterns in Hele-Shaw flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25154)
*Irio M. Coutinho,Liam C. Morrow,Scott W. McCue*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过全非线性数值模拟，系统检验了Beeson-Jones-Woods（2019）提出的Hele-Shaw流动中粘性指进的饱和度模型，发现该模型在单相流且表面张力足够小时能有效预测近通用模式，但在两相流及大表面张力参数下存在偏差，偏差原因与指进形态（如基部收缩和尖端分叉率）有关。研究表明该模型适用于DLA-like模式，但在两相流中不具备普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统检验Beeson-Jones-Woods饱和度模型在更广泛参数范围内的有效性，并探究其在单相流与两相流中的适用性差异及其物理机制。

Method: 采用水平集方法进行全非线性数值模拟，在广泛参数范围内计算粘性指进模式，并将模拟结果与经验饱和度模型进行对比分析，同时通过指进形态学（基部收缩、尖端分叉率）解释偏差来源。

Result: 1) 在单相流且表面张力足够小的情况下，饱和度模型能有效预测粘性指进模式的近通用特性；2) 大表面张力参数下模型存在偏差；3) 在两相流中模型预测与数值观测存在系统性差异；4) 偏差程度与指进形态特征（基部收缩、尖端分叉率）密切相关。

Conclusion: Beeson-Jones-Woods饱和度模型是DLA（扩散限制聚集）类模式的有效描述，但在两相流中不具备普适性，因为表面张力和粘度比会显著改变指进形态及相应的饱和度分布。

Abstract: Hele-Shaw flows with an interface are known to give rise to complex pattern formation, whereby the Saffman-Taylor instability triggers a viscous fingering process accompanied by tip splitting and branching. The most popular of these experiments involves a radial configuration with a less viscous fluid injected into a more viscous fluid. In an attempt to characterize the resulting complexity in such an experiment, Beeson-Jones and Woods (2019) have proposed a type of simple empirical model that aims to predict the saturation profile of the invading fingers as a function of a radial coordinate. We revisit the proposed saturation model and test its validity over a broad parameter range using fully nonlinear numerical simulations computed with a level set method. We find that the saturation model is very effective at predicting some near-universal properties of the viscous fingering patterns for one-phase flows, where the invading fluid is neglected, with a sufficiently small surface tension parameter. For larger values of this parameter and for two-phase flows, there are discrepancies between the model and our observations. We explain these differences by studying the morphology of the advancing fingers, including pinching at the base and the rate of tip splitting. Overall, our study shows that the Beeson-Jones-Woods saturation model serves as a valid description of DLA-like patterns, but is not universal over two-phase flows, where surface tension and viscosity ratio substantially alter finger morphology and the resulting saturation profile.

</details>


### [105] [On the scaling of bubble interactions in dynamic turbulence: theoretical, numerical, and experimental study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25251)
*Vivek Kumar,Prasoon Suchandra,Shivam Prajapati,Suhas S. Jain,Cyrus K. Aidun*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过理论、直接数值模拟(DNS)和实验，研究了高雷诺数下稀释气泡衰减均匀各向同性湍流。发现湍动能和耗散率遵循幂律衰减，气泡群随Hinze尺度演化而重组。当耗散衰减足够快时，Hinze尺度增长快于特征气泡直径，驱动气泡群从super-Hinze向sub-Hinze转变。系统经历混合区（聚并主导但破碎仍活跃）和纯聚并区。理论、DNS和实验（泵驱动气泡管道流）预测了气泡直径、数密度、界面面积和聚并率在不同阶段的增长规律，表明Hinze尺度漂移是气泡相互作用的关键组织机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高雷诺数下稀释气泡湍流的衰减动力学，特别是气泡群如何随Hinze尺度演化而重组，以及这一过程对气泡尺寸分布、聚并和破碎的影响机制。

Method: 结合理论分析、DNS和实验三种手段。理论推导衰减规律；DNS模拟稀释气泡负载湍流；实验在空间发展的泵驱动气泡管道流（更高Re）中验证。

Result: 1) 湍动能和耗散率服从幂律衰减；2) 气泡群经历从super-Hinze到sub-Hinze的转变，依次经过混合区（聚并+破碎）和纯聚并区；3) 混合区中残余破碎增加小气泡数量并增强聚并；4) DNS显示衰减指数接近单相湍流，气泡尺寸分布向低于Hinze尺度偏移；5) 分布呈现双幂律（毛细效应+惯性破碎）到单幂律（毛细主导）的转变；6) 理论与DNS预测了不同区间的增长律。

Conclusion: Hinze尺度漂移是气泡相互作用在理想和实际衰减湍流中的组织机制，为群体平衡模型和界面面积输运模型提供了指导。

Abstract: This study investigates dilute bubbly decaying homogeneous isotropic turbulence at high Reynolds number using theory, direct numerical simulation, and experiments. The turbulent kinetic energy and dissipation rate follow power-law decay, while the bubble population reorganizes relative to the evolving Hinze scale. When the dissipation decays sufficiently rapidly, the Hinze scale grows faster than the characteristic bubble diameter, driving the population from super-Hinze toward sub-Hinze sizes. The system passes through a mixed regime in which coalescence dominates but breakup remains active, followed by a pure-coalescence regime. Residual breakup in the mixed regime increases the number of small bubbles and enhances coalescence, leading to faster growth of the characteristic bubble size. DNS of dilute bubble-laden turbulence shows decay exponents close to single-phase turbulence and a bubble-size distribution that shifts toward smaller diameter relative to the Hinze scale. Before the transition, the distribution exhibits two power-law ranges associated with capillary effects and inertial breakup; after the transition, it approaches a single capillary-dominated scaling. Theory and DNS predict distinct growth laws for bubble diameter in the mixed and pure-coalescence regimes, together with corresponding scalings for number density, interfacial area, and coalescence rate. These predictions are further assessed in a spatially developing pump-driven bubbly duct flow at higher Reynolds number. Despite confinement, inhomogeneity, and wall production, the measured trends agree with the theoretical and DNS-based scalings. The results identify Hinze-scale drift as the organizing mechanism for bubble interactions in both idealized and practical decaying turbulent flows, and provide guidance for population-balance and interfacial-area-transport models.

</details>


### [106] [Accurate wetting dynamics via a conservative Allen-Cahn based lattice Boltzmann approach for multiphase flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25444)
*Paolo Bello,Luca Mander,Marco Lauricella,Gianmarco Guglielmo,Michele La Rocca,Andrea Montessori*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于保守Allen-Cahn晶格玻尔兹曼框架的局部几何润湿边界条件，通过从局部重构的壁面法向和供体流体外推构建的鬼影相场值来施加指定接触角，实现了局部、几何一致且兼容线程安全大规模实现的润湿更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有润湿边界条件在复杂几何中的大规模并行实现存在困难，需要一种局部、几何一致且可扩展的润湿边界处理方法。

Method: 基于保守Allen-Cahn方程的晶格玻尔兹曼方法，采用局部重构壁面法向和供体外推构建鬼影节点相场值来施加接触角，并辅以全局体积校正控制相场质量。

Result: 静态接触角测试验证了平衡角的准确恢复；短时液滴铺展复现了1/2至1/4之间的接触角依赖铺展指数；最大变形遵循We^1/4经典标度律；成功捕捉了捕获、释放及伴随破碎的释放等流态转变。

Conclusion: 该方法为复杂几何中的润湿控制流动提供了精确且可扩展的数值框架。

Abstract: In this work we propose a local geometric wetting boundary condition for a conservative Allen--Cahn-based lattice Boltzmann framework. The prescribed contact angle is imposed through ghost phase-field values constructed from a locally reconstructed wall normal and a donor-fluid extrapolation. The ghost-node wetting update is local, geometrically consistent, and compatible with thread-safe large-scale implementations, while phase-field mass is controlled through a separate global volume correction. Validation includes static contact-angle tests, short-time droplet spreading, impact on a hydrophobic surface, and gravity-driven motion through a sharp-edged orifice. The simulations recover the imposed equilibrium angles, reproduce contact-angle-dependent spreading exponents between approximately 1/2 and 1/4, and follow the classical W e1/4 maximum-deformation scaling. The model also captures the transition between capture, release, and release with breakup. The proposed approach provides an accurate and scalable framework for wetting-controlled flows in complex geometries.

</details>


### [107] [A universal diffuse interface modeling framework for surfactants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25453)
*Shahab Mirjalili,Mathieu Bignolles*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于所有溶解度场景和任意守恒相场方法的通用扩散界面建模框架，用于两相流中表面活性剂输运。该框架基于三标量非平衡模型，可简化为两种单标量模型，并经过一维、二维及全耦合剪切流模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对保守型Allen-Cahn方程的扩散界面表面活性剂模型仅处理不溶和单相可溶情形，未解决两相中部分溶解的一般场景；且缺乏伽利略不变性和降阶一致性，不适用于CAC之外的相场方法。

Method: 提出通用三标量非平衡模型，假设热化学平衡后推导出两种单标量简化模型（全平衡和单相可溶），通过表面张力（含Marangoni应力）与Navier-Stokes方程耦合，适用于任意保守相场方法。

Result: 在一维输运测试中验证了与解析解的一致性，在二维对流-扩散模拟中评估了收敛性，并在液滴剪切流模拟中展示了不溶、可溶和部分可溶三种表面活性剂场景。

Conclusion: 该框架具有局部和全局守恒、无泄漏、伽利略不变和降阶一致的特性，填补了现有模型在部分溶解性和泛用性方面的空白。

Abstract: We propose a universal diffuse-interface modeling framework for surfactant transport in two-phase flows, applicable to all solubility scenarios and to any conservative phase field method. The foundation of our framework is a general three-scalar non-equilibrium model governing the surfactant concentrations in each bulk phase and at the interface, which builds on our prior consistent scalar transport framework and is locally and globally conservative, leakage-free, Galilean-invariant, and reduction-consistent. Assuming thermochemical equilibrium, we derive two one-scalar models: one for a surfactant in full equilibrium between both bulk phases and the interface, and one for a surfactant confined to a single bulk phase and the interface. All models are coupled to the Navier-Stokes equations through a surface tension force that incorporates the Marangoni stress arising from non-uniform interfacial surfactant distributions. While diffuse-interface surfactant models have been developed for the Cahn-Hilliard equation and, more recently, for the conservative Allen-Cahn (CAC) equation, existing models for CAC address only the insoluble and single-phase-soluble cases, leaving the general scenario of partial solubility in both bulk phases unaddressed; furthermore, these models lack Galilean invariance and reduction consistency, and are not applicable beyond the CAC setting. The framework is validated against analytical solutions in one-dimensional transport tests, assessed for convergence in two-dimensional advection-diffusion simulations, and demonstrated in fully-coupled drop-in-shear flow simulations covering insoluble, soluble, and partially-soluble surfactant scenarios.

</details>


### [108] [Foundations of a solved-volatility stochastic turbulence closure: Itô--Hencky kinematics, source-consistent momentum and finite-correlation realisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25536)
*Hsieh-Chen Tsai*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的随机闭合框架，将位移-波动场与解析速度联立求解。基于单通道Itô构型映射和局部矩阵对数展开，推导出材料与空间Hencky增量、二次变差漂移及精确路径体积约束。在常密度、单布朗通道、路径等容且解析欧拉漂移无独立鞅条件下，证明材料拉回动量方程为完整随机雷诺输运平衡的在壳形式，得到速度-波动-压力指标1微分代数系统。虚功率确定应力冲量的力学类型并分离功与二次协变差，有限相关前驱给出格林-库伯实现的位移协方差。状态依赖引入Lyapunov噪声诱导漂移，动态边界引入反应功与主动/被动协方差兼容条件。分析得到四个极限：非零布朗输运极限无法保留有限普通未解析动能；总能量不能单独确定熵产；壁面切向限制协方差秩而非随机模式数；齐次解耦亥姆霍兹-斯托克斯方程仅有平凡周期解。最终形成理论完备、可检验的闭合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机闭合方法通常预设协方差张量、噪声基或涡粘场，缺乏将位移-波动场与解析速度联立求解的框架。本文旨在发展一种新的理论闭合架构，从第一性原理推导速度-波动-压力耦合系统，以克服传统方法的经验性局限。

Method: 基于单通道Itô构型映射，通过局部矩阵对数展开推导材料/空间Hencky增量及其二次变差漂移，施加路径体积约束。在特定假设下证明材料拉回动量方程是随机雷诺输运平衡的在壳形式。引入虚功率原理确定应力冲量力学类型，利用有限相关前驱实现位移协方差的格林-库伯表示。分析状态依赖和动态边界效应，推导四个极限情形。

Result: 得到速度-波动-压力指标1微分代数系统；虚功率分离功与二次协变差；格林-库伯关系实现位移协方差；Lyapunov漂移和边界反应功与协方差兼容条件；四个极限结论（布朗输运极限、熵产非唯一性、壁面协方差秩限制、亥姆霍兹-斯托克斯平凡解）。

Conclusion: 发展了一种理论自洽、可通过计算和实验检验的随机闭合架构，为湍流统计、对数壁标度和CFD验证提供了完整的理论框架，具体数值验证留待后续流体力学研究。

Abstract: Most stochastic closures prescribe a covariance tensor, a noise basis or an eddy-viscosity field. This paper develops a different framework in which the displacement-volatility field is solved together with the resolved velocity. The starting point is a one-channel Itô configuration map. A local matrix-logarithm expansion gives distinct material and spatial Hencky increments, their quadratic-variation drifts and the exact pathwise volume constraint. Under constant density, one Brownian channel, pathwise isochoricity and no independent martingale in the resolved Eulerian drift, the material pull-back momentum equation is shown to be the on-shell form of the full stochastic Reynolds transport balance when the momentum-source covariation is retained. The resulting velocity--volatility--pressure system has an index-one differential--algebraic structure. Virtual power fixes the mechanical type of the stress impulse and separates work from quadratic covariation. A finite-correlation precursor then gives a Green--Kubo realisation of the solved displacement covariance. State dependence adds a Lyapunov noise-induced drift, while dynamic boundaries add reaction work and active/passive covariance compatibility conditions. The analysis also gives four limits: a non-zero Brownian transport limit cannot retain finite ordinary unresolved kinetic energy; total energy alone does not fix entropy production; wall tangency limits covariance rank rather than the number of stochastic modes; and a homogeneous decoupled Helmholtz--Stokes equation has only the trivial periodic solution. The result is a theory-complete, testable closure architecture. Developed turbulent statistics, logarithmic wall scaling and computational-fluid-dynamics validation are deliberately left to the expanded fluid-mechanics study.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Parallel Model-Based Derivative-Free Optimization via Rank-Two KKT Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24813)
*Donghan Wu,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于坐标反射的模型构建方法，将KKT矩阵更新降为秩至多2的扰动，利用Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury公式将模型参数求解复杂度从O(m³)降至O(n²)，并嵌入主从并行框架，在530个基准问题上验证了收敛性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的信赖域DFO方法在构建欠定二次插值模型时，需求解KKT系统确定模型参数，其计算复杂度为O(m³)，限制了并行可扩展性。本文旨在降低模型构建的计算开销，提升算法在大规模问题上的效率。

Method: 1. 证明最小Frobenius范数更新模型的KKT矩阵完全依赖于移位坐标的内积；2. 提出坐标轴反射技巧——沿单一坐标轴反射插值集可保持内积不变，仅改变KKT矩阵的一行一列，形成秩至多2的扰动；3. 利用Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury公式实现O(n²)的逆更新（当m=O(n)时）；4. 证明反射是中心化欧氏信赖域上的等距变换，保持插值集的poisedness常数，结合标准fully linear模型管理假设保证一阶全局收敛；5. 嵌入主从并行框架，使用截断共轭梯度法求解子问题。

Result: 在530个基准问题上与已有DFO求解器对比，验证了所提算法的性能优势。

Conclusion: 提出的坐标反射机制有效降低了模型构建的计算复杂度，支持并行扩展，并在理论上保证了一阶全局收敛性，数值实验表明其在实际问题中具有竞争力。

Abstract: Derivative-free optimization (DFO) addresses unconstrained problems $\min_{\x\in\RR^n} f(\x)$ where $f$ is accessed only through a zeroth-order oracle. Model-based trust-region methods construct underdetermined quadratic interpolation models from $\mathcal{O}(n)$ points and solve a KKT system to determine model parameters, costing $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ operations and limiting parallel scalability. It is shown that the KKT matrix for the minimum Frobenius norm updating model depends entirely on inner products of shifted coordinates. Reflecting the interpolation set across a single coordinate axis preserves these inner products and changes only one row and column of the KKT matrix, inducing a rank-at-most-two perturbation whose inverse update via the Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury formula costs $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ when $m=\mathcal{O}(n)$. The reflection is an isometry in centered Euclidean trust regions and preserves the poisedness constant of the interpolation set; together with standard fully linear model-management assumptions this supports first-order global convergence. The mechanism is embedded in a master-worker parallel algorithm with a Truncated Conjugate Gradient subproblem solver. Numerical results on 530 benchmark problems compare performance against an established DFO solver.

</details>


### [110] [Extremal problem of area approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24828)
*O. O. Pokutnyi,R. R. Salimov,M. V. Stefanchuk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了光滑曲线的最优分段线性逼近问题，目标是最小化原始函数图与插值折线之间所围区域的面积。对于二次抛物线，证实了最优区间划分为均匀划分并给出了最小面积的精确值；对于三次函数，证明了最优插值节点呈非均匀分布，并给出了两个内分点情形下的精确坐标和最小面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于激光/等离子切割、铣削、增材制造等工业过程中CNC设备控制程序的生成，需要优化刀具路径。

Method: 通过数学分析推导，分别针对二次抛物线和三次函数建立最优分段线性逼近的解析解，求解最小包围面积并分析插值节点的最优分布特征。

Result: 二次抛物线的最优划分为均匀划分，且获得了最小面积的精确表达式；三次函数的最优节点呈非均匀分布，给出了两个内分点情形下的精确坐标与最小面积。

Conclusion: 研究成果可应用于CAD系统中，基于最小偏差面积准则优化刀具路径。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of optimal piecewise linear approximation of a smooth curve, in which the area of the region enclosed between the graph of the original function and the constructed interpolating polyline is minimized. This problem arises in technological processes such as laser and plasma cutting of metals, milling, additive manufacturing, and in the preparation of control programs for CNC equipment. For a quadratic parabola, the exact value of the minimum area is established, and it is shown that the optimal partition of the interval is uniform. For a cubic function, it is demonstrated that the optimal interpolation nodes are distributed non-uniformly; in the case of two internal partition points, their exact coordinates and the corresponding minimum area are obtained. The results can be applied in CAD systems for optimizing tool paths based on the criterion of minimum deviation area.

</details>


### [111] [Functional H_infinity Filtering for Descriptor Systems with Incrementally Quadratic Nonlinearities under Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25000)
*Rishabh Sharma,Nutan Kumar Tomar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性描述符系统，提出了一种可直接初始化的显式状态空间功能H_infinity滤波器，通过增量二次约束刻画非线性，利用秩条件和LMI确保误差渐近稳定与L2性能界，滤波器阶数不超过待估功能向量维数，降低了计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有描述符系统的H_infinity滤波方法存在正则性假设过于严格、滤波器采用隐式描述符形式导致实现困难，且当仅需估计状态的特定功能函数时，全阶或降阶状态估计计算效率低下。

Method: 采用显式状态空间框架设计滤波器，可任意初始化；滤波器阶数≤待估功能向量维数以降低计算量；利用增量二次约束（含乘子矩阵参数化）统一刻画Lipschitz、单边Lipschitz、单调等非线性；通过系统矩阵的秩条件与LMI给出存在性充分准则。

Result: 所提条件保证了估计误差动态的渐近稳定性，并将外部扰动对误差的影响控制在预设的L2性能范数界内，数值仿真验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功克服了传统描述符系统H_infinity滤波在正则性假设、滤波器实现形式和计算效率方面的局限，为非线性描述符系统的功能滤波提供了一套完整的理论框架与设计方法。

Abstract: This paper develops a functional H_infinity filter for nonlinear descriptor systems subject to external disturbances. Conventional H_infinity filtering approaches for descriptor systems impose restrictive regularity assumptions and employ implicit descriptor-form filters, leading to practical implementation difficulties. Moreover, existing approaches mainly target full-or reduced-order state estimation, which is computationally inefficient when only a specific functional of the state is required. To address these limitations, the filter is formulated directly in an explicit state-space framework and can be initialized with arbitrary initial values. The filter order is chosen to be less than or equal to the dimension of the functional vector to be estimated, thereby reducing computational complexity. The considered nonlinearities are characterized using incremental quadratic constraints parameterized by appropriate multiplier matrices, which encompass Lipschitz, one-sided Lipschitz, monotone, and many other nonlinearities. Sufficient criteria for the existence of the proposed filter are established through a rank condition imposed on the system matrices together with a set of linear matrix inequalities (LMIs). Under these conditions, asymptotic stability of the estimation error dynamics is guaranteed, while the influence of external disturbances on the error is bounded within a prescribed L2-performance framework. Finally, numerical simulations demonstrate and validate the effectiveness of our theoretical results.

</details>


### [112] [Derivative-free optimization approach for structured symmetric matrices with fixed eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25046)
*Carmo P. Brás,Evelin H. M. Krulikovski,Marcos Raydan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种基于导数自由优化（DFO）的模型，用于解决具有指定特征值的结构化对称矩阵逆问题，其中部分元素被预分配，其余元素自由。采用确定性DFO方法（GLODS）及启发式策略进行求解，证明了基于Lipschitz连续目标函数的收敛性，并通过数值实验比较了不同方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一类特殊的逆特征值问题：构造一个对称矩阵，使其满足指定的特征值、部分零/非零元素的预分配约束，且矩阵结构对应于带权无向图的稀疏模式。该问题在多个应用领域具有重要意义。

Method: 建立导数自由优化（DFO）模型，目标函数通过对对称矩阵进行特征值分解（不可微）来评估；采用确定性全局方向直接搜索方法（GLODS）作为主要求解器，并利用目标函数的Lipschitz连续性证明收敛性；同时探索多种启发式策略作为替代方案。

Result: 初步数值结果表明，所提出的确定性DFO方法（GLODS）与多种启发式策略在不同场景下均能有效求解该逆特征值问题，且性能对比呈现差异。

Conclusion: DFO框架能够有效处理带有结构约束和特征值规格的对称矩阵逆问题，GLODS方法具有理论上的收敛保证，而启发式策略在实用中可能提供互补优势。

Abstract: A Derivative-Free Optimization (DFO) model is developed and analyzed for solving inverse structured symmetric matrix problems for which the eigenvalues are specified. Some (zero and nonzero) entries are preassigned and cannot be changed, while others should be nonzero but their values are not given. The rest of the entries are completely free. The obtained matrix must meet these criteria and have the specified eigenvalues. This specialized inverse eigenvalue problem is relevant to various applications and is linked to determining the graph, with weights on the undirected edges, of the matrix associated with its sparse pattern. Our novel optimization model requires computing the eigenvalues of a symmetric matrix to evaluate the non-differentiable objective function. We apply deterministic DFO schemes, specifically the global variant GLODS of the well-known family of directional direct search (DDS) methods. We discuss its convergence properties which are based on the fact that the objective function of our model is Lipschitz continuous. Additionally, we explore the potential benefits of using several well-established heuristic strategies to solve the proposed optimization model. We present preliminary numerical results to illustrate and compare the performance of the considered deterministic and heuristic DFO options in various possible scenarios.

</details>


### [113] [Functional Subspace Projection for Detection of Coordinated Stealthy Attacks in Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25093)
*Koto Omiloli,Satish Vedula,Olugbenga Moses Anubi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对多区域电力系统中可逃避传统残差检测器的协调动态虚假数据注入攻击，提出了时间聚合攻击模型和基于核嵌入函数子空间检测（KEFSD）方法，通过在再生核希尔伯特空间中建模残差轨迹并利用函数主成分分析识别异常时间模式，在改进的IEEE 14节点系统上验证了优于传统残差2-范数检测器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电网面临日益复杂的协同网络攻击，特别是虚假数据注入攻击（FDIAs）能规避传统的基于残差的检测器。现有大多数检测方法依赖瞬时测量值，而协调动态攻击可以在每个时间步保持隐蔽，同时引入结构化时间偏差，因此需要更有效的检测方法。

Method: 首先提出时间聚合攻击模型以捕捉时间演变和区域间协调特性；然后提出核嵌入函数子空间检测（KEFSD）方法，在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中建模残差轨迹，利用RKHS约束的函数主成分分析（PCA）识别异常时间模式。

Result: 在改进的IEEE 14节点系统上的仿真结果表明，所提方法相比传统基于残差的2-范数检测器取得了更优的检测性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合框架能够有效建模并检测多区域电力系统中的协调动态虚假数据注入攻击，克服了传统瞬时测量检测方法的局限性。

Abstract: Modern power grids are increasingly vulnerable to coordinated cyber-attacks, particularly false data injection attacks (FDIAs) that can evade conventional residual-based detectors. While most existing detection methods rely on instantaneous measurements, coordinated dynamic attacks can remain stealthy at each time step while introducing structured temporal deviations. This paper develops a joint framework for modeling and detecting such attacks in multi-area power systems. A time-aggregated attack model is first formulated to capture temporal evolution and inter-area coordination. For detection, a kernel-embedded functional subspace detection (KEFSD) method is proposed, which models residual trajectories in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) and employs RKHS-constrained functional principal component analysis (PCA) to identify anomalous temporal patterns. Simulation results on a modified IEEE 14-bus system demonstrates the proposed method achieves improved detection performance compared to the conventional residual based 2-norm detector.

</details>


### [114] [Intercity EV Charging Readiness in Türkiye: A Countrywide Scheduling and Economic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25143)
*Taner Cokyasar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文评估了土耳其电动汽车充电基础设施在城际旅行中与燃油车竞争的准备情况。通过有向无环图调度框架优化了200多万次城际EV行程，并进行了多场景分析。经济上，EV运营成本优势23-26%，总持有成本优势10-12%，但基础设施投资回报取决于充电站规模（5插头正收益，50插头负收益）。放电速率是最关键的经济杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对新兴市场国家缺乏全国层面、车辆级的城际EV充电基础设施准备度评估的空白，为土耳其提供政策参考。

Method: 采用基于有向无环图的调度框架，优化200多万次合成城际EV行程；评估9,641个已获许可充电站；进行8种情景案例研究（功率5-350kW、站容量5/50插头），以及200次参数敏感性分析（续航、放电速率、充电功率、SoC范围）；三层次经济分析（运营成本、TCO、基础设施投资）。

Result: EV运营成本优势23-26%，TCO优势10-12%；基础设施收益取决于站规模，5插头正收益而50插头负收益；放电速率是主导经济杠杆。

Conclusion: 土耳其有利的燃料-电价比使EV在城际旅行中具备成本竞争力，但充电站规模设计至关重要；放电速率是影响经济性的关键因素；为新兴市场政策制定者提供了基础设施差距、站规模权衡和普及激励的可行建议。

Abstract: This paper assesses the readiness of Türkiye's electric vehicle (EV) charging infrastructure to support cost-competitive intercity travel compared to internal combustion engine (ICE) vehicles. Utilizing a directed acyclic graph-based scheduling framework, charging stops are optimized for over 2 million synthetic intercity EV trips across the national highway network, evaluating 9,641 licensed charging stations. An eight-scenario case study examines the interplay between charger power (5--350 kW) and station capacity (5 and 50 plugs), supplemented by a 200-run parametric sensitivity analysis on EV range, discharge rate, charger power, and state-of-charge (SoC) operating bounds. A three-tier economic analysis isolates operational costs, total cost of ownership (TCO), and infrastructure investments. Economically, EVs demonstrate a 23--26% operational cost advantage driven by Türkiye's favorable fuel-to-electricity price ratio, maintaining a 10--12% TCO advantage despite higher purchase prices. However, full-system infrastructure savings heavily depend on station sizing, yielding positive returns at 5 plugs per station but negative returns at 50. Ultimately, vehicle discharge rate emerges as the dominant economic lever. This study provides the first countrywide, vehicle-level assessment of intercity EV charging readiness for an emerging market, offering actionable insights for policymakers on infrastructure gaps, station sizing tradeoffs, and adoption incentives.

</details>


### [115] [A Foundational Perspective for Partitional Clustering on Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25144)
*Derya Ipek Eroglu,Cem Iyigun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对网络上的划分聚类进行了理论分析，比较了硬分配（P-Median、SSC）和软分配（PDC、FCM）四种模型，发现SSC和FCM的聚类中心可位于边上，而PMP和PDC则倾向于顶点位置，并揭示了分配瓶颈点等结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络聚类研究多集中于顶点上的划分，缺乏对聚类中心可位于边上的理论分析，且不同分配方案（硬/软）与目标函数之间的结构差异尚未被系统揭示。

Method: 通过数学分析对四种聚类模型（PMP、SSC、PDC、FCM）进行理论对比，考察聚类中心位于顶点或边上的最优性条件，分析分配瓶颈点和顶点受限解的作用。

Result: SSC和FCM的聚类中心可位于边上，而PMP和PDC的最优中心倾向于顶点位置；不同模型在分配瓶颈点等结构性质上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 这些理论发现为设计高效聚类算法提供了新方向，并对设施选址、网络设计以及基于嵌入图的相似性搜索等应用具有指导意义。

Abstract: This study presents a theoretical analysis of partitional clustering on networks, analyzing both hard and soft assignment schemes with different objective functions. Cluster centers are not restricted to vertices but can also be located along the edges. We examine four key models: P-Median (PMP) and Sum of Squares Clustering (SSC) under hard assignment, and Probabilistic Distance Clustering (PDC) and Fuzzy C-Means (FCM) under soft assignment. Through mathematical analysis, we uncover structural properties that differentiate these models, such as the significance of assignment bottleneck points and the role of vertex-restricted solutions in determining optimal cluster centers. Our findings reveal that, while SSC and FCM can yield optimal centers along edges, PMP and PDC inherently favor vertex placement, leading to insights into clustering behavior on networks. These insights offer new directions for designing efficient algorithms and have implications ranging from facility location and network design to clustering on the embedding graphs that power similarity search in modern retrieval systems.

</details>


### [116] [Distributed Nonlinear Equality-Constrained Optimization via Feedback Linearization and Singular Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25193)
*Zihao Ren,Lei Wang,Hongye Su,Guodong Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对非凸目标与非线性等式约束下的分布式优化问题，本文提出了一种反馈线性化与奇异摄动框架，通过分离共识/可行性残差调节与可行流形上的优化，并引入快速残差跟踪动力学实现分布式实现，在局部二次增长条件下证明了指数收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指数收敛方法大多依赖凸性或仿射约束，无法处理非凸目标与非线性等式约束并存时可行性网络耦合的难题。

Method: 构建理想反馈线性化动力学，使输出行为可显式指定而零输出动力学等于可行流形上的投影梯度流；用快速残差跟踪动力学替代非局部代数解，得到仅需局部和邻居信息的奇异摄动实现。

Result: 在可行流形上的局部二次增长条件下，理想与分布式动力学均局部指数收敛；时间尺度分离足够强时，分布式实现收敛率可任意接近理想动力学；显式欧拉离散化保持局部指数收敛。

Conclusion: 该框架为非线性等式约束下的分布式非凸优化提供了可理论保证的指数收敛方法，且在仿射约束下吸引域可扩展至整个可行状态空间。

Abstract: Distributed optimization becomes particularly challenging when nonconvex objectives are combined with nonlinear equality constraints: feasibility is network coupled, while most existing exponentially convergent methods rely on convexity or affine constraints. This paper introduces a feedback-linearization and singular-perturbation framework for two representative problem classes involving, respectively, distributed local nonlinear equality constraints and nonlinear aggregate equality constraints. The framework separates the regulation of consensus and feasibility residuals from optimization along the feasible manifold. Specifically, an ideal feedback-linearized dynamics is constructed whose output behavior can be explicitly assigned, while its zero-output dynamics coincides with the projected gradient flow of the aggregate objective on the feasible manifold. The ideal feedback-linearizing input, however, is determined by a state-dependent, network-coupled algebraic equation and is therefore not directly implementable in a distributed manner. To overcome this obstruction, we replace the nonlocal algebraic solution with a fast residual-tracking dynamics, yielding a singular-perturbation realization that uses only local and neighboring information. Under a local quadratic-growth condition imposed only on the feasible manifold, we establish local exponential convergence of both the ideal and distributed dynamics. For sufficiently strong time-scale separation, the convergence rate of the distributed realization can be chosen arbitrarily close to that of the ideal dynamics. Explicit Euler discretizations are also proved to preserve local exponential convergence. Under stronger manifold regularity conditions, the region of attraction extends to a tubular neighborhood of the feasible manifold and, for affine local equality constraints, to the whole admissible state space.

</details>


### [117] [Projection and contraction methods with double inertial steps for variational inclusion problems on Hilbert spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25203)
*Moin Uddin,Mohammed Alshahrani,Qamrul Hasan Ansari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种基于双重惯性技术的投影-收缩算法用于求解Hilbert空间中的变分包含问题，分别实现了弱收敛、R-线性收敛和强收敛到最小范数解，并在分裂可行问题和弹性网正则化问题上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分包含问题在优化、偏微分方程、博弈论等领域有广泛应用，现有投影-收缩算法在收敛速度和解的选取上仍有改进空间。作者希望通过引入双重惯性技术来加速收敛并获取更优的解（如最小范数解）。

Method: 将双重惯性技术（Double Inertial Technique）融入投影-收缩（Projection-Contraction）框架，设计了三种算法：\algoO（弱收敛，自适应步长，无需Lipschitz常数先验知识）、\algoT（强单调假设下R-线性收敛）、\algoDIM（无需强单调性即可强收敛到最小范数解）。

Result: \algoO在单调且Lipschitz连续条件下弱收敛；\algoT在强单调条件下实现R-线性收敛；\algoDIM在不要求强单调性的情况下强收敛到最小范数解。数值实验验证了\algoT的R-线性收敛性，并在分裂可行问题和弹性网正则化问题上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的三种算法在不同假设条件下各有优势，双重惯性技术有效提升了投影-收缩算法的收敛性能，尤其在获取最小范数解方面具有独特价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose three projection--contraction algorithms for solving variational inclusion problems in the setting of Hilbert spaces, each incorporating a double inertial technique into the projection--contraction framework. The first algorithm \algoO achieves weak convergence under monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity of the single-valued operator, with an adaptive stepsize rule that does not require prior knowledge of the Lipschitz constant. A modified variant \algoT of \algoO attains $R$-linear convergence under the strong monotonicity assumption. The third algorithm \algoDIM obtains strong convergence to the minimum-norm solution without requiring strong monotonicity. We illustrate the proposed methods on an abstract variational inclusion problem and apply them to the split feasibility problem and the elastic net regularization problem, comparing them with existing methods in the literature. The $R$-linear convergence of \algoT is also verified through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [118] [Event-Triggered Discrete-Time Multivariable Extremum Seeking Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25223)
*Victor Hugo Pereira Rodrigues,Tiago Roux Oliveira,Miroslav Krstić,Frank Allgöwer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离散时间事件触发极值搜索框架，用于多变量非线性系统的实时优化。该框架通过状态相关触发条件实现非周期性控制更新，大幅减少驱动与通信负担，并在理论上证明了系统轨迹对未知极值邻域的实际收敛性以及平均动力学指数稳定性。仿真表明，该方法能以显著更少的执行事件实现与经典极值搜索相当的优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散时间极值搜索需要周期性输入更新，导致大量不必要的执行与通信资源消耗。本文旨在将事件触发的非周期执行思想引入极值搜索，在保证优化性能的前提下大幅降低资源开销，适用于资源受限的数字和网络控制系统。

Method: 设计状态相关的触发条件来决定控制更新的时机；结合离散时间平均分析与Lyapunov方法，证明系统轨迹实际收敛到未知极值邻域，且平均动力学具有指数稳定性；通过数值仿真验证方法有效性。

Result: 所提方法能够在非均匀采样的条件下，保持经典极值搜索的核心优化机制；理论证明了实际收敛性与指数稳定性；数值仿真显示在显著减少执行事件数量的情况下实现可比的优化性能。

Conclusion: 精心设计的事件触发规则可以在大幅降低输入更新次数的同时，维持极值搜索的优化能力，该方法适用于资源感知的数字与网络化控制实现。

Abstract: This paper introduces a discrete-time event-triggered extremum seeking framework for real-time optimization of multivariable nonlinear systems. In contrast to conventional discrete-time extremum seeking implementations that rely on periodic input updates, the proposed scheme updates the control action only when a state-dependent triggering condition is met, enabling aperiodic execution and substantial reduction of actuation and communication effort. The resulting closed-loop architecture reconciles two structurally different paradigms: the periodic excitation required for gradient recovery in extremum seeking and the aperiodic philosophy of event-triggered control. By combining discrete-time averaging arguments with Lyapunov-based analysis, we prove practical convergence of the system trajectories to a neighborhood of the unknown extremum and exponential stability of the corresponding average dynamics despite the loss of uniform sampling. The results show that appropriately designed triggering rules preserve the essential optimization mechanisms of classical extremum seeking while drastically reducing the number of input updates. Numerical simulations demonstrate that comparable optimization performance can be achieved with significantly fewer actuation events, highlighting the suitability of the method for resource-aware digital and networked control implementations.

</details>


### [119] [Proximal Gradient Methods for Unconstrained Set Optimization Problems with Set-Valued Maps of Finite Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25252)
*Ravi Raushan,Debdas Ghosh,Anshika,V. Vetrivel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对无约束集值优化问题，提出了带线搜索和不带线搜索的两种近端梯度方法，在凸、尖、闭固体锥诱导的下集序关系下，建立了平稳性条件与弱极小点的联系，证明了全局收敛性和O(1/√k)的收敛速率，并通过数值实验验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集值优化问题的求解方法（如最速下降法）收敛性结果有限，缺乏更高效的近端梯度方法及其理论分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 通过一族向量优化问题的弱有效点来刻画原问题的弱极小点；2) 建立平稳性条件并将其与弱极小点关联；3) 基于平稳条件定义非平稳点处的下降方向；4) 对带线搜索的方法，设计Armijo型线搜索条件并证明步长存在性；5) 在温和假设下证明全局收敛性，并分析计算复杂度。

Result: 两种近端梯度方法均具有全局收敛性，收敛速率均为O(1/√k)；带线搜索的方法在理论上优于先前最速下降法的收敛结果；数值实验验证了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的近端梯度方法（尤其带线搜索版本）为求解无约束集值优化问题提供了理论上更优、实践中有效的求解方案。

Abstract: This work presents two different types of proximal gradient methods, with line search and without line search, for solving unconstrained set-valued optimization problems under the lower set-less ordering relation induced by a solid cone that is convex, pointed, and closed. The objective mapping of the problem involves finitely many functions, with each one being the sum of a continuously differentiable function and a convex function that is proper and closed. We present an approach to characterize weakly minimal points of the problem with the help of weakly efficient points of a family of vector optimization problems. Thereafter, we establish a stationarity condition along with its connection with weakly minimal points of the problem under study. Based on the stationary condition, the concept of a descent direction at a non-stationary point is discussed. In view of the line search-based method, we formulate an Armijo-type line search condition and establish the existence of such a step-size. For the proposed methods, global convergence is established under mild assumptions. The convergence analysis of the proximal gradient method with line search provides a theoretical advancement over the convergence results previously established for the steepest descent method in set-valued optimization problems. In addition, we analyze the computational complexity of the proposed methods and show that both methods achieve a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{k})$. Numerical results are reported to test the performance of the methods in practice.

</details>


### [120] [Microsecond-Class Powered-Descent Optimization via Exact Condensation and Strong Convex Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25260)
*Wenbo Li,Ziqi Xu,Dai Shen,Shengping Gong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过三种结构性简化（含能量加权项、终端状态灵敏度递推消除中间状态、燃料范数与推力球的近端算子闭式解），将燃料主导的凸优化动力下降问题从300个原始变量+174个等式乘子的规模压缩为90个控制变量+6个终端乘子，并使用固定预算外推比例-积分投影梯度法在C17数组中求解。在火星动力下降算例中，迭代次数从2744次降至93次，燃料消耗仅增加0.033%，平均求解时间68.2微秒，P99为128.1微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 燃料主导的动力下降问题可写为凸规划，但全状态epigraph形式存在大量状态变量、燃料epigraph变量和动力学等式约束，且纯燃料目标函数缺乏强凸性曲率，导致求解效率低下。

Method: 三种结构简化：(1) 引入维度一致的低权重能量项使控制解唯一；(2) 终端状态灵敏度递推精确消除所有中间状态，结合可逆行归一化将30节点基线问题从300个原始变量+174个等式乘子压缩为90个控制变量+6个终端乘子；(3) 利用燃料范数与推力球的共享径向结构推导出群收缩+幅度裁剪的精确闭式近端算子。最后采用固定预算外推比例-积分投影梯度法迭代求解。

Result: 在能量权重0.02、相对参考解容差10^{-3}条件下，迭代次数从2744降至93，燃料指标仅增加0.033%。平均求解时间68.2微秒，P99为128.1微秒（Intel i7-10875H）。

Conclusion: 本文贡献不在于新的无损凸化定理，而在于凸核心问题的快速求解。由于测试案例中推力集已是凸球，该方法实现了微秒级实时求解。

Abstract: Fuel-dominant powered descent can be written as a convex program, but the usual full-state epigraph formulation still carries many state variables, fuel epigraph variables, and dynamics equalities. In addition, the pure-fuel objective provides no strong-convexity curvature. This paper combines three structural reductions. First, a dimensionally consistent low-weight energy term makes the control solution unique. Second, a terminal-state sensitivity recursion eliminates every intermediate state exactly; invertible row normalization turns the 30-node baseline with 300 primal variables and 174 equality multipliers into a problem with 90 control variables and six terminal multipliers. Third, the shared radial structure of the fuel norm and thrust ball gives an exact closed-form proximal operator consisting of group shrinkage followed by magnitude clipping. The condensed problem is solved with a fixed-budget extrapolated proportional--integral projected-gradient iteration implemented in fixed-size C17 arrays. In a Mars powered-descent case with energy weight 0.02 and relative reference-solution tolerance $10^{-3}$, the iteration count decreases from 2744 for the pure-fuel full-state epigraph baseline to 93, while the fuel metric increases by only 0.033\%. The mean end-to-end solve time is \SI{68.2}{\micro\second}, and P99 is \SI{128.1}{\micro\second}, on an Intel i7-10875H. Because the thrust set in this test case is already a convex ball, the contribution is fast solution of the convex core rather than a new lossless-convexification theorem.

</details>


### [121] [A Numerical Investigation of the Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn Inequality for Polyhedral Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25419)
*Josué Díaz-Avalos,Antoine Laurain*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维凸多面体中最小化拉普拉斯算子第一Dirichlet特征值的问题，通过支持超平面参数化将问题转化为有限维约束优化，并采用拉格朗日框架进行数值求解。数值结果得到了柏拉图立体、棱柱、十四面体等已知多面体，以及具有对称性的非正则最优形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题是经典Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn不等式的多面体版本，旨在寻找给定面数下使第一Dirichlet特征值最小的凸多面体形状。

Method: 通过支持超平面对多面体进行参数化，将原问题转化为等价的有穷维约束极小化问题；采用拉格朗日框架进行数值求解；在假设每个顶点恰好与d个面相邻的条件下，将多面体面的有限维摄动与特征值和体积的无穷维形状导数相结合，进行灵敏度分析。

Result: 数值结果给出了已知多面体（如柏拉图立体、棱柱、十四面体），也揭示了具有多种对称性的非正则最优形状。

Conclusion: 所提出的数值方法能有效求解多面体约束下的特征值优化问题，得到的最优形状依赖于给定的面数，既有正则多面体也有非正则对称多面体。

Abstract: We present numerical results for the problem of minimizing the first Dirichlet eigenvalue of the Laplacian among three-dimensional convex polyhedra with a prescribed number of facets. This problem can be viewed as a polyhedral version of the classical Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn inequality. Using a parameterization of polytopes by supporting hyperplanes, the problem is first reformulated as an equivalent finite-dimensional constrained minimization problem. A Lagrangian framework is employed for the numerical solution. Under the assumption that each vertex is incident to exactly $d$ facets, the sensitivity analysis of the Lagrangian combines finite-dimensional perturbations of the facets of $d$-dimensional polyhedra with infinite-dimensional shape derivatives of both the eigenvalue and the volume. Depending on the number of facets, the numerical results yield well-known polyhedra, such as Platonic solids, prisms, and the tetrakaidecahedron, or reveal nonregular optimal shapes exhibiting several symmetries.

</details>


### [122] [Exact discrete-adjoint optimization of trap timing and placement in a Stieltjes-time reaction-diffusion model: A Galicia case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25450)
*Francisco J. Fernández,Iván Area*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对季节性种群模型，提出了一种Stieltjes时间反应-扩散模型的有限维时序与投放位置控制问题，推导了全离散残差的精确伴随，并在真实网格上应用于Vespa velutina诱捕 campaigns，验证了机器精度一致性和伴随加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性种群模型需要同时处理扩散、非活跃期和突发生物迁移，同时保持对局部干预的可微性，现有方法难以满足这些要求。

Method: 采用连续有限元和隐式Stieltjes-Euler格式处理传播、房室替换、重置和历史相位平均；推导了盒约束惩罚问题的离散控制-状态映射的适定性和可微性，证明了极小值存在性、一阶平稳性以及直接敏感度与伴随梯度的等价性。

Result: 基准测试显示机器精度一致（低于3×10⁻¹⁵），伴随加速比随诱捕器数量从1增加到4而从约7.5提升到30.9；在加利西亚网格上，双簇控制方案的缩放梯度无穷范数为2.97×10⁻⁵，精细控制比仅时间控制和参考控制降低诊断目标0.49209%。

Conclusion: 所提框架完全可复现，但不声称全局最优性或校准的田间管理处方；目标降低依赖于未校准的诱捕-死亡率强度，不应视为实际捕获效果。

Abstract: Seasonal population models must combine diffusion, inactive periods and abrupt biological transfers while remaining differentiable with respect to localized interventions. We formulate a finite-dimensional timing-and-placement control problem for a Stieltjes-time reaction--diffusion model and derive the exact adjoint of the fully discrete residual. Continuous finite elements and an implicit Stieltjes--Euler scheme represent propagation, compartment replacement, resets and historical phase averages. For the resulting box-constrained penalized problem, we prove well-posedness and differentiability of the discrete control-to-state map, existence of a minimizer, first-order stationarity, and equivalence between direct-sensitivity and adjoint gradients. Benchmark tests show machine-precision agreement (below $3\times10^{-15}$) and adjoint speed-ups from about $7.5$ to $30.9$ as the number of traps increases from one to four. The method is applied to \textit{Vespa velutina} trap campaigns on a realistic Galicia mesh. Smoothed activation windows, domain-normalized moving kernels and the complete adjoint are independently verified. The final two-cluster control remains admissible and has a scaled gradient infinity norm of $2.97\times10^{-5}$ with 120 temporal subdivisions per month. Fixed-control evaluations at 60, 120 and 240 subdivisions yield a refinement-increment ratio of $0.4984$. At the finest level, the joint control outperforms time-only and reference controls, reducing the diagnostic objective by $0.49209%$. This reduction depends on uncalibrated trap-to-mortality intensities and should not be interpreted as field capture efficacy. The framework is fully reproducible, without claiming global optimality or a calibrated field-management prescription.

</details>


### [123] [Optimal Parameter Design for DIGing on Minimizing Unweighted Sum of Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25463)
*Qiuchen Tian,Li Chai,Jinming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式不精确梯度追踪（DIGing）算法，通过将其表示为动态线性系统并分解为图频率解耦子系统，利用控制理论中的劳斯稳定判据，推导了最优最坏情况收敛率的显式公式及相应参数，发现即使对于最简单的目标函数，DIGing收敛速度也较慢，因此加速是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化算法中缺乏设计合适参数以实现更快收敛的通用方法。

Method: 将DIGing迭代算法表示为动态线性系统，分解为不同图频率获得解耦子系统；利用控制理论中的劳斯稳定判据推导最优最坏情况收敛率的显式公式及对应参数。

Result: 给出了最优最坏情况收敛率的显式公式及相应参数；发现即使对于最简单的目标函数（未加权平方和），DIGing收敛速度仍然较慢。

Conclusion: 所提方法可视为DIGing算法在求解一般目标函数时实现最优参数设计的第一步；加速对于一般应用是必要的。

Abstract: There is no general method for designing proper parameters to achieve faster convergence in distributed optimization algorithms. In this paper, we consider the distributed inexact gradient tracking (DIGing) algorithm with the objective function being the unweighted sum of squares. By representing the iteration algorithm as a dynamical linear system, we decompose it into different graph frequencies and obtain a set of decoupled subsystems, on which we can easily analyze the convergence rate. By using Routh stability criterion from control theory, we derive the explicit formula of the optimal worst-case convergence rate and the corresponding parameters. We can see that the convergence rate of DIGing is slow even for the simplest objective functions, thus acceleration is necessary for general application. The proposed method can be viewed as the first step toward optimal parameter design of DIGing algorithm in solving general objective functions.

</details>


### [124] [Conditional gradient methods on unbounded feasible regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25594)
*R. Díaz Millán,Tuan Thanh Lu,Julien Ugon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于紧致限制原理的条件梯度方法，用于处理无界可行域上的凸优化问题。通过固定或动态更新的紧致子集替代原无界区域，在仅对紧致子集求解线性最小化的情况下，仍能保证目标值收敛到全局最优，并证明了光滑凸目标下的超线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典条件梯度法的收敛理论针对紧致可行域建立，但许多实际凸可行区域是闭无界的。现有方法在处理无界区域时面临线性最小化不易直接求解的问题，因此需要一种能够利用紧致限制来适配条件梯度法的理论框架。

Method: 提出两种紧致限制方案：(1) 固定限制：使用包含初始目标次水平集的一个紧致凸集；(2) 动态限制：沿迭代过程通过交运算更新紧致凸集。两种方案均确保限制集包含相应目标次水平集，从而线性最小化仅在紧致子集上求解。

Result: 对于光滑凸目标，证明了标准超线性收敛率；在曲率假设下，非光滑条件次梯度扩展具有次线性收敛率。还给出了基于强凸性、精确次水平集和上境图帽的构造性限制方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧致限制原理有效扩展了条件梯度法到无界可行域，在理论和算法上均可行，数值实验验证了固定和动态限制在无界可行域上的有效性。

Abstract: The conditional gradient method is attractive when linear minimization over the feasible region is substantially cheaper than projection. Its classical convergence theory, however, is formulated for compact feasible sets, whereas many natural convex feasible regions are closed and unbounded. This paper studies a simple compact-restriction principle for applying conditional gradient steps to unbounded feasible regions. The first scheme uses one compact convex set containing the initial objective sublevel set. The second scheme updates the restriction by intersecting compact convex sets generated along the iterations. In both cases the linear minimization oracle is solved only over compact subsets, but the resulting objective values converge to the global optimum of the original problem, provided the compact restrictions contain the corresponding objective sublevel sets. For smooth convex objectives we obtain the standard superlinear convergence rate of the objective. We also record constructive restrictions based on strong convexity, exact sublevel sets, and epigraph caps, and include a nonsmooth conditional subgradient extension with a sublinear convergence rate under a curvature assumption. Numerical experiments illustrate the behaviour of the fixed and dynamic restrictions on unbounded feasible regions.

</details>


### [125] [Bilinear Koopman-Based Robust Model Predictive Control for Unknown Nonlinear Systems via Contraction Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25658)
*Yuki Higuchi,Kazuhiro Sato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于数据驱动双线性Koopman实现的一般非线性约束未知非线性系统，提出了一种鲁棒模型预测控制（RMPC）框架。通过将预测的升维状态重新投影回有效流形，构建误差感知的离散时间控制仿射预测器，并基于鲁棒控制收缩度量开发同位管结构，实现了对原始非线性约束的鲁棒满足、递归可行性和收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Koopman的RMPC方法多局限于线性Koopman实现，而双线性Koopman实现虽能提供更高近似精度，但如何将建模误差纳入RMPC框架尚未得到充分研究。核心难点在于有限维Koopman预测器不保持有效升维状态流形，多步预测可能离开一步误差证书适用区域。

Method: 1) 将预测的升维状态重新投影回有效流形，构建原始状态空间中的误差感知离散时间控制仿射预测器；2) 基于离散时间鲁棒控制收缩度量（RCCM）开发同位管（homothetic tube）结构；3) 设计包含终端代价的管式RMPC问题。

Result: 理论证明了真实闭环轨迹对原始非线性约束的鲁棒满足、递归可行性以及收敛到目标状态邻域。数值实验表明该方法能实现非线性系统的鲁棒镇定，并在性能上优于现有基于Koopman的RMPC方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于双线性Koopman实现的RMPC框架有效解决了多步预测中升维状态偏离流形的问题，无需不切实际的假设即可实现非线性系统的鲁棒控制，为数据驱动MPC提供了新的理论支撑与实践方案。

Abstract: Data-driven model predictive control (MPC) using Koopman operator theory is a promising approach for constrained control of unknown nonlinear systems. While linear Koopman realizations are commonly used due to their simplicity, bilinear Koopman realizations can provide significantly higher approximation accuracy for nonlinear control systems. However, robust MPC (RMPC) formulations that account for modeling errors in bilinear Koopman realizations remain limited. This paper proposes a RMPC framework for unknown nonlinear systems with general nonlinear constraints based on data-driven bilinear Koopman realizations. A central difficulty is that finite-dimensional Koopman predictors need not preserve the manifold of valid lifted states, so multi-step prediction in lifted coordinates may leave the region where one-step error certificates apply. We address this issue by reprojecting each predicted lifted state back onto the manifold, thereby obtaining an error-aware discrete-time control-affine predictor in the original state space without impractical assumptions. For this predictor, we develop a discrete-time robust control contraction metric based homothetic tube construction, and then formulate a tube-based RMPC problem with terminal ingredients. Under the proposed formulation, we prove robust satisfaction of the original nonlinear constraints by the true closed-loop trajectory, recursive feasibility, and convergence to a neighborhood of the target state. Numerical experiments demonstrate robust stabilization of nonlinear systems and the advantages of the proposed method over existing Koopman-based RMPC approaches in terms of performance.

</details>


### [126] [Face Structure in Partial Facial Reduction of Semidefinite Relaxations of Binary Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25689)
*Hao Hu,Mingming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究二元优化问题的半定松弛中，基于非负对角、对角占优(DD)和缩放对角占优(SDD)锥的可行内逼近的部分面约简(FR)方法。证明了SDD-部分FR识别的每个终端面可由固定为0/1的变量和被迫相等的变量组描述，且具有全列秩{0,1}基矩阵。同时揭示了DD和SDD-部分FR在奇异度二族上需要最大步数，并指出因子宽度限制会阻碍稠密有效仿射等式的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半定松弛在二元优化问题中常违反Slater条件，完全面约简虽能恢复严格可行性但辅助问题代价高昂。本文旨在通过可处理的锥内逼近（DD、SDD等）实现部分面约简，在计算代价与恢复结构之间取得平衡。

Method: 理论分析：1) 证明SDD-部分FR识别的终端面结构（固定变量和等式约束）；2) 刻画多面体内逼近中每个步骤暴露的面；3) 构造反例证明DD/SDD-部分FR的最坏情况步数；4) 分析因子宽度限制对检测稠密仿射等式的局限性。

Result: 1) SDD-部分FR终端面由{0,1}基矩阵描述，支持稀疏保持的约简公式；2) 多面体内逼近中面由外逼近上的活动不等式刻画；3) 存在奇异度二的族使DD/SDD-部分FR需要最大可能步数；4) 因子宽度限制导致无法检测某些稠密有效仿射等式。

Conclusion: 可追踪的部分FR能有效恢复二元优化半定松弛中的特定结构（固定变量和等式约束），但存在根本性局限：最坏情况下步数指数增长，且因子宽度约束会遗漏某些稠密结构。这为选择部分FR策略提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Semidefinite relaxations of binary optimization problems often fail Slater's condition. Full facial reduction restores strict feasibility but may require auxiliary problems as costly as the original relaxation. We analyze partial facial reduction based on tractable inner approximations of the positive semidefinite cone: the nonnegative diagonal, diagonally dominant (DD), and scaled diagonally dominant (SDD) cones.
  For this class of SDP relaxations, we prove that every terminal face identified by SDD-partial FR is described by variables fixed to zero or one and groups of variables forced to be equal. The face admits a full-column-rank \(\{0,1\}\) basis matrix with disjoint column supports, yielding a natural reduced formulation that can preserve sparsity. For polyhedral inner approximations, we characterize the face exposed at each step through the inequalities active on the associated outer relaxation.
  We also establish sharp limitations. We construct a family with singularity degree two for which DD- and SDD-partial FR require the maximum possible number of steps. We further show that factor-width restrictions can prevent detection of dense valid affine equalities. These results characterize both the structure recovered by tractable partial FR and the cost of restricting the exposing-matrix search.

</details>


### [127] [On the boundedness of infinite products of relaxed projections: perturbations resilience and dynamic string-averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25797)
*Daniel Reem,Yair Censor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Bauschke和Tung（2026）将Meshulam（1996）及Aharoni-Duchet-Wajnryb（1984）关于松弛投影无穷乘积有界性的结果从有限维扩展到无限维Hilbert空间的基础上，进一步推广：允许扰动并证明扰动韧性，考虑闭半空间与闭超平面的混合，使用动态加权和与动态字符串的松弛投影迭代过程。同时通过大量反例说明将结果推广到任意闭凸集的局限性，并建立了关于非扩张映射无穷乘积一致有界/无界性的独立定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Bauschke和Tung（2026）最新工作的基础上，进一步拓展松弛投影无穷乘积有界性定理的适用范围，探索其在更一般设定下的推广可能性与局限性。

Method: 引入扰动机制并证明扰动韧性；将闭半空间与闭超平面混合纳入分析框架；设计动态加权和与动态字符串（动态长度）的松弛投影迭代过程；通过构造大量反例展示推广到任意闭凸集时的局限性；建立非扩张映射无穷乘积一致有界/无界性的一般定理。

Result: 成功将Bauschke-Tung结果推广到含扰动、混合闭半空间与闭超平面、动态加权和与动态字符串的情形；发现当控制非循环甚至非几乎循环时，无法推广到任意闭凸集；建立了关于非扩张映射无穷乘积一致有界性的独立定理。

Conclusion: 本文从多个维度推广了Bauschke-Tung定理，同时明确指出了其推广到任意闭凸集时的本质局限性，为后续研究提供了理论基础和边界条件。

Abstract: Very recently (2026), Bauschke and Tung extended from finite- to infinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces a result published by Meshulam in 1996 (following an earlier result of Aharoni-Duchet-Wajnryb from 1984) regarding the boundedness of infinite products of relaxed projections onto a finite family of closed affine subspaces. In the present note we extend in various ways the result of Bauschke and Tung by allowing certain perturbations and proving perturbation resilience, by considering a mixture of closed half-spaces and closed hyperplanes, and by using dynamic weighted sums of dynamic strings (of dynamic lengths) of relaxed projections in the iterative process. We also discuss the limitation to generalize the Bauschke-Tung result to arbitrary closed and convex sets by presenting a large family of counterexamples in which the associated control is not cyclic and not even almost cyclic. Along the way we establish a general theorem of independent interest regarding the uniform boundedness and uniform unboundedness of infinite products of nonexpansive mappings in a normed space setting.

</details>


### [128] [Analysis and Optimal Design of Equilibria in the Vlasov-Poisson System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25698)
*Alfio Borzì,Gennaro Infante,Giovanni Mascali,Riccardo Scida*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Vlasov-Poisson系统稳态Maxwellian平衡框架下研究外电场产生的平衡粒子分布最优设计问题。分析了归一化Poisson-Boltzmann方程，通过Schauder不动点论证和变分方法证明了自洽平衡势的存在唯一性。建立了统一的平衡密度最优设计框架，涵盖势谷、二次跟踪和Kullback-Leibler三种设计准则，推导了一阶最优性条件，并提出了投影非线性共轭梯度算法进行数值求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在外电场作用下，如何最优设计平衡粒子分布是一个重要的理论问题。作者希望在Vlasov-Poisson系统框架内，系统性地建立平衡势的存在唯一性，并在此基础上构建统一的最优设计框架，以处理不同类型的设计准则。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：1) 基于归一化非线性源均匀估计的Schauder不动点论证；2) 基于严格凸自由能泛函的变分方法。在最优设计方面，推导一阶最优性条件，并采用投影非线性共轭梯度算法进行数值求解。

Result: 证明了自洽平衡势的存在唯一性，建立了覆盖势谷、二次跟踪和Kullback-Leibler准则的统一优化框架，并给出了数值求解算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为外电场作用下平衡粒子分布的最优设计提供了完整的理论分析和数值求解框架。

Abstract: The optimal design of equilibrium particle distributions generated by external electric fields is investigated in the framework of stationary Maxwellian equilibria of the Vlasov-Poisson system. The resulting normalized Poisson-Boltzmann equation is analyzed, and existence and uniqueness of the self-consistent equilibrium potential are established by two complementary approaches. A Schauder fixed-point argument based on uniform estimates for the normalized nonlinear source and a variational approach based on a strictly convex free-energy functional are developed. Building upon these analytical results, a unified optimal design framework is formulated for equilibrium densities. Valley-potential, quadratic tracking, and Kullback-Leibler design criteria are treated within the same optimization framework. First-order optimality conditions are derived, and a projected nonlinear conjugate-gradient algorithm is proposed for the numerical solution of the resulting optimization problems.

</details>


### [129] [Vertex Loci of Max-Indicator Polytopes and Vertex Retrieval in Ball Polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25752)
*Marius Costandin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在球心位于球面且半径相等的条件下，通过扰动目标点并利用最大指示多面体的顶点轨迹线，提出了一种线性规划算法来唯一确定满足特定条件的Q的顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最大指示多面体家族参数变化时，其顶点沿某些直线（顶点轨迹）滑动，这些直线通过空间原点和Q的顶点。通过沿特定方向对称扰动目标点，可证明扰动后的两个多面体的交集严格包含于未扰动多面体，唯一公共边界点位于连接原点与满足条件的Q的顶点的直线上，从而可唯一确定该顶点。

Method: 1. 假设球心位于球面、半径相等且Q只有一个顶点x满足1^T·x=0；2. 利用顶点轨迹线性质（通过原点与Q的顶点）；3. 沿1_{n×1}方向上下扰动目标点C_S；4. 证明对称扰动后的两个最大指示多面体的交集严格包含于未扰动多面体，且唯一公共边界点在连接原点与目标顶点的直线上；5. 通过线性规划求解该顶点。

Result: 在给定假设条件下，提出了一种高效的线性规划算法来检索满足1^T·x=0的唯一顶点x。

Conclusion: 通过扰动目标点并利用最大指示多面体顶点轨迹线的几何性质，可在特定假设下用线性规划高效求解Q的唯一指定顶点。

Abstract: The so called "max-indicator polytopes" were introduced in \cite{mainB} as an intersection of half-spaces related to the problem of finding the farthest in an intersection of balls $\mathcal{Q}$, from a given target point $C_S$. It was shown that if the target point belongs to the convex hull of the balls centers, then a specific intersection of half-spaces forms a single real variable parameterized polytope family which is used to characterized the extreme points.
  This paper, under the assumptions that the intersecting balls centers lie on a sphere and all have equal radii with only one vertex $x$, of $\mathcal{Q}$, meeting $1_{n \times 1}\cdot x = 0$, we provide an efficient algorithm to retrieve that vertex.
  The main idea behind the result is that the max-indicator polytopes vertices, as the family parameter varies, slide on some lines, that we call vertex locii. These lines pass through the space origin and the vertices of $\mathcal{Q}$. Perturbing the target point $C_S$ up and down along the direction $1_{n\times 1}$ we are able to show that the intersection of two simetrically perturbed max-indicator polytopes is strictly included in the unperturbed polytope, with the only boundary common points being on the line connecting the origin with the vertex of $\mathcal{Q}$ meeting $1_{n\times 1}^T\cdot x = 0$. As such, under the said assumptions, the only such vertex can be retrieved using linear programming.

</details>


### [130] [Variance-Reduced Conditional Gradient Methods under Markovian Sampling for Nonconvex Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25785)
*Zhaojun Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对固定遍历马尔可夫链单轨迹下的随机复合非凸优化问题，提出MC-ALFCG算法，结合动量条件梯度法、耦合截断多层蒙特卡洛估计和逐迭代裁剪，在广义Frank-Wolfe间隙下实现投影无约束的复合优化。理论分析将马尔可夫递归简化为独立采样情形，导出混合时间相关的样本复杂度上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单轨迹方差缩减理论仅覆盖光滑无约束目标，缺乏对投影无约束复合非凸优化（带紧凸集约束）的处理。本文旨在填补这一空白，将理论推广至更一般的设置。

Method: 提出MC-ALFCG算法：1) 采用动量条件梯度法（Frank-Wolfe变体）；2) 耦合截断多层蒙特卡洛（Capped Multilevel Monte Carlo）估计，最深嵌套平均利用同一轨迹的连续状态，产生条件偏差O(τ_mix/T)；3) 通过耦合控制迭代位移的梯度差二阶矩；4) 逐迭代裁剪确保路径有界性。分析上将马尔可夫递归简化为独立采样，参数映射σ²→2ΛG_σ², L²→2ΛL², Λ=O(τ_mix log T)。

Result: 正定中心噪声下，期望样本复杂度为Õ((τ_mix²G_σ+τ_mix^{5/2}G_σ²)ε^{-3}+τ_mix⁵ε^{-2})；无噪声特例达到Õ(ε^{-2})且常数与混合时间无关；混合时间无关变体达到Õ(τ_mix⁶ε^{-3}+τ_mix³ε^{-2})。

Conclusion: 本文成功将单轨迹方差缩减理论从光滑无约束扩展至投影无约束复合非凸优化，提出首个具有严格理论保证的算法。数值实验验证了依赖敏感性、非凸复合实例和裁剪行为。所有保证在固定转移核下以期望形式成立。

Abstract: We study stochastic composite nonconvex optimization over a compact convex set when gradient samples arrive along a single trajectory of a fixed ergodic Markov chain. Existing single-trajectory variance-reduction theory covers smooth unconstrained objectives; we address the projection-free composite setting using the generalized Frank-Wolfe gap. We propose MC-ALFCG, which combines a momentum conditional-gradient method with coupled capped multilevel Monte Carlo estimation and per-iteration clipping. The deepest nested average uses consecutive states from the same trajectory, yielding conditional bias $O(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}/T)$ uniformly over the starting state, while coupling controls the gradient-difference second moment through the iterate displacement. Clipping enforces the pathwise bounds needed by the adaptive analysis. We reduce the Markovian recursion to its independent-sampling counterpart under $σ^2\mapsto 2ΛG_σ^2$ and $L^2\mapsto 2ΛL^2$, where $Λ=O(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}\log T)$. For positive centered noise, the tuned method achieves expected sample complexity $\widetilde{O}((τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2G_σ+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^{5/2}G_σ^2)\varepsilon^{-3}+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^5\varepsilon^{-2})$. The exactly noiseless specialization achieves $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ with mixing-time-free constants, while a mixing-time-oblivious variant achieves $\widetilde{O}(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^6\varepsilon^{-3}+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^3\varepsilon^{-2})$. All guarantees are in expectation under a fixed transition kernel. Controlled numerical studies examine dependence sensitivity, a nonconvex composite instance, and clipping behavior.

</details>


### [131] [Optimization under Persistent State-Dependent Bias: Gradient-based Method and Complexity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26032)
*Zhaoxian Wu,Quan Xiao,Tayfun Gokmen,Tianyi Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在持续且状态依赖偏差的随机梯度下降（SGD）的收敛性，提出了Residual Learning算法，通过将原问题转化为等效双层优化问题，消除硬件缺陷导致的收敛偏差，并量化了响应函数对收敛复杂度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SGD假设更新无偏，但实际硬件中更新会受状态依赖的响应函数影响而产生持续偏差，导致SGD收敛到非真实最优解。本文旨在解决这一收敛失败问题。

Method: 将原始有偏优化问题重新表述为等效的双层优化问题，提出基于梯度的Residual Learning算法，从理论上证明该算法能在硬件缺陷存在下找到原无偏优化问题的解，并通过硬件条件数量化响应函数对收敛复杂度的影响。

Result: Residual Learning算法能够实现精确收敛，找到原始无偏优化问题的解；通过构造困难实例证明了多项式依赖硬件条件数在一般情况下是不可避免的。

Conclusion: 所提Residual Learning算法能有效消除硬件响应函数引起的持续状态依赖偏差，数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies the convergence of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) when the implemented updates are subject to a persistent and state-dependent bias, in which the desired update is scaled by response functions component-wise. Our first contribution is to demonstrate that SGD in this setting implicitly optimizes a penalized problem whose minimizer does not coincide with the true minimizer. To mitigate this convergence failure, we reformulate the original task as an equivalent bilevel optimization problem and propose a gradient-based algorithm, termed Residual Learning. Theoretical analysis shows that Residual Learning finds a solution to the original, unbiased optimization problem despite the hardware imperfections. Beyond exact convergence, we quantify how the response functions affect convergence complexity via the hardware condition number and show that a polynomial dependence on it is unavoidable in general, via a construction of a hard instance. The theoretical results are supported by numerical simulations that demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>
