# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Effective Field Theory of Operator Scrambling from Strong-to-Weak Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24925)
*Bai-Lin Cheng,Shao-Kai Jian,Zhi-Cheng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对布朗或短时关联的大N马约拉纳系统，建立了基于强-弱U(1)对称性破缺的算符置乱有效场论。发现非相互作用费米子极限下，OTOC的四重Keldysh轮廓存在 emergent 强U(1)对称性，其慢模相位对应局域算符大小。相互作用破缺该对称性产生质量项，将扩散传播转化为混沌增长。高阶破缺项受时间反演与轮廓置换对偶性约束，最终OTOC动力学由含噪FKPP方程描述，统一了指数增长、弹道传播、非线性饱和与随机前锋展宽等特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTOC是诊断算符置乱的标准工具，但其有效动力学的对称性原理一直不清楚。本文旨在为算符置乱建立对称性基础的有效场论框架，揭示算符大小流体力学和置乱现象的对称性起源。

Method: 基于强-弱U(1)对称性破缺框架，从非相互作用费米子极限下的emergent强对称性出发，引入相互作用作为对称性破缺项，利用时间反演与轮廓置换对偶性约束有效作用量的形式，推导出含噪FKPP方程描述OTOC动力学，并在Brownian SYK链中通过鞍点展开验证。

Result: 建立了强-弱U(1)对称性破缺的算符置乱有效场论；识别了算符大小为共轭密度；推导出对称性破缺产生质量项将扩散转化为混沌增长；证明对偶性约束下有效作用量至多二次；Lyapunov指数的正性源于实时间路径积分的收敛性；OTOC动力学由含噪FKPP方程统一描述。

Conclusion: 揭示了算符置乱的对称性起源，建立了算符大小流体力学与混沌之间的深层联系，为理解量子混沌提供了对称性视角。

Abstract: Operator scrambling is commonly diagnosed by the growth of out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs), yet a general symmetry principle underlying their effective dynamics has remained elusive. For Brownian or short-time-correlated large-$N$ Majorana systems, we develop a symmetry-based effective field theory for operator scrambling, organized by a strong-to-weak U(1) symmetry breaking in operator space. The key observation is that, in the noninteracting fermion limit, the four-fold Keldysh contour representation of an OTOC admits an emergent strong U(1) symmetry in a doubled Hilbert-space description, even when the original system has no ordinary conserved quantity. The associated slow mode is the phase of the strong-charge creation operator, whose conjugate density is identified with the local operator size. Generic interactions explicitly break the strong symmetry and generate a mass term at lowest order for the would-be Goldstone mode, thereby converting diffusive operator spreading into chaotic growth. We further show that higher-order symmetry breaking terms are tightly constrained by an emergent duality that combines time reversal with contour permutation. This duality fixes the effective action up to quadratic order in the response field, relates the multiplicative noise strength directly to the Lyapunov exponent, and makes the positivity of the Lyapunov exponent a consequence of convergence of the real-time path integral. The resulting OTOC dynamics is governed by a noisy FKPP equation, which captures within a unified framework the early-time exponential growth, ballistic propagation, nonlinear saturation, and stochastic front broadening of operator scrambling. We verify this construction in a Brownian SYK chain, where a direct saddle-point expansion reproduces the symmetry-based effective action. Our results reveal a symmetry origin of operator-size hydrodynamics and scrambling.

</details>


### [133] [Strongly-connected percolation on directed lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24975)
*M. E. J. Newman,P. Grassberger,R. M. Ziff*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向键渗流模型中强连通簇的临界行为，发现在二维正方格点上不同各向同性方向排列下，其临界指数、分形维数等属于与无向渗流截然不同的普适类，但各种有向排列属于同一普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有向键渗流中强连通簇的临界行为及其普适类归属，探究其与无向渗流以及不同方向排列之间的差异或相似性。

Method: 在二维正方格点上构建多种全局各向同性的有向键排列，采用多种算法进行大规模数值模拟，高精度计算临界指数、分形维数、跨越概率和渗流阈值等关键量。

Result: 发现强连通簇的临界行为属于与标准无向渗流不同的普适类；所有各向同性有向键排列均表现出相同的普适类行为。

Conclusion: 有向键渗流中的强连通簇形成了一类新的渗流普适类，且该普适类对具体方向排列不敏感，具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study percolation on lattices with directed bonds, focusing on the behavior of strongly-connected percolation clusters -- clusters in which every site is reachable from every other along a directed path. We consider the two-dimensional square lattice and various globally isotropic arrangements of the directions of the bonds. Performing simulations using a range of algorithmic approaches, we calculate high-precision values for critical exponents, fractal dimensions, crossing probabilities, and percolation thresholds for bond percolation with each bond arrangement. We find that the critical behavior is in a distinctly different universality class from that of traditional undirected percolation, but that all bond arrangements appear to fall in the same universality class.

</details>


### [134] [Phase Transitions and Order Parameters in Correlation Matrices: A Wishart-Ensemble Perspective on the Largest Eigenvalue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25030)
*Roberto da Silva,Antonio Mihara,Henrique Tramontina,Sandra D. Prado*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了Wishart系综中相关矩阵的最大特征值，提出将其作为检测混沌和自旋系统中相变的有效经验序参量，并类比其导数与热力学响应函数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种新的经验序参量来检测混沌和自旋系统中的相变行为，利用随机矩阵理论中的最大特征值作为工具。

Method: 基于Wishart系综框架，研究相关矩阵最大特征值的性质，将其导数与自由能导出的热力学响应函数进行类比分析。

Result: 发现最大特征值可作为检测相变的有效经验序参量，其导数行为与热力学响应函数具有相似性，但不一定关联于相变理论中原始的临界发散行为。

Conclusion: 最大特征值提供了一种新的视角来表征复杂系统中的相变，拓展了随机矩阵理论在统计物理中的应用。

Abstract: We investigate the properties of the largest eigenvalue of correlation matrices within the framework of Wishart ensembles. In this work, we propose the largest eigenvalue as an effective empirical order parameter for detecting phase transitions in chaotic and spin systems, drawing an analogy between its derivatives and thermodynamic response functions derived from the free energy, however not necessarily linked to a critical divergence originally observed in the context of phase transitions theory.

</details>


### [135] [Logarithmic Aging Diffusion from a Multiplicative Event Clock: Rare Event Statistics, Ultraslow Transport, and Ensemble-Time Inequivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25374)
*Chunyan Li,Zheng Li,Yueyan Li,Haiwen Liu,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了一种通过迭代年龄条件前向递推律定义的对数老化过程，揭示了事件时间具有乘法结构，事件对数比值为独立同分布的非指数随机变量。该过程产生了对数漂移与展宽、爱因斯坦关系、超慢输运及靶标存活定律，并区分了轨迹可重复性与系综-时间等价性。作者强调，验证该时钟模型需要联合检验多种观测量而非单一对数曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多老化材料中观察到对数时间依赖关系，但现有的ln t松弛律或1/t事件率无法唯一确定背后的随机机制。作者旨在构建并分析一个具体的对数老化过程，提供更严格的检验标准。

Method: 定义年龄条件化的前向递推律（age-conditioned forward-recurrence law）在每次事件后迭代，使事件时间呈乘法结构：对数比值U_n = ln(T_{n+1}/T_n)为独立同分布的非指数随机变量。通过分析事件计数、事件时间密度、输运性质、首次通过时间等统计量，并与扩散极限下的广义Fokker-Planck方程、随机时钟从属表示及广义Langevin闭合进行对比。

Result: 事件计数均值和方差随ln(t/t_0)线性增长；第n次事件时间密度具有对数正态中心区域和固定n的代数远尾；产生对数漂移与展宽、局部细致平衡下的爱因斯坦关系、超慢输运和靶标存活定律；时间平均均方位移的相对离散度按1/ln(T/t_0)衰减，但其均值不收敛于系综滞后MSD。

Conclusion: 该对数时钟模型不能仅通过单一对数曲线验证，而需要通过乘法因子、计数统计、输运、首次通过和有限窗口观测量的一致联合检验来确认。

Abstract: Logarithmic time dependences occur in many aging materials, but neither a $\ln t$ relaxation law nor a $1/t$ event rate uniquely identifies the underlying stochastic mechanism. We examine a specific log-aging process defined by iterating the age-conditioned forward-recurrence law after every event. This rule makes the event times multiplicative: the logarithmic ratios $U_n=\ln(T_{n+1}/T_n)$ are independent and identically distributed with an explicit non-exponential density. Consequently, both the mean and the variance of the event count grow linearly with $\ln(t/t_0)$, while the density of the $n$th event time has a log-normal central sector and a fixed-$n$ algebraic far tail. These clock statistics generate logarithmic drift and spreading, an Einstein relation under local detailed balance, and ultraslow transit and target-survival laws. They also separate trajectory reproducibility from ensemble--time equivalence: the relative scatter of the time-averaged mean-square displacement decays as $1/\ln(T/t_0)$, although its mean does not converge to the ensemble lag MSD. We distinguish the exact event-level construction from its diffusion-limit generalized Fokker--Planck and random-clock subordination representations, and from a generalized-Langevin closure that can match selected responses and covariances but need not reproduce event counts or rare-duration statistics. The proposed clock is therefore tested not by a single logarithmic curve, but by the joint, no-refitting consistency of multiplier, count, transport, first-passage, and finite-window observables.

</details>


### [136] [Don't truncate, decompose: mean-field dynamics of long-range quantum systems from strongly correlated states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25434)
*Federico Carollo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文挑战了平均场理论无法描述长程开放量子系统的普遍共识，证明在强长程区域，系统演化可分解为独立平均场动力学并精确重构，从而恢复了平均场理论的精确性，并首次识别了累积量展开可精确预测低阶累积量的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献普遍认为平均场理论在描述存在对称性破缺或从强关联态（如宏观叠加态）出发的长程开放量子系统时失效，转而依赖累积量展开方法，但该方法的截断缺乏明确依据。本文旨在证明平均场理论在强长程区域实际上是精确的，且累积量展开在概念上是冗余的。

Method: 证明系统演化可以分解为并完全重构自独立的平均场动力学；该分解生成了整个累积量层级，并首次识别出累积量展开能够精确预测低阶累积量的参数区域。

Result: 在非平衡Z₂对称性破缺中计算了矩生成函数，在时间晶体中构建了恢复时间平移对称性的态。在这两个应用中，该方法完全重现了精确的多体动力学，而这是累积量展开无法做到的。

Conclusion: 恢复了平均场理论的精确性，为大规模开放量子系统提供了一个透明的框架，表明在强长程区域平均场理论不仅适用，而且优于累积量展开方法。

Abstract: We challenge the widespread consensus that mean-field theory fails to describe long-range open quantum systems in the presence of symmetry breaking and/or when starting from strongly correlated states (e.g., macroscopic superpositions). While recent literature relies on cumulant expansions to capture such systems, this approach rests on truncations with no clear justification. Here, we show that it is, at best, conceptually redundant in the strong long-range regime. We show that the evolution can be decomposed into, and fully reconstructed from, independent mean-field dynamics. This decomposition generates the entire hierarchy of cumulants and, as a byproduct, identifies---to our knowledge, for the first time---a regime in which cumulant expansions exactly predict low-order cumulants. We illustrate the power of our findings with two applications: we compute the moment generating function for nonequilibrium $\mathcal{Z}_2$ symmetry breaking, and construct states restoring time-translation symmetry in time crystals. In both cases, our method fully reproduces the exact many-body dynamics, which is out of reach of cumulant expansions. Our results reclaim the exactness of mean-field theory, offering a transparent framework for large-scale open quantum systems.

</details>


### [137] [Entanglement asymmetry in the gapped XYZ spin-$\frac12$ chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25625)
*Felipe Taha Sant'Ana*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过结合荷矩恒等式、非守恒求和规则和Baxter-Johnson-Krinsky-McCoy解，计算了相互作用XYZ链在带隙、U(1)破缺相中大量子区间的Rényi纠缠不对称性，并通过无限系统密度矩阵重正化群模拟验证了主公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平衡态下，纠缠不对称性的解析结果仅限于自由理论和临界XXZ链的微扰情况，需要扩展到相互作用系统中U(1)破缺相的非微扰分析。

Method: 结合三种方法：(1) 荷矩恒等式——对费米子高斯态和内射矩阵乘积基态证明不对称性与破缺荷的静态磁化率相关；(2) 非守恒求和规则——通过sine-Gordon形状因子评估磁化率，双扭结和单呼吸子通道提供普适振幅下界；(3) Baxter-Johnson-Krinsky-McCoy解——给出不同耦合下的扭结质量。最后用无限系统DMRG模拟验证。

Result: 给出了XYZ链在U(1)破缺相中Rényi纠缠不对称性的解析结果，并通过数值模拟复现了主公式。

Conclusion: 本文建立的框架为强关联系统中对称性破缺的非平衡量子信息测量提供了可解析计算的方法，并验证了其与数值模拟的一致性。

Abstract: The entanglement asymmetry measures how strongly a symmetry is broken inside a subsystem. Analytic results at equilibrium have so far covered free theories and, perturbatively, the critical XXZ chain. We compute the Rényi entanglement asymmetries of a large interval in the gapped, $U(1)$-breaking phase of the interacting XYZ chain. The calculation combines three ingredients. A charged-moment identity, which we prove for fermionic Gaussian and for injective matrix-product ground states, ties the asymmetry to the static susceptibility of the broken charge. A non-conservation sum rule then evaluates the susceptibility from sine-Gordon form factors, its two-kink and one-breather channels providing a lower bound on the universal amplitude. The Baxter--Johnson--Krinsky--McCoy solution supplies the kink mass for different couplings. Infinite-system density-matrix renormalization group simulations built on these masses reproduce the master formula.

</details>


### [138] [Solvable Quantum Circuits with non-Markovian Influence Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25969)
*Samuel H. Pickering,Max McGinley,Bhavik Kumar,Bruno Bertini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，用于生成具有复杂动力学的量子电路，其影响矩阵可以精确写出，且表现出非马尔可夫性（非平凡的时间关联），基于 dressed 自由费米子（matchgate）电路构建，产生的关联模式与典型多体系统高度相似，并可解释为纠错方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影响矩阵编码了扩展量子多体系统中局部子系统受其余部分作用的信息，有助于高效模拟局部动力学。现有框架（如双幺正电路）产生的影响矩阵是马尔可夫的，缺乏非平凡时间关联，因此需要一种能生成非马尔可夫影响矩阵的新方法，以更接近真实多体系统的行为。

Method: 基于 dressed 自由费米子（matchgate）电路，通过适当选择的相互作用项进行修饰，显式构造了一族广义可解电路，使其影响矩阵可精确写出且具有非马尔可夫性。

Result: 与以往可解实例（如双幺正电路）不同，这些电路产生的关联模式与典型多体系统高度相似。该方法可直接解释为纠错方案，其中破坏影响矩阵可解性的项扮演误差的角色。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种系统生成具有非马尔可夫影响矩阵的量子电路的方法，更接近真实多体系统的动力学行为，并为量子模拟和纠错提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Influence matrices encode the action exerted on local subsystems by the rest of an extended quantum many-body system during their evolution. Thus, knowledge of the influence matrix facilitates computationally efficient simulations of local dynamics. Here we propose a new systematic approach to generating quantum circuits with complex dynamics for which the influence matrices can be written down exactly. In contrast to previous frameworks of this kind, such as dual-unitary circuits, the resulting influence matrices are non-Markovian, exhibiting nontrivial temporal correlations. We explicitly construct a broad family of circuits of this kind, based on dressing free-fermion (matchgate) circuits with appropriately chosen interaction terms. We show that, contrary to previous solvable instances, these circuits produce patterns of correlations that closely resemble that of typical many-body systems. Our approach can be directly interpreted in terms of an error correction scheme where the terms breaking the solvability of the influence matrices play the role of errors.

</details>


### [139] [Gravity-controlled non-equilibrium Casimir pressure in a binary liquid mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25697)
*Marcin Piotr Pruszczyk,Roberto Cerbino,Andrea Gambassi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了等温二元液体混合物在恒定浓度梯度和重力场共同作用下，处于非平衡态时受限于平板间的Casimir压力。利用涨落流体动力学，发现该有限尺寸过剩压力由无量纲变量k_RO L标度，大间距时呈1/(k_RO L)衰减，力可为吸引或排斥，完全源于非平衡态，并提出了胶体悬浮液中的实验测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索远离平衡态和临界点的二元液体混合物中，由浓度梯度和重力协同作用产生的非平衡Casimir压力的物理机制与标度行为。

Method: 使用涨落流体动力学理论分析，引入重力诱导的roll-off波矢k_RO，推导有限尺寸过剩压力的标度函数，并基于胶体悬浮液扩散体系估计实验可行性。

Result: 非平衡Casimir压力由k_RO L标度，大分离时按1/(k_RO L)衰减；力可为吸引或排斥，理想溶液中为零；该力完全源于非平衡态，无浓度梯度时消失。

Conclusion: 非平衡Casimir压力在远离临界点的混合物中由浓度梯度和重力共同产生，具有可调符号（吸引/排斥）和明确的标度律，可通过光学陷阱胶体颗粒实验测量。

Abstract: We investigate the non-equilibrium Casimir pressure in an isothermal binary liquid mixture maintained in a spatially constant and stationary concentration gradient parallel to gravity and confined within a three-dimensional slab of thickness $L$, bounded by two infinite plates parallel to both the gravitational field and the imposed gradient. We assume that the liquid mixture, under the same non-equilibrium conditions, occupies both the interior and the exterior of the slab. Using fluctuating hydrodynamics, we show that the resulting finite-size excess pressure on the plates is described by a scaling function of the dimensionless variable $k_{\mathrm{RO}}L$, where $k_{\mathrm{RO}}$ is the gravity-induced roll-off wavevector. At large separations, this Casimir pressure decays as $1/(k_{\mathrm{RO}}L)$. Depending on the thermodynamic properties of the mixture, the corresponding force can be either attractive or repulsive, while it vanishes for ideal solutions. Since the mixture is assumed to be far from its consolute critical point, the Casimir pressure investigated here is entirely of non-equilibrium origin and it vanishes in the absence of the imposed concentration gradient. Finally, we propose an experimental setup where this force might be measured, consisting of two optically trapped colloidal particles immersed in a dense aqueous colloidal suspension diffusing into an overlying layer of pure water, estimating the expected magnitude of the resulting force.

</details>


### [140] [Macroscopic wall pressure and microscopic contact load in crowds without egress: social-group cohesion and boundary buffering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25780)
*Bo-Shiun Shen,Son-Hsien Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了密集场所无疏散情况下的机械危险，通过弹性重定向模型(ERM)和社会力模型(SFM)的耦合动力学，揭示了群体凝聚力与墙壁缓冲对碰撞风险的影响，发现了由独立智能体和群体智能体诱导的两种相变边界，为高密度场所的 crowd 风险缓解提供了机制性指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 crowd 安全研究主要关注疏散性能或碰撞前规避，但密集聚集且无疏散条件下的直接机械危险（如碰撞冲量、壁面压力）尚未被充分理解。

Method: 研究结合 Elastic Reorientation Model (ERM) 和 Social Force Model (SFM) 的耦合动力学，引入社会群体凝聚力（γ_g）和墙壁缓冲（γ_w）两个参数表征碰撞后行为，使用宏观壁面线压力（P_wall）和微观最大单智能体碰撞冲量（δp_max）量化风险，并通过有限尺寸标度分析相变行为。

Result: ERM中凝聚力和缓冲通过将智能体保留在 bulk 中降低 P_wall，但大群体在中等凝聚力下出现高 δp_max 危险窗口；γ_g→1 时局部配对抑制簇生长、降低 δp_max；SFM推动和滑动放大 δp_max，主动驱动通过近壁积累提高 P_wall。耦合动力学产生 P-p 权衡。有限尺寸标度揭示 γ_w=0.5 处独立智能体诱导的相边界（磁化率不连续）以及 (1-γ_w)(1-γ_g)=0.5 线段上群体诱导的连续相边界（磁化率发散并终止于临界点），两者均需 ERM+SFM 耦合才出现。

Conclusion: 结果为无疏散出口的高密度场所的 crowd 风险缓解和安全规划提供了机制性指导。

Abstract: Crowd safety in confined venues is usually evaluated through evacuation performance or pre-collision avoidance, while direct mechanical hazards in dense gatherings without egress remain poorly understood. We study an Elastic Reorientation Model (ERM), a Social Force Model (SFM), and their coupled dynamics. Post-collision behavior is represented by social-group cohesion ($γ_g$) and wall buffering ($γ_w$), while risk is quantified by the macroscopic wall line pressure ($P_{\text{wall}}$) and the microscopic maximum per-agent collision impulse ($δp_{\text{max}}$). In the ERM, cohesion and wall buffering generally reduce $P_{\text{wall}}$ by retaining agents in the bulk, but large groups exhibit a high-$δp_{\text{max}}$ hazard window at intermediate cohesion. As $γ_g\rightarrow1$, local pairing suppresses cluster growth and shifts kinetic energy from relative to center-of-mass motion, reducing $δp_{\text{max}}$. SFM pushing and sliding amplify $δp_{\text{max}}$, especially when agent-agent and agent-wall interactions coexist, while active driving raises $P_{\text{wall}}$ through near-wall accumulation. The coupled dynamics produces a wall-pressure/contact-load ($P$-$p$) trade-off. Finite-size scaling reveals an independent-agent-induced phase boundary at $γ_w=0.5$, characterized by a susceptibility discontinuity, and a grouped-agent-induced continuous phase boundary along a finite segment of $(1-γ_w)(1-γ_g)=0.5$, characterized by divergent susceptibility and terminating at a critical point. Both disappear in the social-force-free ERM, showing that they emerge from the coupled ERM+SFM dynamics. These results provide mechanistic guidance for crowd-risk mitigation and safety planning in high-density venues without egress.

</details>


### [141] [Fluctuation-dissipation violations in mean-field non-reciprocal spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25782)
*Ot Garcés,Demian Levis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了非互易不对称耦合的球形Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型的非平衡动力学，推导了动力学平均场方程存在稳定时间平移不变解的条件，解析求解了对称、非关联和反对称极限下的关联与响应函数渐近行为，发现非互易性导致涨落-耗散定理普遍破坏（源于细致平衡破缺而非老化），数值结果揭示不对称性增加使动力学加快，反对称耦合驱动振荡行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非互易无序系统的非平衡动力学，区分涨落-耗散定理破坏的两种不同起源（细致平衡破缺与老化）。

Method: 对球形Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型进行非平衡动力学分析，推导动力学平均场方程的时间平移不变解稳定条件，解析求解对称、非关联和反对称三种极限情形，结合数值模拟研究一般非对称情况。

Result: 非互易性导致涨落-耗散定理普遍破坏，即使系统指数弛豫也会发生，源于细致平衡破缺而非老化；不对称性增强使动力学加速，反对称耦合诱发振荡行为。

Conclusion: 为非互易无序系统的动力学提供了参考框架，厘清了涨落-耗散定理破坏的两种不同机制。

Abstract: We study the out-of-equilibrium dynamics of the spherical Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model with non-reciprocal asymmetric couplings. Rather than assuming stationarity, we derive the conditions under which the dynamical mean-field equations admit stable time-translational invariant solutions. We analytically solve the asymptotics of the correlation and response functions in the symmetric, uncorrelated and antisymmetric limits, showing that the fluctuation-dissipation theorem is generically violated in the presence of non-reciprocity despite exponential relaxation, due to broken detailed balance rather than aging. Numerical results for generic asymmetry allow us to interpolate between these solvable limit cases, revealing faster dynamics as the asymmetry increases, together with oscillatory dynamics driven by antisymmetric couplings. These results provide a reference framework for understanding the dynamics of disordered, non-reciprocal systems, disentangling two distinct origins of fluctuation-dissipation violations.

</details>


### [142] [Non-monotonic diffusion from nonequilibrium driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25902)
*Manish Patel,Ritwick Sarkar,Urna Basu,Debasish Chaudhuri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的理论框架，用于描述具有互易和非互易耦合的相互作用粒子系统，涵盖主动物质的主要类别（包括run-and-tumble、active Brownian和active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck粒子）。以被动粒子被主动粒子驱动为最小示例，作者解析证明驱动粒子在长时下始终是扩散的，并发现有效扩散系数随驱动活性呈非单调变化（既增强又抑制输运），该现象在主动驱动和被动非平衡驱动（局部高温）系统中均存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远离平衡态的相互作用粒子随机动力学仍是统计物理中的基本挑战。非互易相互作用在驱动和活性系统中普遍存在，但不同于互易相互作用可以简化为有效单体描述，非互易相互作用无法进行这种简化，因此亟需一个统一的理论框架来理解此类系统的输运行为。

Method: 发展了适用于互易和非互易耦合的相互作用粒子统一理论框架，涵盖主动物质的主要类别（run-and-tumble、active Brownian、active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck粒子）。以周期性环上一个被动粒子被一个主动粒子驱动为最小模型，通过解析分析和数值模拟研究其输运性质。

Result: 解析证明被动驱动粒子在长时下始终表现为扩散行为，与微观驱动机制无关。有效扩散系数随驱动活性呈非单调变化，既可能出现增强输运也可能出现抑制输运。相同行为在一个平衡系统（通过耦合驱动粒子到更高局部温度而驱动出平衡）中也被观察到。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论框架能够定量刻画主动驱动和被动非平衡驱动两种系统，识别出非单调输运的共同机制，建立了主动和被动非平衡驱动下输运的统一描述。

Abstract: The stochastic dynamics of interacting particles far from equilibrium remains a fundamental challenge in statistical physics. While reciprocal interactions often permit effective one-body descriptions, such reductions generally fail for nonreciprocal interactions, which are ubiquitous in driven and active systems. We develop a unified theoretical framework for interacting particles with reciprocal and nonreciprocal couplings, applicable to the principal classes of active matter, including run-and-tumble, active Brownian, and active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck particles. As a minimal example, we study a passive particle driven by an active particle. On a periodic ring, we show analytically that the driven particle is always diffusive at long times, independent of the microscopic driving mechanism. Analytical predictions and numerical simulations reveal a nonmonotonic dependence of the effective diffusivity on the driving activity, giving rise to both enhanced and suppressed transport. Remarkably, the same behavior occurs in an equilibrium system driven out of equilibrium by coupling the driving particle to a higher local temperature. Our framework quantitatively captures both systems, identifies the common mechanism underlying the nonmonotonic transport, and establishes a unified description of transport under active and passive nonequilibrium driving.

</details>


### [143] [Interacting hydrodynamic modes in spinless fermions with dephasing noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25938)
*Fabian H. L. Essler,Patrik Penc*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究存在退相干噪声的自旋less费米子的非平衡动力学，通过映射到虚隧穿振幅的一维Hubbard模型，分析了四次费米子算符的海德堡图动力学及其流体力学投影，发现平移不变的四次算符存在非零的扩散晚期拖尾，表明流体力学尾不能仅由对称性或粗粒化流体力学推断，还依赖于流体力学本征算符的空间结构和有效尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解存在退相干噪声的开放量子系统中多体算符的流体力学行为，特别是探讨高阶（四次）费米子算符的扩散动力学及其与双线性算符的差异。

Method: 采用多粒子Lindblad主方程框架，通过映射到具有纯虚隧穿振幅的一维可解Hubbard模型，解析分析海森堡绘景中四次费米子算符的动力学，并构造其流体力学投影和扩散本征算符。

Result: 构造了四次费米子算符的扩散本征算符，发现平移不变的四次算符存在非零扩散晚期拖尾，而平移不变的双线性算符则没有；四次本征算符可解释为双线性流体力学模的相互作用对。

Conclusion: 微观算符的流体力学尾不能仅从对称性或粗粒化涨落流体力学推断，还取决于流体力学本征算符的空间结构和有效尺寸。

Abstract: We study the non-equilibrium dynamics of spinless fermions with dephasing noise in the framework of a many-particle Lindblad equation. Using a mapping to the exactly solvable one-dimensional Hubbard model with purely imaginary tunneling amplitude we analyze the Heisenberg-picture dynamics of operators quartic in fermions and determine their hydrodynamic projections. We construct the relevant diffusive eigenoperators explicitly and show that, in the quartic sector, they can be interpreted as interacting pairs of bilinear hydrodynamic modes. As a consequence, translationally invariant quartic operators generically exhibit non-vanishing diffusive late-time tails, unlike translationally invariant bilinears. Our results show that the hydrodynamic tails of microscopic operators cannot in general be inferred from symmetry constraints or coarse-grained fluctuating hydrodynamics alone; they also depend crucially on the spatial structure and effective size of the hydrodynamic eigenoperators.

</details>

### [144] [On the Wave of Klein-Gordon Type: Propagation Equation at a Boundary and Interior Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24839)
*Siwar Ben Said*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究波动方程在半经典测度输运中的可观测性，在系数仅为C^1类且边界条件为∂_νu + i∂_t u = 0的情况下，推导了阻碍可观测性的高频波集中所产生测度满足的输运方程，并在几何控制条件下通过反证法获得了正时间频率解的观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在系数仅为C^1类（双特征曲线存在的极限情况）下，具有特定边界条件的波动方程的可观测性问题，这是向更一般的Lopatinskii型边界条件迈出的第一步。

Method: 利用半经典测度沿广义双特征曲线的输运理论，推导高频波集中所产生测度满足的输运方程，并通过反证法在几何控制条件下建立可观测性。

Result: 推导了在C^1系数和∂_νu + i∂_t u = 0边界条件下，阻碍可观测性的高频波集中测度所满足的输运方程，并在几何控制条件下证明了正时间频率解的观测性。

Conclusion: 在系数仅为C^1类的极限正则性条件下，结合几何控制条件，可以建立波动方程的可观测性结果，为处理更一般的Lopatinskii型边界条件奠定了基础。

Abstract: Understanding the transport of semiclassical measures along generalized bicharacteristics is a key ingredient in the proof of observability for the wave equation. Here, the coefficients are only assumed to be of class $C^1$, which is a limit case for the existence of bicharacteristics. We consider boundary conditions of the form $\partial_νu + i \partial_t u = 0$, as a first step towards more general Lopatinskii-type conditions. We derive the transport equation satisfied by the measure arising from the concentration of high-frequency waves that obstruct observability. Observability of solutions associated with positive time-frequencies is then obtained by contradiction under the geometrical control condition.

</details>


### [145] [No Gelation and Global Existence for a Boltzmann Equation with Regularly Varying Mass-Exchange Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25112)
*Siwei Luo,Jian-Guo Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了无凝胶化机制，用于求解具有质量交换的Boltzmann方程全局解，通过构造凸超线性权函数并利用规则变化的质量交换率，证明了对任意有限物理矩初始条件存在全局积分弱解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有质量交换的Boltzmann方程全局解的存在性问题，克服有限时间内质量逃逸到无穷远的困难，无需假设高阶质量矩。

Method: 构造基于二段铰链的凸超线性权函数；利用规则变化使两种碰撞配置中的符号增量可比；通过质量守恒和γ<1得到衰减因子L^{γ-1}；获得质量截断近似的一致矩边界。

Result: 对任意非负且具有有限物理矩的初始密度，获得了全局积分弱解；排除了有限时间内质量逃逸到无穷远的可能性。

Conclusion: 无凝胶化机制成功建立了Grad截断硬势和规则变化质量交换率下Boltzmann方程的全局适定性。

Abstract: We develop a no-gelation mechanism that yields global solutions to the spatially homogeneous Boltzmann equation with mass exchange for Grad-cutoff hard potentials $B=E^γb(ξ), 0<γ<1$ and regularly varying mass-exchange rates. Without assuming any higher mass moment, we construct a convex superlinear weight assembled from dyadic hinges. Production of the weighted moment by collisions between large particles of comparable mass is absorbed by dissipation through collisions with a uniformly populated reservoir of bounded-mass particles. Regular variation makes the signed increments in these two collision configurations comparable, while mass conservation and $γ<1$ yield the vanishing factor $L^{γ-1}$. This yields a uniform moment bound of the mass-cutoff approximations on every finite time interval and rules out finite-time mass escape to infinity. Consequently, for every nonnegative initial density with finite physical moments, we obtain a global integral weak solution.

</details>


### [146] [Diffusion stabilises time-periodic solutions in conservation laws coupled to a relaxation oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24994)
*Julien Barré,Nils Berglund,Hiroshi Horii*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类耦合快速常微分方程的粘性一维守恒律，证明了在对称初始条件下，小正粘性会选择一个唯一的周期解且该解是线性稳定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解粘性如何从无穷维时间周期解族中筛选出唯一稳定解，揭示慢-快系统的动力学选择机制。

Method: 利用慢-快结构的平均化策略结合谱理论方法进行分析，并通过数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 证明了小正粘性存在时，对称初始条件下系统会收敛到唯一的时间周期解，且该解是线性稳定的。

Conclusion: 粘性在无穷维周期解族中起到选择唯一稳定解的关键作用，平均化方法和谱理论是分析此类问题的有效工具。

Abstract: We study a viscous one-dimensional conservation law, coupled to a fast ordinary differential equation. For a vanishing viscosity, the system has an infinite-dimensional family of time-periodic solutions, corresponding to relaxation oscillations. We show that for symmetric initial conditions, a small positive viscosity selects a unique periodic solution, which we prove to be linearly stable. The proof exploits the slow-fast structure through an averaging strategy, as well as spectral-theoretic methods. The results are illustrated by numerical simulations.

</details>


### [147] [The Navier-Stokes equations with dual-scale hereditary viscosity: supercritical norm inflation and global well-posedness in critical spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25009)
*Bruno de Andrade*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究具有双尺度记忆的不可压缩黏弹性流体方程的柯西问题，建立了L^q-L^p衰减估计，确定了临界Lebesgue阈值p_c，证明了超临界区间的瞬时范数膨胀与本质不适定性，以及在拓扑极限p→∞下利用Bony仿微分算子证明了全局Hadamard适定性，其适定性区域严格宽于可分离小Besov闭包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Navier-Stokes方程无法描述具有异常动量输运的复杂流体行为，特别是双尺度记忆效应（如黏弹性流体）破坏了全局尺度不变性，需要新的伪微分分析框架。现有理论在Besov空间中的适定性边界尚不清晰，特别是高频非可分离拓扑结构下的适定性问题有待解决。

Method: 采用Hörmander类S^{-2}_{1,0}中的伪微分算子分析非局部耗散；利用Bernstein不等式和频率调制双线性流的Fourier空间映射证明超临界区间的本质不适定性；通过Bony仿微分算子中的非对称插值技术处理高-高对流共振级联，证明时间平滑性压倒高频共振。

Result: 建立了L^q-L^p衰减估计；确定了临界阈值p_c = N((1+α_∞)/(1-α_∞))；在1 < p < p_c区间证明了瞬时范数膨胀与本质不适定性；在p→∞极限下，证明了在最大临界Besov空间\dot{B}^{-1}_{∞,∞}中双尺度记忆结构性地阻止了经典流体中的坍缩，建立了全局Hadamard适定性，适定性区域\dot{B}^{-κ}_{∞,∞}严格宽于可分离小Besov闭包\dot{b}^{-κ}_{∞,∞}。

Conclusion: 双尺度记忆效应在超临界Lebesgue空间导致本质不适定性，但在高频主导的Besov拓扑下反而通过时间平滑效应扩展了适定性区域，揭示了非局部耗散与对流非线性之间的精细平衡机制，为黏弹性流体方程的数学理论提供了新的适定性边界刻画。

Abstract: This manuscript investigates the Cauchy problem for an incompressible fluid flow governed by a dual-scale hereditary memory, representing a viscoelastic variant of the classical Navier-Stokes equations that captures anomalous momentum transport. The non-local dissipation breaks exact global scale invariance, dictating a pseudo-differential analysis within the Hörmander class $S^{-2}_{1,0}$ where the spatial gradient induces a fractional temporal penalty. We rigorously establish $L^q-L^p$ decay estimates and identify the critical Lebesgue threshold $p_c = N (\frac{1+α_\infty}{1-α_\infty})$. In the supercritical regime $1 < p < p_c$, we bypass the loss of spatial localization by mapping the frequency-modulated bilinear flow directly into Fourier space; by utilizing Bernstein's inequalities, we prove instantaneous norm inflation at the origin and confirm intrinsic ill-posedness. Conversely, in the topological limit $p \to \infty$, we demonstrate that the dual-scale memory structurally prevents the collapse traditionally observed for classical fluids within the maximal critical Besov space $\dot{B}^{-1}_{\infty, \infty}$. By exploiting an asymmetric interpolation within Bony's para-differential calculus, we prove that the temporal smoothing overpowers the high-high convective resonant cascade. This delicate analytical balance confines ill-posedness to the non-separable high-frequency tail of the Besov topology, thereby establishing global-in-time Hadamard well-posedness for small initial data possessing high-frequency adherence within $\dot{B}^{-κ}_{\infty, \infty}(\mathbb{R}^N)$, a well-posedness regime strictly broader than the separable little Besov closure $\dot{b}^{-κ}_{\infty, \infty}(\mathbb{R}^N)$, where $κ= \frac{1-α_\infty}{1+α_\infty}$.

</details>


### [148] [Homogenization of Time-Discrete Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25206)
*Yunhong Deng,Yuan Gao,Li Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了平坦环面上Fokker-Planck方程在时间离散层面的均质化极限，比较了输运型度量与加权L2度量两种最小移动方案，发现度量选择导致不同的均质化极限和演化方程，其中仅加权L2度量方案能恢复正确的有效动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究时间离散化方案中度量选择对Fokker-Planck方程均质化极限的影响，特别是能否在粗尺度上正确恢复有效动力学行为。

Method: 采用最小移动方案对Fokker-Planck方程进行时间离散化，分别使用输运型度量和加权L2度量，分析两种方案在快速振荡扩散系数下的均质化极限，并比较时间步长趋于零时的极限演化方程。

Result: 度量选择至关重要：两种方案导致不同的均质化极限和不同的极限演化方程；仅加权L2度量方案能正确恢复有效动力学。

Conclusion: 时间离散化中度量选择对多尺度问题的极限行为有本质影响，加权L2度量是保留正确有效动力学的关键。

Abstract: In this work, we study the homogenization limit for Fokker--Planck equations on the flat torus with rapidly oscillating diffusion coefficients at the time-discrete level. We discretize the equation in time using a minimizing movement scheme and compare two choices of metric: a transport-type metric and a weighted L2 metric. We prove that this choice is crucial: the two schemes lead to different homogenization limits as the scaling parameter tends to zero, and consequently to different evolution equations as the time step is sent to zero. In particular, only the scheme based on the weighted L2 metric recovers the correct effective dynamics.

</details>


### [149] [Inviscid Incompressible Limit for Degenerate Compressible Navier-Stokes Equations on Expanding Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25028)
*Tong Tang,Emil Wiedemann,Lu Zhu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维退化可压缩Navier-Stokes方程（粘度依赖于密度）在膨胀域上的无粘极限和低马赫数极限，证明了极限系统为不可压Euler系统。该结果将Feireisl等人关于常粘度Navier-Stokes方程的结果推广到了密度依赖粘度的情形，首次同时建立了密度依赖粘度情形下的无粘和不可压极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Feireisl等人关于常粘度Navier-Stokes方程的无粘和低马赫数极限结果推广到更实际的密度依赖粘度情形，填补该领域在退化粘性系数下的理论空白。

Method: 在膨胀域上同时考虑无粘极限和低马赫数极限，从"ill-prepared"（非准备良好）初始数据出发，利用适当的先验估计和紧性分析方法处理密度依赖粘度带来的退化性困难。

Result: 在三维退化可压缩Navier-Stokes方程中，当同时取无粘极限和低马赫数极限时，极限系统为不可压Euler系统。

Conclusion: 成功将常粘度情形的结果推广到密度依赖粘度情形，首次同时建立了该情形下的无粘和不可压极限，为理解退化粘性可压缩流体的极限行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We simultaneously investigate the inviscid and Low Mach number limits on expanding domains for the $3D$ degenerate compressible Navier-Stokes equations, whose viscosity depends on density. Starting from ill-prepared data, we show the limit system is the incompressible Euler system.
  Our result extends a previous result of Feireisl et al. concerning the constant viscosity Navier-Stokes equations, and is the first to establish the inviscid and incompressible limits at the same time for density-dependent viscosity.

</details>


### [150] [Well-posed homogenized strain-gradient models for linear elastodynamics and elastostatics in arbitrary periodic media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25602)
*Rémi Cornaggia,Marc Bonnet,Giuseppe Rosi,Saad El Ouafa,Nicolas Auffray*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对周期介质中的线性弹性静力学和弹性动力学，发展了适定（well-posed）的应变梯度均质化模型，重点用于弹性波传播。通过二阶周期均质化与Boussinesq技巧，将原本病态的四阶方程转化为具有对称性、正定性和强制性的应变梯度弹性（SGE）模型，并利用Hille-Yosida定理建立瞬态问题的适定性。数值算例验证了模型捕获各向异性和色散效应的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典二阶均质化直接导出的有效算子通常是病态的（ill-posed），无法保证解的存在唯一性和稳定性，因此需要发展一种既能捕获高阶效应（如色散和各向异性）又具有数学适定性的均质化模型。

Method: 采用经典的双尺度渐近展开方法进行二阶周期均质化，利用互易恒等式简化细胞问题求解；引入含可调标量权重的Boussinesq技巧，将四阶PDE重新表述为应变梯度弹性（SGE）模型，确保对称性、正定性和强制性；借助Hille-Yosida定理证明瞬态初值问题和受迫响应问题的适定性。

Result: 推导了有效刚度张量和惯性张量的显式表达式；证明了SGE模型的适定性条件；在正方形、六边形和非中心对称手性晶格三种二维周期胞上，色散关系与Floquet-Bloch参考解吻合良好，瞬态波传播结果准确。

Conclusion: 所发展的应变梯度均质化模型能够在保持数学适定性的前提下，有效捕获经典弹性理论无法描述的色散和各向异性效应，为周期介质的波传播分析提供了可靠的理论框架。

Abstract: This work develops well-posed homogenized strain-gradient models for linear elastostatics and elastodynamics in periodic media, with a primary focus on elastic wave propagation. Using the classical two-scale asymptotic expansion method, we carry out second-order periodic homogenization for media in $\mathbb{R}^d$ ($d = 2, 3$), with no restriction on the periodicity cell geometry or material distribution. Reciprocity identities applied to suitably chosen pairs of cell solutions provide alternative expressions for the effective stiffness and inertia tensors arising at the leading, first and second orders, substantially reducing the number of cell problems that must actually be solved. Since direct two-scale homogenization beyond leading order generically yields ill-posed effective operators, a Boussinesq-trick procedure is introduced, involving a tunable scalar weight, to recast the resulting fourth-order partial differential equation as a valid strain-gradient elasticity (SGE) model possessing the requisite symmetry, sign-definiteness and coercivity properties. These properties are then used, via the Hille-Yosida theorem, to establish the well-posedness of the corresponding transient initial-value and forced-response problems. Several practically relevant special cases are examined, including centrosymmetric cells, homogeneous mass density and homogeneous elasticity, each yielding simplified model structures. Numerical illustrations on three two-dimensional periodicity cells (square, hexagonal and a non-centrosymmetric chiral lattice) compare the resulting dispersion relations against reference Floquet-Bloch computations and assess transient wave propagation, demonstrating the model's capacity to capture anisotropic and dispersive effects beyond classical elasticity while preserving mathematical well-posedness.

</details>


### [151] [Elliptic Regularity Theory in Barron Spaces and Applications to the Deep Ritz Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25100)
*Stephan Wojtowytsch*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了具有Dirichlet边界条件的调和函数在Barron空间中一般既不是Lipschitz连续的，也不属于Sobolev空间$H^2$，因此排除了Barron空间以及更深ReLU网络函数类的正则性。但这类函数可以用低范数的Barron函数以$\sim \varepsilon$精度逼近（半空间和二维矩形域），并由此推导了Deep Ritz神经PDE求解器的先验误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究调和函数在Barron空间（宽单隐层ReLU网络对应的函数类）中的正则性，并探索其近似能力及对神经PDE求解器的理论支撑。

Method: 通过理论分析证明调和函数在Barron空间中的正则性缺失（非Lipschitz、非$H^2$）；利用构造性方法证明在半空间和二维矩形域上可用低范数Barron函数逼近；基于此正则性理论推导Deep Ritz方法的先验误差界。

Result: 调和函数一般不在Barron空间中（非Lipschitz、非$H^2$）；但在简单域上可被低范数Barron函数以$\sim \varepsilon$精度逼近（范数$\sim |\log\varepsilon|$）；获得了Deep Ritz方法的误差估计。

Conclusion: Barron空间对调和函数正则性不足，但近似能力仍可用于神经PDE求解器的理论分析，为深度学习方法求解PDE提供了正则性视角的理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that harmonic functions with Dirichlet boundary data in Barron space, a function class tailored to wide ReLU networks with a single hidden layer and suitably bounded weights, are generally neither Lipschitz continuous nor in the Sobolev class $H^2$. A fortiori, they are not in any function class in which the norm controls the Lipschitz constant, which rules out not only Barron space regularity, but also regularity in function classes for deeper ReLU networks with bounded coefficients. They can, however, be approximated to accuracy $\sim \varepsilon$ by Barron functions of low norm $\sim |\log\varepsilon|$ in various Lebesgue and Sobolev norms (with at most two derivatives). The positive result holds on very simple domains: Half-spaces in arbitrary dimension and rectangular domains in two dimensions. As an application of this regularity theory, we obtain a priori error estimates for Deep Ritz neural PDE solvers.

</details>


### [152] [Long-wave instability of periodic shear flows with constant magnetic field for the 2D resistive MHD equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25836)
*Roberto Feola,Luca Franzoi,Riccardo Montalto,Claudia Peña*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维粘性电阻MHD方程在周期域上围绕剪切流与背景磁场的线性长波稳定性，通过正规形变换和特征值渐近展开，建立了稳定/不稳定的显式条件，并揭示了相空间的指数增长/衰减分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Colombo等人关于Navier-Stokes方程的长波稳定性研究扩展至MHD框架，继承Kolmogorov-Meshalkin-Sinai-Yudovich经典工作精神，探究磁场与剪切流耦合下的线性稳定性机制。

Method: 采用非摄动正规形变换解耦零傅里叶模式与非零模式，结合关于小参数α的特征值渐近展开，分析从零未扰特征值分岔出的特征值行为。

Result: 建立了涉及粘性ν、电阻η、背景磁场分量b₁,b₂及剪切流剖面U(y)的显式条件，刻画了长波(α≪1)线性稳定与不稳定的阈值。

Conclusion: 获得了相空间的不稳定与稳定子空间分解，解在Sobolev范数下呈现指数增长或衰减，为MHD系统的长波动力学提供了严格分析基础。

Abstract: We investigate the long-wave linear stability and instability of the two-dimensional viscous, resistive Magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) equations, in vorticity-current formulation, on the periodic domain ${\mathbb T}_α\times {\mathbb T} = \Big( {\mathbb R}/(\frac{2 π}α {\mathbb Z}) \times {\mathbb R}/(2 π{\mathbb Z}) \Big)$, around a periodic shear flow $(U(y),0)$ coupled with a constant background magnetic field ${\bf b}=({\rm b}_1,{\rm b}_2)$. It is a non-trivial extension of a recent paper for the Navier-Stokes equations by Colombo, Dolce, Montalto & Ventura to the MHD setting in the spirit of the classical works of Kolmogorov, Meshalkin, Sinai and Yudovich. We establish explicit conditions on the shear flow profile $U(y)$ involving the viscosity $ν$, the resistivity $η$ and the components of the background magnetic field ${\bf b}$ to obtain linear long-wave stability and instability in the regime $α\ll 1$. The proof combines a non-perturbative normal form transformation decoupling the zero Fourier mode from the non-zero modes with sharp asymptotic expansions of the eigenvalues bifurcating from the zero unperturbed eigenvalue with respect to the parameter $α$. As a dynamical consequence, we obtain a splitting of the phase space into unstable and stable subspaces, on which solutions grow or decay exponentially in Sobolev norm.

</details>


### [153] [Propagation-Window Bounds in Degenerate Plant-Consumer Reaction-Diffusion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25149)
*Chiun-Chuan Chen,Ting-Yang Hsiao,Li-Chang Hung,Haoyuan Li,Shun-Chieh Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类高维植物-消费者反应扩散系统中连接灭绝态与共存态的严格正行波的速度问题。在弱相互作用条件下，系统存在唯一正共存平衡态。定义了植物速度上界s_P、消费者存在性速度阈值s_C^{ex}和必要性速度阈值s_C^{nec}。证明当s_P < s_C^{ex}时，速度集S满足(s_P, s_C^{ex}) ⊆ S ⊆ [s_P, s_C^{nec})。通过构造混合下解（多项式-指数前沿凸起+逻辑斯蒂桥接的植物轮廓，高斯前沿+常数后沿的消费者轮廓）证明存在性；利用Sturm振荡论证证明s_P以下无非平凡波；通过前沿渐近分析和Riccati交叉论证证明s_C^{nec}为普适上界。两物种系统中构造显式波速s_{exact}，证明s_C^{ex}仅为构造性阈值而非内禀最大波速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维植物-消费者系统中多个物种共存时的行波问题具有理论和生态意义。经典两物种理论基于扩散分离和辅助兼容性假设，难以推广至多物种情形。本文旨在去除这些假设，建立更普适的存在性与非存在性判据，并澄清速度阈值的本质。

Method: (1) 提出混合下解构造方法：植物下轮廓为多项式-指数前沿凸起与逻辑斯蒂函数桥接；消费者下轮廓在波前为高斯型、波后为常数。(2) 利用Sturm型振荡论证证明s_P以下无波。(3) 利用前沿渐近展开与Riccati方程穿越论证推导上界s_C^{nec}。(4) 在两物种系统中通过显式构造波解验证速度可超越s_C^{ex}。

Result: (1) 当s_P < s_C^{ex}时，(s_P, s_C^{ex}) ⊆ S ⊆ [s_P, s_C^{nec})。(2) s_P以下不存在严格正行波。(3) s_C^{nec}是普适上界。(4) 两物种系统中存在s_{exact}满足s_P < s_C^{ex} < s_{exact} < s_C^{nec}，证明s_C^{ex}只是构造性阈值。

Conclusion: 去除了早期理论中的扩散分离和辅助兼容性假设，建立了多物种植物-消费者系统行波速度的更普适理论框架；揭示了s_C^{ex}与s_C^{nec}之间的速度间隙，表明前者仅为构造方法导致的阈值而非系统的内在限制。

Abstract: We study traveling waves in a class of high-dimensional plant-consumer reaction-diffusion systems with $N$ competing plant species and $N$ associated consumer populations. The consumer equations are linearly degenerate at the extinction state. Under the weak-interaction condition $\|K\|_{\infty}<1$, the system admits a unique positive coexistence equilibrium. Define $ s*{\mathrm P}:=\max_{1\le i\le N}2\sqrt{d_i r_i},\, s_{\mathrm C}^{\mathrm{ex}}:=\min_{1\le i\le N}2\sqrt{D_i e_iσ_i},\, s_{\mathrm C}^{\mathrm{nec}}:=\min_{1\le i\le N}2\sqrt{D_i e_i}$, where $σ_i:=1-\sum_{j=1}^Nκ_{ij}$, and let $\mathcal S$ be the set of speeds admitting a strictly positive traveling wave connecting extinction to coexistence. If $s_{\mathrm P}<s_{\mathrm C}^{\mathrm{ex}}$, we prove $ (s_{\mathrm P},s_{\mathrm C}^{\mathrm{ex}})\subseteq \mathcal S \subseteq [s_{\mathrm P},s_{\mathrm C}^{\mathrm{nec}})$. The existence result is based on a new hybrid lower-solution construction that removes the diffusion-separation and auxiliary compatibility assumptions from the earlier four-component theory. Plant lower profiles combine a polynomial-exponential leading-edge bump with a logistic bridge, while consumer lower profiles are Gaussian near the leading edge and constant behind the front. Nonexistence below $s_{\mathrm P}$ follows from a Sturm-type oscillation argument, whereas leading-edge asymptotics and a Riccati crossing argument yield the universal upper obstruction $s<s_{\mathrm C}^{\mathrm{nec}}$. Finally, for a fixed two-species system, we construct an explicit wave with $ s_{\mathrm P}<s_{\mathrm C}^{\mathrm{ex}}<s_{\mathrm{exact}}<s_{\mathrm C}^{\mathrm{nec}}$, showing that $s_{\mathrm C}^{\mathrm{ex}}$ is only a constructive threshold, not an intrinsic maximal wave speed.

</details>


### [154] [Many-point tropical relaxation and the Monge--Ampère equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25878)
*Nikita Kalinin,Ernesto Lupercio,Higinio Serrano,Mikhail Shkolnikov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构建了平面Aleksandrov Monge-Ampère方程的incidence-driven热带近似。对凸域Ω中N个点的配置，构造零边值非负凹热带级数F_N。当经验测度收敛到μ时，N^{-1/2}F_N一致收敛到Aleksandrov解F_{μ,Ω}，并给出O(N^{-1/2})的曲率差异估计。对有理多边形，热带曲线恰有N个有界胞腔，标记对偶边构成生成树。证明结合了热带插值、半线性标记拓扑、Euler-Pick曲率公式、最小系数形变、切向余面积和加权Crofton估计。还获得了随机点云的几乎必然极限、连续解的完全仿射协变性和依赖构形的Abelian沙堆对角线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立离散（热带）逼近连续Monge-Ampère方程的一般框架，在无正则性或严格凸性假设下，通过incidence-driven的热带构造实现弱收敛，并给出定量误差估计。

Method: 热带插值、半线性标记拓扑、Euler-Pick曲率公式、最小系数形变、切向余面积、加权Crofton估计、有理多边形穷竭。

Result: 1) 普遍通用配置下N^{-1/2}F_N一致收敛到连续解；2) 紧集上O(N^{-1/2})的Lipschitz型曲率差异估计；3) 有理多边形上热带曲线恰有N个有界胞腔，对偶边为生成树；4) 全局弱曲率收敛及Monge-Ampère质量恒等式。

Conclusion: 该热带近似框架有效连接了离散组合与连续几何，在无正则性假设下实现了Monge-Ampère方程的逼近，并推广到随机点云和仿射协变情形。

Abstract: We construct an incidence-driven tropical approximation of the planar Aleksandrov Monge--Ampère equation. Let $Ω\subset\mathbb R^2$ be a bounded open convex domain, let $K\SubsetΩ$, and let $F_N=G_{P_N}0_Ω$ be the minimal nonnegative concave tropical series with integral slopes, zero boundary values, and corner locus containing an $N$-point set $P_N\subset K$. For universally generic configurations whose empirical measures converge to $μ$, $N^{-1/2}F_N\longrightarrow F_{μ,Ω}$ uniformly on $\overlineΩ$, where $F_{μ,Ω}$ is the unique continuous concave Aleksandrov solution of $\mathrm{MA}(F)=μ$ with zero boundary values. On every compact $L\SubsetΩ$ we prove an $O(N^{-1/2})$ bounded-Lipschitz-type estimate for the curvature discrepancy. No regularity or strict-convexity assumption is imposed on $\partialΩ$.
  For rational polygons, strong genericity holds on an open dense full-measure locus. The tropical curve has exactly $N$ bounded cells, the marked dual edges form a spanning tree, and every compact internal edge has weight one. These finite statements yield global weak curvature convergence and the exact identity $\mathrm{MA}(F_N)(Ω^\circ)=N-1+\frac{1}{2}D_{\mathrm{term}}(F_N)$, with $D_{\mathrm{term}}(F_N)=O(\sqrt N)$. The proof combines tropical interpolation, semilinear marked topology, an Euler--Pick curvature formula, minimal coefficient deformations, tangential coarea, and a weighted Crofton estimate uniform over rational polygonal exhaustions. We also obtain almost-sure limits for random point clouds, full affine covariance of the continuum solution, and a configuration-dependent Abelian-sandpile diagonal.

</details>


### [155] [Turbulent Flame Speed Can Increase under Curvature Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25185)
*Hung V. Tran,Jack Xin,Yifeng Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 论文发现一个三维周期性剪切流中，Markstein曲率扩散反而提升了湍流火焰速度，这与二维剪切流中曲率平滑总是降低火焰速度的严格单调性结论相反，揭示了一种多维机制：局部曲率平滑可以增强大尺度火焰传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲率效应对湍流火焰速度的影响，特别是检验在三维流动中曲率平滑是否总是降低火焰传播速度这一普遍预期。

Method: 构造一个光滑的三维周期剪切流，在水平集G方程中引入Markstein曲率扩散项，计算有效火焰速度并与二维情形进行对比分析。

Result: 在该三维剪切流中，曲率扩散反而增加了有效火焰速度，这构成了该模型下曲率平滑导致速度降低这一单调性规律的第一个反例。

Conclusion: 存在一种真正的多维机制，使得局部曲率平滑可以增强而非抑制大尺度前沿传播，这与二维情形下的严格单调性结论形成对比。

Abstract: Curvature effects are expected to smooth flame-front wrinkles and thereby reduce turbulent flame speed. We construct a smooth three-dimensional periodic shear flow for which introducing Markstein curvature diffusivity instead increases the effective flame speed predicted by the level-set G-equation. This gives the first counterexample, within this model, to monotone slowdown under curvature smoothing and contrasts with the rigorous monotonicity result for two-dimensional shear flows. The example reveals a genuinely multidimensional mechanism in which local curvature smoothing can enhance, rather than suppress, large-scale front propagation.

</details>


### [156] [The Barron-Lipschitz Energy Gap and Depth Separation Phenomena in Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25905)
*Nima Rezaei,Stephan Wojtowytsch*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过多个例子说明，即使无限宽度的神经网络（Barron函数）在变分法问题中作为模型类也可能遇到重大障碍。一个实际相关的例子涉及薄弹性壳的弯曲、拉伸和折叠，其中能量可通过沿圆形线折叠降低，但神经网络只能描述沿整条直线的折叠。相反，对于一大类一阶积分泛函，Barron函数和Lipschitz函数能实现的能量之间没有差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无限宽度神经网络（Barron函数）在变分法问题中作为模型类时的局限性，以及其与Lipschitz函数在能量实现能力上的差距。

Method: 通过多个具体算例分析，特别是薄弹性壳边界条件下的弯曲、拉伸和折叠问题，对比Barron函数和Lipschitz函数在不同类型泛函中的表现。

Result: 对于弹性壳折叠问题，Barron函数只能描述沿直线的折叠，无法实现沿圆形线的折叠，存在显著障碍。但对于一大类一阶积分泛函，Barron函数与Lipschitz函数之间没有能量差距。

Conclusion: 无限宽度神经网络（Barron函数）在变分法问题中存在结构性限制，特别是在需要描述曲线型折叠的问题中；但在某些一阶积分泛函类中，其表达能力与Lipschitz函数相当。

Abstract: We illustrate in several examples that even neural networks of infinite width (specifically, Barron functions) may encounter substantial obstacles when used as a model class for problems in the calculus of variations. An instance of practical relevance concerns the bending, stretching and folding of a thin elastic shell with anchored or clamped boundary conditions where elastic energy could be reduced by folding along a circular line, but the neural networks can only describe straight folds along entire lines. Conversely, we show that there is no gap between the energy that Barron functions and Lipschitz functions can achieve for a large class of integral first-order functionals.

</details>


### [157] [An indefinite Coulomb interaction from the Steklov spectrum of perforated manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25211)
*Zhonggan Huang,Raghavendra Venkatraman*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了Steklov特征值及其调和延拓特征函数在闭流形上被许多小测地球穿孔后的最优收敛率，并推导了Steklov特征值的高阶展开（在二维和三维中识别出两个修正尺度），该展开由边界与体测度之间的不定Coulomb型能量控制，通过极限算子的约化格林函数介导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立穿孔流形上Steklov特征值向加权Laplace-Beltrami特征值收敛的最优速率，并推导其高阶渐近展开，从而桥接谱几何与通过Coulomb型能量相互作用的点系统理论这两个新兴文献领域。

Method: 引入辅助函数量化孔洞表面测度与其背景密度之间的差异测度；利用这些函数建立尖锐估计；分析临界尺度缩放（孔半径与间距的关系，使每个孔的边界面积与其Voronoi胞元的加权体积平衡）；运用约化格林函数和不定Coulomb型能量进行展开推导。

Result: 建立了Steklov特征值和调和延拓特征函数的最优收敛率；在二维和三维中识别出两个修正尺度的高阶展开；该展开由边界与体测度差异的不定Coulomb型能量控制。

Conclusion: 本文成功桥接了谱几何与点系统Coulomb相互作用理论，为穿孔流形上Steklov问题的渐近分析提供了完整的理论框架和精确展开结果。

Abstract: We establish optimal convergence rates for Steklov eigenvalues and harmonically extended eigenfunctions toward their weighted Laplace--Beltrami counterparts on a closed manifold perforated by many small geodesic balls. The holes have radii that scale critically with respect to their spacing in the sense that the boundary area of each hole balances with the weighted volume of its Voronoi cell. We then derive a higher-order expansion of the Steklov eigenvalues; in dimensions two and three, we identify two correction scales. The expansion is governed by an indefinite Coulomb-type energy of the discrepancy between the boundary and bulk measures, mediated by the reduced Green function of the limiting operator. The proof relies on sharp estimates for certain auxiliary functions that we introduce in order to quantify the discrepancy measure between the surface measure on the holes and their background density. Our paper serves to bridge an emerging literature in spectral geometry with one on systems of points interacting via Coulomb-type energies.

</details>


### [158] [Double phase flow under Lavrentiev phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25296)
*Yoshiki Kaiho*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究双相位泛函关联的抛物型方程，揭示了Lavrentiev现象的动态方面：证明了流会在有限时间内失去光滑可逼近性，并给出了定量估计；同时证明若初始数据正则且泛函非退化，光滑可逼近性可在短时间内保持。前者基于发展变分不等式，后者借助解析半群理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双相位泛函已知存在Lavrentiev现象（奇异极小化子的存在），但该现象通常被视为稳态现象。本文旨在研究相关双相位流如何演化趋向奇异极小化子的动态过程。

Method: 采用发展变分不等式证明有限时间内光滑可逼近性的丧失及其定量估计；利用解析半群理论证明短时内光滑可逼近性的保持。

Result: 揭示了有限时间内光滑可逼近性丧失的现象并给出时间估计；证明了在初始数据正则且泛函关于梯度变量非退化的条件下，光滑可逼近性可在短时间内持续存在。

Conclusion: 将通常被视为稳态现象的Lavrentiev现象拓展到动态情境，揭示了双相位流演化过程中光滑可逼近性的有限时间丧失与短时保持特性。

Abstract: This paper deals with parabolic equations associated with double phase functionals. It is known that double phase functionals may exhibit the Lavrentiev phenomenon, which indicates an existence of a singular minimizer. Our aim is to investigate the process of the associated double phase flow evolving toward the singular minimizer. For this purpose, we study whether solutions can be approximated by smooth functions. We first observe a phenomenon that we call finite-time loss of smooth approximability; more precisely, we prove that the flow eventually ceases to be smoothly approximable. We also establish quantitative estimates on the time of loss. On the other hand, we investigate a phenomenon that we call short-time persistence of smooth approximability; we prove that the smooth approximability persists for a short time provided that the initial datum is regular and that the functional is nondegenerate with respect to the gradient variable. The results concerning finite-time loss of smooth approximability are derived from the evolution variational inequality, whereas the short-time persistence result is obtained by applying analytic semigroup theory. The novelty of this paper lies in studying the dynamical aspect of the Lavrentiev phenomenon, which is usually regarded as a stationary phenomenon.

</details>


### [159] [Semilinear damped wave equation on a compact Lie group with a non-autonomous forcing term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25571)
*Wenhui Chen,Sandra Lucente,Alessandro Palmieri*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧致李群上半线性阻尼波动方程，其中非线性项含非自治因子φ(t)。发现φ的可积性是小初值问题解全局存在与有限时间爆破的判别准则，并在φ满足一致上尺度条件时给出了精确的生命跨度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究时间依赖因子φ(t)如何影响半线性阻尼波动方程局部解的整体可延拓性，建立解爆破与全局存在的判别准则。

Method: 利用φ的Lebesgue可积性作为判别准则，引入一致上尺度条件(Uniform upper scaling condition)描述φ的尺度行为，结合小初值分析技术推导生命跨度估计。

Result: φ∈L¹([0,+∞))时小数据解全局存在；φ∉L¹([0,+∞))且满足一致上尺度条件时解在有限时间内爆破，并给出了精确的生命跨度估计。

Conclusion: 非自治因子φ的求和性（可积性）是决定半线性阻尼波动方程解全局存在或有限时间爆破的关键判据，一致上尺度条件可量化爆破时间。

Abstract: In the present note, we consider a semilinear damped wave equation on a compact Lie group with a non-autonomous nonlinearity $\varphi(t)|u|^p$. We are interested in describing how the nonnegative time-dependent factor $\varphi$ affects the global in time prolongability of a local solution. In particular, the summability of the function $\varphi$ provides a criterion to distinguish between the blow-up in finite time and global existence of small data. Finally, we derive sharp lifespan estimates for local in time solutions when $\varphi\not\in L^1([0,+\infty)))$ and satisfies a certain scaling condition, that we named uniform upper scaling condition.

</details>


### [160] [Global Dynamics of Trait-Structured Generalised Lotka-Volterra Systems with Trait-Independent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25573)
*Manh Hong Duong,Nataliya Balabanova,Blaine van Rensburg*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究了一类选择-突变积分微分Lotka-Volterra系统的长时间动力学，证明了在种群间相互作用的通常假设下，解的长时间行为由广义Lotka-Volterra常微分方程精确决定。在静态环境中最小突变率被选择，而在变化环境中中间突变率可能被选择。关键步骤是证明总种群大小渐近地服从广义Lotka-Volterra方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究连续表型性状依赖适应度的选择-突变积分微分Lotka-Volterra系统的长时间动力学，探究突变率在不同环境下的演化选择。

Method: 通过证明总种群大小渐近服从广义Lotka-Volterra方程，利用渐近自治动力系统的一般结果，分析系统的长时间行为。

Result: 在通常的种群间相互作用假设下，系统解的长时间行为由广义Lotka-Volterra常微分方程精确决定；静态环境中选择最小突变率，变化环境中可能选择中间突变率。

Conclusion: 该选择-突变积分微分系统的长期动力学可简化为广义Lotka-Volterra常微分方程，且突变率的选择取决于环境稳定性。

Abstract: We study the long time dynamics of a selection-mutation integro-differential Lotka-Volterra system of $N$ populations. In our model, fitness depends on a continuous phenotypic trait, but the effect of one population on another is independent of this trait. We establish that, under some usual assumptions on the interactions between populations, the long time behaviour of solutions is exactly determined by the well studied Generalised Lotka-Volterra (ordinary differential) Equation. We also show that, all else being equal, the minimal mutation rate is selected for in a static environment, whereas in an a changing environment, an intermediate mutation rate could be selected instead. The key step in our proofs is establishing that the total population sizes are asymptotically governed by a Generalised Lotka-Volterra Equation, which then allows the use of a general result on asymptotically autonomous dynamical systems.

</details>


### [161] [Finite-mass soliton-type rigidity and four-channel reduction for the three-dimensional nonradial focusing energy-critical nonlinear Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25735)
*Pang-Hung Chung,Dan Han*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 针对三维非径向聚焦能量临界非线性薛定谔方程，研究了基态阈值以下的集中-紧致通道。经过单侧归一化后，最小临界元素分为四类：有限时间、快速级联、有界尺度有限质量和残余准孤子通道。前三个被严格排除。主要结果表明，在没有径向对称性、零动量或慢空间中心的情况下，每个有限质量有界尺度几乎周期解都恒为零。因此，任何低于阈值散射的最小反例必须位于残余准孤子通道中；若其尺度有界，则每个时刻都具有无限质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维非径向聚焦能量临界非线性薛定谔方程在基态阈值以下的集中-紧致通道，旨在理解解的动力学行为并排除非散射的反例。

Method: 采用集中-紧致性分析框架，对最小临界元素进行单侧归一化分类，并通过排除前三类通道来证明主要定理。

Result: 在无径向对称性、零动量或慢空间中心的条件下，任何有限质量有界尺度几乎周期解必为零；低于阈值散射的最小反例只能位于残余准孤子通道，且若尺度有界则每个时刻具有无限质量。

Conclusion: 该结果显著简化了三维非径向能量临界NLS的散射理论，将反例限制在残余准孤子通道，为后续完全解决散射猜想提供了关键突破口。

Abstract: The concentration--compactness channels below the ground-state threshold are investigated for the three-dimensional nonradial focusing energy-critical nonlinear Schrödinger equation. After one-sided normalization, a minimal critical element falls into four classes: the finite-time, rapid-cascade, bounded-scale finite-mass, and residual quasi-soliton channels. The first three classes are rigorously excluded. The main result shows, without radial symmetry, zero momentum, or a slow spatial center, that every finite-mass bounded-scale almost-periodic solution is identically zero. Consequently, any minimal counterexample to below-threshold scattering must lie in the residual quasi-soliton channel; if its scale is bounded, then it has infinite mass at every time.

</details>


### [162] [Blow-up asymptotics for a critical Hartree-type Brézis--Nirenberg problem in dimension three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25759)
*Yue Wu,Minbo Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有临界Hartree项的半线性椭圆问题，在Hebey-Vaugon临界意义下，对优化序列解进行渐近展开，确定了精确爆破率和集中点，并得到了逐点爆破行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解临界Hartree问题中解的爆破现象，特别是当势函数Q在Hebey-Vaugon意义下为临界时，解序列作为Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev不等式优化序列的渐近行为。

Method: 在自然非退化假设下，通过分析Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev不等式优化序列，对解进行精确的渐近展开，推导爆破率并识别集中点。

Result: 得到了解u_ε的精确渐近展开式，确定了精确的爆破速率和集中点位置，并给出了集中点附近及远离集中点的逐点爆破行为描述。

Conclusion: 本文为临界Hartree问题提供了完整的爆破分析，揭示了在临界势函数影响下解序列的精细结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the following critical Hartree problem \begin{equation}\label{equationabstract}
  \begin{cases} \displaystyle-Δu+(Q+\varepsilon V)u= A_{3,μ} \left(\int_Ω\frac{u^{6-μ}(y)}{|x-y|^μ}dy\right) u^{5-μ}
  &\mathrm{~in~}Ω,\\ \displaystyle u>0&\mathrm{~in~}Ω,\\u=0&\mathrm{~on~}\partialΩ,\end{cases} \end{equation} where $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^3$ is a bounded open set, $0<μ<2$, the exponent $6-μ$ is the upper critical exponent in the sense of the Hardy--Littlewood--Sobolev inequality and $A_{3,μ}>0$ is a normalization constant. The function $Q$ is assumed to be critical in the sense of Hebey and Vaugon, and the solutions $u_{\varepsilon}$ of \eqref{equationabstract} are assumed to be an optimizing sequence for the Hardy--Littlewood--Sobolev inequality. Under a natural nondegeneracy assumption, we derive a precise asymptotic expansion of \(u_{\varepsilon}\), determine the exact blow-up rate, and identify the concentration point. We also obtain the pointwise blow-up behavior both near and away from the concentration point.

</details>


### [163] [Bisingular $(ρ,δ)$ pseudodifferential operators on products of compact Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25821)
*G. Drei,S. Federico,A. Parmeggiani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在紧致Lie群乘积$G_1\times G_2$上建立了双奇异拟微分算子的$(ρ,δ)$演算，将此前第二、第三作者在$(1,0)$情形的工作推广到了更适合构造亚椭圆算子参数逆的$(ρ,δ)$情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将双奇异拟微分算子演算从$(1,0)$情形推广到更一般的$(ρ,δ)$情形，以适用于构造乘积紧致Lie群上亚椭圆算子的参数逆。

Method: 在乘积紧致Lie群$G_1\times G_2$上发展$(ρ,δ)$型双奇异拟微分算子演算理论。

Result: 成功建立了$(ρ,δ)$演算，扩展了此前$(1,0)$情形的结果。

Conclusion: 所发展的$(ρ,δ)$演算为构造$G_1\times G_2$上亚椭圆算子的参数逆提供了合适的工具。

Abstract: In this paper we develop the $(ρ,δ)$ calculus of bisingular pseudodifferential operators on the product compact Lie groups, which extends the one that was earlier introduced by the second and third authors in the $(1,0)$ case. The $(ρ,δ)$ case is the calculus suitable to construct parametrices of hypoelliptic operators on $G_1\times G_2$.

</details>


### [164] [Sharp anisotropic $L^2$-Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg inequalities associated with the Minkowski functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25876)
*Zhenzhen Wei*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对一般凸体（不要求原点对称）建立了各向异性的L²-Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg恒等式与不等式框架，通过显式非负余项得到最佳常数和极值函数，并推广了经典欧氏和基于范数的各向异性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的各向异性Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg理论大多假设凸体是原点对称的（从而Minkowski泛函为偶函数），不适用于一般凸体。本文旨在将这一理论推广到仅含原点内点的任意凸体情形，处理非偶Minkowski泛函带来的技术困难。

Method: 通过构造各向异性径向导数R_K(u)的L²-Hardy和L²-Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg恒等式，并给出显式非负余项，进而导出相应不等式；在自然的完备化空间中研究最佳常数的可达性并求解极值函数。

Result: 获得了依赖于参数(a,b)区域的最佳各向异性L²-Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg不等式；推导出尖锐的Heisenberg型不确定性原理和max型各向异性梯度不等式；当K为欧氏单位球时恢复经典结果，当K原点对称时与现有各向异性理论一致。

Conclusion: 本文将sharp L²-Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg理论完整推广到仅含原点内点的一般凸体（Minkowski泛函可非偶）情形，统一并扩展了现有框架。

Abstract: Let $K\subset \RN$ be a convex body containing the origin in its interior, and let $\hK{\cdot}$ be its Minkowski functional. In this paper, we develop an identity-based framework for sharp anisotropic $L^2$-Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg inequalities associated with the anisotropic radial derivative $$ \mathcal R_K(u)(x)=\frac{x\cdot\nabla u(x)}{\hK{x}}, \quad x\in\RN\setminus\{o\}. $$ A key point of the present work is that $K$ is not assumed to be origin-symmetric. Consequently, the Minkowski functional $\hK{\cdot}$ need not be even, and the usual norm-based anisotropic arguments do not apply directly. The main tools are anisotropic $L^2$-Hardy and $L^2$-Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg identities with explicit nonnegative remainders. These identities yield sharp anisotropic $L^2$-Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg inequalities whose best constants depend on the parameter region of $(a,b)\in\mathbb R^2$. We also study the attainability of the sharp constants in a natural completion space and obtain the corresponding extremal functions. As further consequences, we derive sharp anisotropic Heisenberg-type uncertainty principles and max-type anisotropic gradient inequalities. When $K$ is the Euclidean unit ball, our results recover the classical Euclidean $L^2$ theory; when $K$ is origin-symmetric, they are consistent with the usual norm-based anisotropic framework. In particular, the present results extend the sharp $L^2$-Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg theory to general convex bodies containing the origin in their interiors, for which the Minkowski functional may be non-even.

</details>


### [165] [Large Solutions for Fractional Laplacian on Infinite Cylindrical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25906)
*Indranil Chowdhury,N N Dattatreya*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了分数阶拉普拉斯算子在部分无界域上的线性与半线性方程的大解（边界爆破解）。与局部算子不同，非局部算子即使在线性方程中也会产生边界爆破现象。作者在有限圆柱域上研究了边界爆破解的存在性与定性行为，并利用这些性质在无限圆柱域上构造大解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶拉普拉斯算子的非局部性导致即使在线性方程中也会出现边界爆破现象，这与局部算子（仅在有低阶非线性项时才有大解）形成鲜明对比。作者旨在揭示非局部算子特有的边界爆破机制，并建立无限圆柱域上大解的存在性。

Method: 在有限圆柱域上研究分数阶线性与半线性方程的边界爆破解的存在性与定性行为，然后利用这些结果在无限圆柱域上构造大解。

Result: 证明了分数阶线性方程在有限圆柱域上存在边界爆破解，且这些解的行为可用于在无限圆柱域上构造大解。

Conclusion: 分数阶非局部算子的边界爆破现象与局部算子有本质不同，即使在线性情形下也会出现，这为理解非局部方程的大解理论提供了新的视角和方法。

Abstract: We investigate large solutions of linear and semi-linear equations involving the fractional Laplacian on domains that are becoming unbounded in some, but not all, directions. Solutions that blow up on the boundary of a domain are commonly called large solutions. For local operators, such behaviour arises only in the presence of lower-order nonlinear terms. However, for nonlocal operators, the existence of such solutions is more subtle. In contrast to the local case, the nonlocal nature of the operator gives rise to different boundary blow-up phenomena even in the case of linear equations. In this article, we study the existence and qualitative behaviour of boundary blow-up solutions to fractional linear and semi-linear equations on finite cylindrical domains and employ these properties to construct large solutions on infinite cylinders.

</details>


### [166] [On the emergence of dead cores in elliptic systems with sublinear competitive interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25943)
*Hugo Tavares,Gianmaria Verzini,Junwei Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究半线性椭圆系统中具有次线性耦合项的解，发现竞争型相互作用下解会出现死核（在某个开子集上为零），并将结果应用于基态和最小能量变号解等情形，证明基于次线性方程的次解具有死核的一般结论和一致Hölder界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解竞争型相互作用下半线性椭圆系统解的死核现象，为已有文献中各类解提供统一的理论框架。

Method: 基于次线性方程的次解具有死核的一般性结论，结合一致Hölder界估计进行证明。

Result: 证明在竞争型次线性耦合项作用下，解会在定义域的某个开子集上为零（死核），该结果适用于Dirichlet边界条件或全空间下的基态和最小能量变号解。

Conclusion: 次线性耦合项的竞争型相互作用是导致解出现死核的关键机制，建立了一般性理论框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study semilinear elliptic systems with sublinear coupling terms, showing that, under competition-type interactions, solutions typically have dead cores, i.e., they vanish on certain open subsets of the domain. We apply our results to a large class of solutions treated in the literature, for instance to ground states and least energy sign-changing solutions, under Dirichlet boundary conditions or in the whole space. The proofs are based on a general result stating that subsolutions to a certain sublinear equation have dead cores, and on uniform Hölder bounds.

</details>


### [167] [Sharp rigidity for quasilinear Liouville equation on manifolds with nonnegative Ricci curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25981)
*Xiaohan Cai*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究非负Ricci曲率完备非紧Riemann流形上的拟线性Liouville方程，证明了若解满足最优对数下界，则流形等距于欧氏空间且解为标准气泡解；同时提出亚对数衰减与几何相互作用的猜想并部分证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非负Ricci曲率完备非紧Riemann流形上拟线性Liouville方程解的性质与底层流形几何结构之间的定量关系，尤其是解的对数下界如何刻画流形的欧氏性。

Method: 通过建立对数下界与解的总体积上界之间的关键联系，利用几何分析手段证明流形的等距性；对于亚对数衰减情形，采用分维度证明策略（n=2完全证明，n≥3在加强假设下证明）。

Result: (1) 若解满足最优对数下界，则流形等距于欧氏空间且解为标准气泡解，且该下界中的主项系数和余项均为最优；(2) 提出关于亚对数衰减与几何相互作用的猜想，并在n=2和n≥3（加强条件）下证明。

Conclusion: 解的增长/衰减行为与流形几何存在深刻耦合，最优对数下界精确刻画了欧氏几何；亚对数衰减区域的几何刚性需要进一步条件限制。

Abstract: We study the quasilinear Liouville equation \[ -Δ_n u=e^u \] on complete noncompact Riemannian manifolds with nonnegative Ricci curvature. Our first result shows that, if a solution $u$ satisfies the optimal logarithmic lower bound \[ u(x)\ge -\frac{n^2}{n-1}\log r(x)+o(\log r(x)) \quad \text{as }r(x)\to+\infty, \] then the underlying manifold is isometric to the Euclidean space and $u$ is a standard bubble solution. Both the leading coefficient and the remainder term in the assumption are sharp. The key ingredient in the proof is the connection between the logarithmic lower bound and a sharp upper bound on the total volume of the solution.
  We also formulate a conjecture concerning the interaction between the sub-logarithmic decay of solutions and the underlying geometry, and prove it for $n=2$, as well as for $n\ge 3$ under a strengthened assumption.

</details>
